var NOTES_FOLDER_ID = "12Xys2cb9C57etSWwCjxiORZk3dTI19mN"; // Drive folder for patient docs (Leitner Anesthesia Workspace)
var MA_EMAIL = "bradley.burdick@huntington.com";         // MA digest (Bradley Burdick)
var BILLER_EMAIL = "floyd@thesproutmax.com";     // Biller for Medicare packet notifications (same address as inbound BILLER_FROM)
var CLINICIAN_EMAIL = "lindseyallannelson@gmail.com";  // Clinician review notifications
var EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL = "evan@leitneranesthesia.com";   // Auto-CC'd on every clinician email — Evan checks platform notifications more reliably than Lindsey
var SIGNATURES_FOLDER_ID = "16Wewqr3iU28jHF35V71NvzASRPP0vZbD"; // Drive folder for user signature PNGs (Leitner Anesthesia Workspace)
var FALLBACK_COSIGNER_EMAIL = "lindseyallannelson@gmail.com"; // Used when no qualifying physician maps to a site
// Cell numbers for priority SMS alerts on inbound referrals.
// Set via Apps Script editor → Project Settings → Script Properties.
// Leave blank to disable SMS for that recipient (email still fires).
var EVAN_CELL = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("EVAN_CELL") || "+17349729930";
var LINDSEY_CELL = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("LINDSEY_CELL") || "+15136001607";
// ============================================================

// Send an email to the clinician (Lindsey) with Evan auto-CC'd. Use this
// instead of MailApp.sendEmail({to: CLINICIAN_EMAIL, ...}) for any
// notification where Evan should also see it. Reasoning: Lindsey doesn't
// check email frequently; Evan operates the platform daily. Keep both
// looped in by default.
//
// Caller can still pass an explicit `cc` — the helper merges with EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL.
// Pass `skipEvanCc: true` in opts for the rare case Evan should NOT be CC'd
// (e.g. clinical-only escalations, deeply private content). Default = include.
function sendClinicianEmail(opts) {
  if (!opts || !opts.subject) return;
  var to = opts.to || CLINICIAN_EMAIL;
  var ccList = [];
  if (opts.cc) {
    if (Array.isArray(opts.cc)) ccList = opts.cc.slice();
    else ccList = String(opts.cc).split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean);
  }
  // Auto-add EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL unless explicitly opted out OR already in to/cc
  if (!opts.skipEvanCc && EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
    var alreadyIncluded = String(to).toLowerCase().indexOf(EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL.toLowerCase()) !== -1 ||
                          ccList.some(function(c){ return c.toLowerCase() === EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL.toLowerCase(); });
    if (!alreadyIncluded) ccList.push(EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL);
  }
  try {
    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: to,
      cc: ccList.join(","),
      subject: opts.subject,
      body: opts.body || ""
    });
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("sendClinicianEmail failed: " + err);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// STATUS TAXONOMY — single source of truth for valid throughput statuses.
// Used by:
//   - processBillerEmails (STATUS_RULES) — to recognize Floyd's status keywords
//   - backfillThroughputStatuses — to know what's canonical vs. relic
//   - getStatusTaxonomy GET endpoint — frontend reads from here for the "?" indicator
//
// When you add a new status, you change THIS object only. Aliases handle
// legacy values automatically (e.g. "Intake Complete" → "Intake In Progress").
// ============================================================
var STATUS_TAXONOMY = {
  // Each canonical entry: keyword (what Floyd writes/system matches), final status
  // (what's stored), and metadata. Order matters where prefix overlap exists —
  // longer-prefix entries listed FIRST so longest-match wins (e.g. "preauth
  // approved" before "preauth", "trial billing rejected" before "trial billing").
rules: [
    { match: "preauth in review",       status: "Preauth In Review",       stampSubmitted: true },
    { match: "preauth submitted",       status: "Preauth Submitted",       stampSubmitted: true },
    { match: "preauth approved",        status: "Authorized",              stampSubmitted: false, notifyPreauthApproved: true },
    { match: "preauth rejected",        status: "Preauth Rejected",        stampSubmitted: false, isRejection: true, notifyPreauthRejected: true, queueAction: "appeal_required" },
    { match: "preauth denied",          status: "Preauth Rejected",        stampSubmitted: false, isRejection: true, notifyPreauthRejected: true, queueAction: "appeal_required" },
    { match: "peer to peer request",    status: null,                      stampSubmitted: false, queueAction: "p2p_request" },
    { match: "trial billing submitted", status: "Trial Billing Submitted", stampSubmitted: false },
    { match: "trial billing approved",  status: "Trial Billing Approved",  stampSubmitted: false },
    { match: "trial billing rejected",  status: "Trial Billing Rejected",  stampSubmitted: false, isRejection: true },
    { match: "trial billing denied",    status: "Trial Billing Rejected",  stampSubmitted: false, isRejection: true },
    { match: "trial reimbursed",        status: "Reimbursed",              stampSubmitted: false, extractAmount: true }
  ],
  // All valid status values. Generated below from rules + manual additions for
  // statuses that aren't biller-driven (intake stages, visit completion).
  canonical: {
    "Referral Received": true,
    "Intake In Progress": true,
    "Awaiting Cosign": true,
    "Cosigned": true,
    "Preauth In Review": true,
    "Preauth Submitted": true,
    "Preauth Rejected": true,
    "Authorized": true,
    "D0 Scheduled": true,
    "D0 Complete": true,
    "Trial Billing Submitted": true,
    "Trial Billing Approved": true,
    "Trial Billing Rejected": true,
    "D20 Complete": true,
    "D90 Complete": true,
    "Reimbursed": true,
    "Complete": true
  },
  // Legacy → canonical aliases. Backfill function uses these to auto-translate
  // older status strings. Add to this map when introducing breaking renames.
  aliases: {
    "Intake Complete": "Intake In Progress",  // legacy name pre-2026-04
    "Called": "Referral Received",            // was a verb, never a real status
    "D0 booked": "D0 Scheduled",
    "Patient Confirmed": "Intake In Progress",
    "Out for Authorization": "Preauth Submitted",
    "New - Direct Entry": "Referral Received" // legacy auto-create marker, fixed at write time
  },
  // Prefixes that allow free-text suffix and remain canonical:
  //   "Removed: Withdrew", "Preauth Rejected: investigational",
  //   "D0 Complete - Awaiting Medicare", "Intake Complete - Awaiting Medicare", etc.
  // The "X Complete - Y" pattern is common — the canonical milestone is the
  // status itself, and the trailing detail explains what's blocking next-step.
  // Keep the suffix; just don't flag it.
  canonicalPrefixes: [
    "Removed:",
    "Removed ",
    "Preauth Rejected",
    "Trial Billing Rejected",
    "Intake In Progress",
    "Intake Complete",      // legacy + real "Intake Complete - Awaiting Medicare"
    "D0 Complete",
    "D20 Complete",
    "D90 Complete",
    "Trial Billing Approved",
    "Trial Billing Submitted"
  ]
};

// Returns true if a given status value is canonical OR canonically-prefixed.
function isCanonicalStatus(status) {
  if (!status) return true; // empty defaults to "Referral Received"
  var s = String(status).trim();
  if (!s) return true;
  if (STATUS_TAXONOMY.canonical[s]) return true;
  for (var i = 0; i < STATUS_TAXONOMY.canonicalPrefixes.length; i++) {
    if (s.indexOf(STATUS_TAXONOMY.canonicalPrefixes[i]) === 0) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// Translate a legacy status string to its canonical form. Returns the original
// string if no alias is defined OR if the input is already canonical.
function translateLegacyStatus(status) {
  if (!status) return status;
  var s = String(status).trim();
  if (STATUS_TAXONOMY.aliases[s]) return STATUS_TAXONOMY.aliases[s];
  return status;
}

// ============================================================
// SITE ID CANONICALIZATION
//
// Single normalizer for site identifiers. Applied at every WRITE point so
// the data on disk is consistent regardless of how it arrived (referral
// parser, manual entry, AI extraction). Read-side comparisons can then use
// strict equality instead of substring/tolerance matching.
//
// Rules (composed in order):
//   1. Trim whitespace
//   2. Uppercase
//   3. Strip trailing parenthetical state codes ("LEWES, DE" → "LEWES")
//   4. Replace internal whitespace with underscore ("DC NORTH" → "DC_NORTH")
//   5. Strip non-alphanumeric except underscore (".", "-", "/" all collapse)
//
// Examples:
//   "Lewes"          → "LEWES"
//   " lewes, de "    → "LEWES"
//   "DC North"       → "DC_NORTH"
//   "Salisbury, MD"  → "SALISBURY"
//   "Mt. Tam"        → "MT_TAM"
//
// If you add a new site, decide on the canonical form ONCE in the Sites
// sheet, then every write point converges on it.
// ============================================================
function normalizeSiteId(input) {
  if (!input) return "";
  var s = String(input).trim().toUpperCase();
  // Strip trailing ", XX" state code (matches "LEWES, DE" but not "DC, NORTH")
  s = s.replace(/,\s*[A-Z]{2}\s*$/, "");
  // Collapse internal whitespace to underscore
  s = s.replace(/\s+/g, "_");
  // Strip anything that's not alphanumeric or underscore
  s = s.replace(/[^A-Z0-9_]/g, "");
  return s;
}

// One-time data migration: rewrite Sites sheet, Users sheet locations, and
// Throughput siteId column to use canonical forms. Safe to re-run (idempotent).
// Default dryRun=true logs proposed changes without writing. Run from editor:
//   migrateSiteIdsCanonical(true)   ← preview
//   migrateSiteIdsCanonical(false)  ← commit
function migrateSiteIdsCanonical(dryRun) {
  if (dryRun === undefined) dryRun = true;
  Logger.log("===== SITE ID MIGRATION " + (dryRun ? "(DRY RUN)" : "(COMMIT)") + " =====");
  var changes = 0;

  // 1. Sites sheet — column 1 (Site ID)
  var sitesSheet = initSitesSheet();
  if (sitesSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var sRows = sitesSheet.getRange(2, 1, sitesSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < sRows.length; i++) {
      var orig = String(sRows[i][0] || "");
      var canon = normalizeSiteId(orig);
      if (orig && canon !== orig) {
        Logger.log("  Sites row " + (i+2) + ": '" + orig + "' → '" + canon + "'");
        if (!dryRun) sitesSheet.getRange(i+2, 1).setValue(canon);
        changes++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Users sheet — column 5 (Locations CSV)
  var usersSheet = initUsersSheet();
  if (usersSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var uRows = usersSheet.getRange(2, 5, usersSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var ui = 0; ui < uRows.length; ui++) {
      var origCsv = String(uRows[ui][0] || "");
      if (!origCsv) continue;
      var parts = origCsv.split(",").map(normalizeSiteId).filter(Boolean);
      var canonCsv = parts.join(",");
      if (canonCsv !== origCsv) {
        Logger.log("  Users row " + (ui+2) + " locations: '" + origCsv + "' → '" + canonCsv + "'");
        if (!dryRun) usersSheet.getRange(ui+2, 5).setValue(canonCsv);
        changes++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Throughput sheet — column 19 (Site ID)
  var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
  if (tpSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var widthForSite = Math.min(tpSheet.getLastColumn(), 19);
    if (widthForSite >= 19) {
      var tRows = tpSheet.getRange(2, 19, tpSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
      for (var ti = 0; ti < tRows.length; ti++) {
        var origT = String(tRows[ti][0] || "");
        if (!origT) continue;
        var canonT = origT.split(",").map(normalizeSiteId).filter(Boolean).join(",");
        if (canonT !== origT) {
          Logger.log("  Throughput row " + (ti+2) + " siteId: '" + origT + "' → '" + canonT + "'");
          if (!dryRun) tpSheet.getRange(ti+2, 19).setValue(canonT);
          changes++;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  Logger.log("===== " + changes + " row(s) " + (dryRun ? "would change" : "changed") + " =====");
  return { changes: changes, dryRun: dryRun };
}
// ============================================================

// Normalize DOB cell value to MM/DD/YYYY string.
// Google Sheets auto-parses "6/4/1952" into a Date object, which JSON-serializes
// to an ISO timestamp like "1952-06-04T04:00:00.000Z" and corrupts the form field.
// This coerces it back to a plain string regardless of what the cell actually holds.
function formatDobString(v) {
  if (!v) return "";
  if (v instanceof Date) {
    var m = v.getMonth() + 1;
    var d = v.getDate();
    var y = v.getFullYear();
    return (m < 10 ? "0" + m : m) + "/" + (d < 10 ? "0" + d : d) + "/" + y;
  }
  return String(v).replace(/^'/, "");
}

function getOrCreateSheet(name) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  if (!sheet) sheet = ss.insertSheet(name);
  return sheet;
}

// ============================================================
// INBOUND DOCUMENT FILING — two-tier (canonical in patient folder + shortcut in doc-type index)
// ============================================================
// Files an inbound document (fax, eligibility printout, P2P request, etc.) so it's
// findable two ways: in the patient's folder grouped by doc type (clinical context),
// AND in a flat doc-type index across all patients (operational triage — "show me
// all P2P requests"). Canonical lives in patient folder; index holds shortcuts only.
// Edits to canonical file flow through the shortcut. Deleting shortcut leaves
// canonical intact.

// Locked taxonomy. Reject unknown types so we don't accumulate "P2P" + "p2p" +
// "Peer to Peer" + "P2P Request" folder drift. Add new types here, then deploy.
var INBOUND_DOC_TYPES = {
  "P2P Request": true,             // Humana / MA payer peer-to-peer requests
  "Auth Approval": true,           // Payer approval letters
  "Auth Denial": true,             // Payer denial letters
  "Eligibility Verification": true,// Manual or system eligibility printouts
  "Medicare Card": true,           // Patient-texted Medicare card images
  "Insurance Card": true,          // Patient-texted commercial insurance cards
  "Other": true                    // Escape hatch — surface in weekly review
};

var DOC_INDEX_FOLDER_NAME = "DNC Document Index";

/**
 * File an inbound document into the patient's folder and the doc-type index.
 *
 * @param {Blob}   pdfBlob   - file content (any binary blob; PDF is typical)
 * @param {string} dncId     - "DNC-XXXX"
 * @param {string} docType   - must be a key in INBOUND_DOC_TYPES
 * @param {object} [metadata] - optional. Recognized keys:
 *                              source (e.g. "Spruce fax"),
 *                              payer (e.g. "Humana"),
 *                              referenceNumber (e.g. "227338391"),
 *                              receivedAt (Date or ISO string; defaults to now)
 * @returns {object} { ok, fileId, fileUrl, shortcutId, patientFolderName, fileName }
 *                   on success, or { ok:false, error } on any failure. Never throws.
 */
function fileInboundDocument(pdfBlob, dncId, docType, metadata) {
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  metadata = metadata || {};

  try {
    if (!pdfBlob) return { ok: false, error: "missing pdfBlob" };
    if (!dncId || !/^DNC-/i.test(dncId)) {
      return { ok: false, error: "invalid dncId: " + dncId };
    }
    dncId = String(dncId).toUpperCase();
    if (!INBOUND_DOC_TYPES[docType]) {
      return { ok: false, error: "unknown docType: '" + docType + "' (add to INBOUND_DOC_TYPES first)" };
    }

    // Resolve patient name from throughput so folder naming is consistent
    // even if we have to auto-create (rare; doc arrives before referral row).
    var patientName = "";
    try {
      var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var tpRow = findThroughputRow(tpSheet, dncId);
      if (tpRow > 0) patientName = String(tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 1).getValue() || "");
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log("fileInboundDocument: throughput lookup failed for " + dncId + ": " + e);
    }

    // Find existing patient folder by DNC ID substring (same pattern as
    // backfillCosignForPatient — folder names are "<Name> - DNC-XXXX").
    var rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(NOTES_FOLDER_ID);
    var patientFolder = null;
    var folderIter = rootFolder.getFolders();
    while (folderIter.hasNext()) {
      var f = folderIter.next();
      if (f.getName().indexOf(dncId) !== -1) {
        patientFolder = f;
        // Recover patient name from folder if throughput didn't have it
        if (!patientName) {
          var fname = f.getName();
          var sepIdx = fname.indexOf(" - " + dncId);
          if (sepIdx > 0) patientName = fname.substring(0, sepIdx);
        }
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!patientFolder) {
      // Auto-create. Edge case: a P2P fax arrives for a patient whose
      // referral hasn't been processed yet. Better to file it than drop it.
      var newFolderName = (patientName ? patientName + " - " : "") + dncId;
      patientFolder = rootFolder.createFolder(newFolderName);
      Logger.log("fileInboundDocument: auto-created patient folder '" + newFolderName + "' (no prior referral row found)");
    }

    // Canonical: <patient folder>/<DocType>/<filename>
    var docTypeSubfolder = getOrCreateSubfolder(patientFolder, docType);

    var receivedAt = metadata.receivedAt ? new Date(metadata.receivedAt) : new Date();
    if (isNaN(receivedAt.getTime())) receivedAt = new Date();
    var datePrefix = Utilities.formatDate(receivedAt, "America/New_York", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    var refSuffix = metadata.referenceNumber ? " - ref" + metadata.referenceNumber : "";
    var fileName = datePrefix + " - " + docType + refSuffix + ".pdf";

    pdfBlob.setName(fileName);
    var canonicalFile = docTypeSubfolder.createFile(pdfBlob);

    // Index shortcut: <root>/DNC Document Index/<DocType>/<shortcut>
    var indexFolder = getOrCreateSubfolder(rootFolder, DOC_INDEX_FOLDER_NAME);
    var docTypeIndexFolder = getOrCreateSubfolder(indexFolder, docType);

    // Shortcut name encodes patient identity since the index folder is flat
    // by doctype across all patients. Last name from "Last, First" format.
    var lastName = patientName ? String(patientName).split(",")[0].trim() : "";
    var shortcutName = dncId + (lastName ? " - " + lastName : "") + " - " + datePrefix + refSuffix;

    var shortcut = DriveApp.createShortcut(canonicalFile.getId());
    shortcut.setName(shortcutName);
    // createShortcut places it in user root; relocate to the index subfolder
    docTypeIndexFolder.addFile(shortcut);
    DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(shortcut);

    auditLog({
      action: "fileInboundDocument",
      user: "system",
      patientId: dncId,
      outcome: "ok",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "docType=" + docType + " size=" + pdfBlob.getBytes().length +
              (metadata.source ? " source=" + metadata.source : "")
    });

    return {
      ok: true,
      fileId: canonicalFile.getId(),
      fileUrl: canonicalFile.getUrl(),
      shortcutId: shortcut.getId(),
      patientFolderName: patientFolder.getName(),
      fileName: fileName
    };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("fileInboundDocument FAILED for " + dncId + " (" + docType + "): " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "fileInboundDocument",
      user: "system",
      patientId: dncId || "",
      outcome: "error",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
    });
    return { ok: false, error: String(err) };
  }
}

// Smoke test — run from editor. Replace dncId with a real one from your throughput.
function testFileInboundDocument() {
  var testBlob = Utilities.newBlob("Test inbound document — safe to delete.", "application/pdf", "test.pdf");
  var result = fileInboundDocument(testBlob, "DNC-BTXF", "Other", {
    source: "manual test",
    referenceNumber: "TEST" + Date.now()
  });
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  return result;
}

// ============================================================
// HIPAA AUDIT LOG
// ============================================================
// Every action that touches PHI writes a row here. Required for HIPAA Security Rule
// audit controls. Do NOT log PHI itself — log the DNC-XXXX pseudonym, the action, and
// the outcome. Names, phones, DOBs, etc. must NEVER appear in this sheet.
//
// Columns: Timestamp | User Email | Action | Patient ID | Outcome | Latency (ms) | Detail
// The Detail column is a short machine-readable string (e.g. "confidence=suspect fields=phone,dob")
// — NEVER free-text clinical content or identifiers.

function initAuditLogSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Audit Log");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Timestamp", "User Email", "Action", "Patient ID",
      "Outcome", "Latency (ms)", "Detail"
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 7).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Write one audit row. Never throws — audit failure must not block the clinical workflow.
// Signature: auditLog({action, user, patientId, outcome, latencyMs, detail})
function auditLog(entry) {
  try {
    var sheet = initAuditLogSheet();
    sheet.appendRow([
      new Date(),
      String(entry.user || "").substring(0, 200),
      String(entry.action || "unknown").substring(0, 100),
      String(entry.patientId || "").substring(0, 50),
      String(entry.outcome || "ok").substring(0, 50),
      entry.latencyMs || "",
      String(entry.detail || "").substring(0, 500)
    ]);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("auditLog write failed: " + e);
  }
}

// ============================================================
// USERS / SITES / COSIGN
// ============================================================

function initUsersSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Users");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Email","Display Name","Credentials","Role",
      "Locations","Licensed States","Signature URL","Created At","Updated At"
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1,1,1,9).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

function initSitesSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Sites");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow(["Site ID","Name","Address","State","Covered States"]);
    sheet.getRange(1,1,1,5).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// ============================================================
// PAYER REFERENCE
// Accumulates institutional knowledge about which insurance plans/payers
// require preauth, are auto-approved (traditional Medicare), or aren't
// covered at all. Without this, every Floyd eligibility verification gets
// re-discovered from scratch — even though "Aetna SEHBP requires auth for
// L8679" is a fact we should know after the first time.
//
// Categories:
//   auto-approved              — typically straight Medicare Part B
//   preauth-required           — Medicare Advantage, Aetna SEHBP, BCBS, etc.
//   not-covered                — payers that flat reject (rare)
//   unknown-needs-verification — default for new payers
//
// "Hybrid" auto-populate model: Floyd's emails write to a Pending Payer
// Updates queue; you review and promote to the main table when ready.
// Floyd's confidence ≠ committed truth.
// ============================================================
function initPayerReferenceSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Payer Reference");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Payer Name",                  // 1 - canonical name (e.g., "Aetna SEHBP Medicare PPO")
      "Category",                    // 2 - auto-approved | preauth-required | not-covered | unknown-needs-verification
      "CPT Codes Requiring Auth",    // 3 - "L8679, 64555" — informational
      "Typical Turnaround (days)",   // 4 - rough estimate
      "Common Rejection Reasons",    // 5 - free text
      "Last Verified Date",          // 6 - ISO date
      "Last Verified By",            // 7 - email of last person who verified
      "Notes"                        // 8 - free text
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 8).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);

    // Seed with known starting points based on what's already verified by Floyd
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Medicare Part B", "auto-approved", "", 0,
      "", new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10), "system:seed",
      "Traditional Medicare — straight Part B with or without Medigap. No preauth required."
    ]);
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Medicare Advantage", "preauth-required", "L8679, 64555", 7,
      "Investigational/experimental flag is most common. Peer-to-peer often required.",
      new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10), "system:seed",
      "Generic MA category. Specific plans (Aetna, Humana, BCBS Advantage) should be added as separate rows when known."
    ]);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Pending Payer Updates — staging area for Floyd's auto-detected eligibility
// info. Each Floyd email that mentions auth required/not-required for a
// payer creates or updates a pending row. You review and promote to main
// table by changing status from "pending" to "approved" (or "rejected").
function initPayerUpdatesQueue() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Payer Updates Queue");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Update ID",        // 1 - PU-<timestamp>-<rand>
      "Payer Name",       // 2 - extracted from Floyd email
      "Suggested Category", // 3 - what Floyd's email implies
      "Detected From",    // 4 - DNC ID + email subject that triggered this
      "Detected At",      // 5 - timestamp
      "Status",           // 6 - pending | approved | rejected
      "Reviewed By",      // 7 - email of reviewer
      "Reviewed At",      // 8 - timestamp
      "Notes"             // 9 - free text
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 9).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#fef3c7");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Fuzzy-match an insurance string from a referral or throughput row against
// the Payer Reference table. Returns the matched category + metadata, or
// "unknown-needs-verification" if no match.
//
// Matching strategy: case-insensitive substring in either direction.
// "Aetna SEHBP" matches "Aetna" entry, and vice versa. This is intentionally
// permissive — payer naming drifts wildly across referral sources.
function lookupPayerCategory(insuranceName) {
  if (!insuranceName) {
    return { category: "unknown-needs-verification", lastVerified: "", payerName: "" };
  }
  try {
    var sheet = initPayerReferenceSheet();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { category: "unknown-needs-verification", lastVerified: "", payerName: "" };
    }
    var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 8).getValues();
    var query = String(insuranceName).trim().toLowerCase();

    // Score each row by match strength: exact > prefix > substring > nothing.
    // Returns the highest-scoring match, breaking ties by most-recently-verified.
    var bestMatch = null;
    var bestScore = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      var rowName = String(rows[i][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!rowName) continue;
      var score = 0;
      if (rowName === query) score = 100;
      else if (query.indexOf(rowName) === 0) score = 80;       // referral starts with payer name
      else if (rowName.indexOf(query) === 0) score = 70;       // payer name starts with referral
      else if (query.indexOf(rowName) !== -1) score = 60;      // payer name appears in referral
      else if (rowName.indexOf(query) !== -1) score = 50;      // referral appears in payer name
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        bestMatch = {
          category: String(rows[i][1] || "unknown-needs-verification"),
          payerName: String(rows[i][0] || ""),
          cptCodes: String(rows[i][2] || ""),
          turnaround: rows[i][3] || 0,
          rejectionReasons: String(rows[i][4] || ""),
          lastVerified: String(rows[i][5] || ""),
          lastVerifiedBy: String(rows[i][6] || ""),
          notes: String(rows[i][7] || "")
        };
      }
    }
    if (bestMatch) return bestMatch;
    return { category: "unknown-needs-verification", lastVerified: "", payerName: "" };
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("lookupPayerCategory failed for '" + insuranceName + "': " + err);
    return { category: "unknown-needs-verification", lastVerified: "", payerName: "" };
  }
}

// Called by biller email parser when Floyd's email body mentions auth
// requirement. Creates a row in Payer Updates Queue (pending review) rather
// than writing directly to Payer Reference. You review weekly via the queue.
function queuePayerUpdate(payerName, suggestedCategory, dncId, subject) {
  if (!payerName) return null;
  try {
    var sheet = initPayerUpdatesQueue();
    var updateId = "PU-" + Date.now() + "-" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000);
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    sheet.appendRow([
      updateId, payerName, suggestedCategory,
      "DNC: " + (dncId || "") + " — " + (subject || ""),
      now, "pending", "", "", ""
    ]);
    Logger.log("Queued payer update: " + payerName + " → " + suggestedCategory + " (" + updateId + ")");
    return updateId;
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("queuePayerUpdate failed: " + err);
    return null;
  }
}

// One-time seed: scan all Throughput rows for unique insurance names mentioned
// in the Notes column (Ins:/Sec: segments), then add Payer Reference rows for
// any not already present. Categories are guessed by payer-name heuristics
// using your historical experience as the truth source:
//
//   "Medicare" + "Advantage|MA Plan|Replacement|HMO|PPO" → preauth-required
//   "Medicare" alone (or "Medicare of <state>", "Medicare Part B")  → auto-approved
//   "Medicaid"                                              → auto-approved
//   "Aetna|BCBS|Cigna|UnitedHealthcare|Humana" + "Medicare" → preauth-required (MA in disguise)
//   "Aetna|BCBS|Cigna|UnitedHealthcare|Humana" alone        → preauth-required (commercial)
//   anything else                                           → unknown-needs-verification
//
// Run from editor as: seedPayerReferenceFromReferrals(true)  ← preview
//                     seedPayerReferenceFromReferrals(false) ← commit
//
// Safe to re-run; only adds rows that don't already exist (matched
// case-insensitively by payer name).
function seedPayerReferenceFromReferrals(dryRun) {
  if (dryRun === undefined) dryRun = true;
  Logger.log("===== PAYER SEED " + (dryRun ? "(DRY RUN)" : "(COMMIT)") + " =====");

  var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
  if (tpSheet.getLastRow() < 2) { Logger.log("No throughput rows."); return; }
  var widthForNotes = Math.min(tpSheet.getLastColumn(), 18);
  var rows = tpSheet.getRange(2, 1, tpSheet.getLastRow() - 1, widthForNotes).getValues();

  // Normalize a payer string for use as a deduplication key. The referral
  // parser generates dirty payer names — trailing punctuation, inconsistent
  // case, "Medicare of Maryland -" vs "Medicare of Maryland" vs "Medicare of
  // Maryland - Secondary" all become separate entries unless we fold them.
  // For seeding we want canonical forms, so:
  //   1. Lowercase for compare
  //   2. Trim leading/trailing whitespace + punctuation (commas, dashes, periods, slashes)
  //   3. Strip "secondary"/"primary" suffixes — same payer, different role
  //   4. Strip trailing "claims only" wording
  //   5. Collapse internal whitespace
  function normalizePayerKey(name) {
    var s = String(name || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!s) return "";
    // Strip trailing role + clarifications
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(secondary|primary)(\s+claims\s+only)?$/i, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*claims\s+only$/i, "");
    // Strip Medicare jurisdiction codes ("- J12", "- J5", etc.) — these are
    // administrative routing identifiers, not different payers. CMS uses
    // jurisdictions J5, J6, J8, J11, J12, J15, JF, JH, JJ, JK, JL, JM, JN.
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*j\d{1,2}\b/gi, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*j[a-z]\b/gi, "");
    // Dangling trailing punctuation
    s = s.replace(/[\s,.\-–—\/:;]+$/g, "");
    // Leading punctuation
    s = s.replace(/^[\s,.\-–—\/:;]+/g, "");
    // Collapse whitespace
    s = s.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    return s;
  }

  // Pretty display name. Same as normalize but preserves original casing
  // when the input has mixed case; otherwise title-case.
  function prettifyPayerName(name) {
    var s = String(name || "").trim();
    // Trim trailing role/punctuation/jurisdiction same as normalize
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(secondary|primary)(\s+claims\s+only)?$/i, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*claims\s+only$/i, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*j\d{1,2}\b/gi, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*j[a-z]\b/gi, "");
    s = s.replace(/[\s,.\-–—\/:;]+$/g, "");
    s = s.replace(/^[\s,.\-–—\/:;]+/g, "");
    s = s.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
    // If all-caps, title-case it. If mixed case, leave alone.
    if (s === s.toUpperCase() && s.length > 4) {
      s = s.toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, function(c){ return c.toUpperCase(); });
      // But preserve common all-caps acronyms that should stay caps
      s = s.replace(/\b(Bcbs|Uhc|Aarp|Ppo|Hmo|Va|Md|Dc|Va|De|Nc|Pa|Sc)\b/g, function(m){ return m.toUpperCase(); });
    }
    return s;
  }

  // Extract unique payer names from Ins: / Sec: segments in column 18,
  // keyed by normalized form. Track the prettiest version of each as the
  // canonical display name.
  var seenPayers = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    var notes = String(rows[i][17] || "");
    var insMatch = /\|\s*Ins:\s*([^|#]+?)(?:\s*#|\s*\||\s*$)/i.exec(notes);
    if (insMatch && insMatch[1]) {
      var k1 = normalizePayerKey(insMatch[1]);
      if (k1) {
        var pretty1 = prettifyPayerName(insMatch[1]);
        if (!seenPayers[k1] || seenPayers[k1].length < pretty1.length) seenPayers[k1] = pretty1;
      }
    }
    var secMatch = /\|\s*Sec:\s*([^|#]+?)(?:\s*#|\s*\||\s*$)/i.exec(notes);
    if (secMatch && secMatch[1]) {
      var k2 = normalizePayerKey(secMatch[1]);
      if (k2) {
        var pretty2 = prettifyPayerName(secMatch[1]);
        if (!seenPayers[k2] || seenPayers[k2].length < pretty2.length) seenPayers[k2] = pretty2;
      }
    }
  }

  // Load existing Payer Reference table to dedupe (also normalized)
  var refSheet = initPayerReferenceSheet();
  var existing = {};
  if (refSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    var refRows = refSheet.getRange(2, 1, refSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var r = 0; r < refRows.length; r++) {
      var ek = normalizePayerKey(refRows[r][0]);
      if (ek) existing[ek] = true;
    }
  }

  // Tightened categorization heuristic per Evan's review of v1 output:
  //   - "AETNA MEDICARE" was wrongly auto-approved → fixed: Medicare-with-carrier
  //     defaults to preauth-required (it's almost always Medicare Advantage).
  //   - Medigap/Supplement → flagged as supplement (not actually a primary
  //     coverage decision; treat as preauth-required since these are secondary
  //     and the underlying Medicare may be either traditional or MA).
  //   - Medicaid → preauth-required ("not yet" — same posture as commercial primary)
  //   - True traditional Medicare patterns → auto-approved
  //   - Charity care / VA / unknowns → flagged for verification
  function guessCategory(name) {
    var n = String(name).toLowerCase();
    var hasMedicare = /\bmedicare\b/.test(n);
    var hasMedicaid = /\bmedicaid\b/.test(n);
    var hasAdvantageMarker = /(advantage|ma plan|replacement|hmo|ppo)\b/.test(n);
    var hasSupplement = /(supplement|supplemental|medigap|gap|fixed indemnity)\b/.test(n);
    var hasCommercialBranded = /(aetna|blue cross|bcbs|cigna|united\s*healthcare|unitedhealthcare|uhc|humana|wellcare|carefirst|amerihealth|highmark|johns hopkins|mutual of omaha|aarp)\b/.test(n);
    var isCharity = /\b(charity|presumptive|self.?pay)\b/.test(n);
    var isVA = /\b(va |veterans|community care network)\b/.test(n);

    // Charity / VA / unspecified → human verify
    if (isCharity || isVA) return "unknown-needs-verification";

    // Supplement plans don't drive primary coverage decisions. Mark as
    // preauth-required so the system flags them; user can split into separate
    // verification or merge with the primary plan.
    if (hasSupplement) return "preauth-required";

    // Explicit Medicare Advantage signal
    if (hasMedicare && hasAdvantageMarker) return "preauth-required";

    // Medicare + commercial carrier name (Aetna Medicare, UHC Medicare, etc.) →
    // almost always MA in disguise. Tightened from v1 which auto-approved these.
    if (hasMedicare && hasCommercialBranded) return "preauth-required";

    // True traditional Medicare patterns: bare "Medicare", "Medicare Part A/B",
    // "Medicare of <state>". No carrier name, no advantage markers.
    if (hasMedicare) return "auto-approved";

    // Medicaid as primary — "not yet" per Evan. Same posture as commercial primary.
    if (hasMedicaid) return "preauth-required";

    // Commercial branded with no Medicare → preauth-required
    if (hasCommercialBranded) return "preauth-required";

    return "unknown-needs-verification";
  }

  var added = 0;
  var skipped = 0;
  var todayDate = new Date().toISOString().substring(0, 10);
  // Sort by display name for readable logs
  var sortedKeys = Object.keys(seenPayers).sort(function(a, b) {
    return seenPayers[a].localeCompare(seenPayers[b]);
  });
  sortedKeys.forEach(function(key) {
    var displayName = seenPayers[key];
    if (existing[key]) {
      skipped++;
      return;
    }
    var cat = guessCategory(displayName);
    Logger.log("  + " + displayName + "  →  " + cat);
    if (!dryRun) {
      refSheet.appendRow([
        displayName, cat, "", 0, "",
        todayDate, "system:auto-seed",
        "Auto-seeded from Throughput referrals on " + todayDate +
        ". Verify the category — adjust if your experience differs from the heuristic guess."
      ]);
    }
    added++;
  });

  Logger.log("===== " + added + " row(s) " + (dryRun ? "would be added" : "added") +
             ", " + skipped + " already present =====");
  return { added: added, skipped: skipped, dryRun: dryRun };
}

// One-time cleanup of artifacts left by the v1/v2 seed functions:
//   - "Medicare of Maryland - J12"   → duplicate of "Medicare of Maryland" (jurisdiction code)
//   - "Medical of Maryland"           → typo for Medicaid; just delete it
//   - "Medicare + HUMANA HMO"         → parser captured primary+secondary as one string
//   - "Mutual of Omaha"               → reclassify to unknown-needs-verification
//                                       (could be Medigap or commercial — too uncertain to lock in)
//
// Run from editor as: cleanupPayerSeedArtifacts(true)  ← preview
//                     cleanupPayerSeedArtifacts(false) ← commit
//
// Safe to re-run; only acts on rows that match the listed names exactly (case-insensitive).
function cleanupPayerSeedArtifacts(dryRun) {
  if (dryRun === undefined) dryRun = true;
  Logger.log("===== PAYER CLEANUP " + (dryRun ? "(DRY RUN)" : "(COMMIT)") + " =====");

  var sheet = initPayerReferenceSheet();
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) { Logger.log("No payer rows."); return; }

  var rowsToDelete = ["medicare of maryland - j12", "medical of maryland", "medicare + humana hmo"];
  var rowsToReclassify = {
    "mutual of omaha": "unknown-needs-verification"
  };

  // Read all rows; collect indices to delete (1-indexed sheet row numbers)
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 8).getValues();
  var deleteIndices = [];
  var reclassified = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    var name = String(data[i][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var sheetRow = i + 2;  // 1-indexed, +1 for header
    if (rowsToDelete.indexOf(name) !== -1) {
      Logger.log("  - DELETE: " + data[i][0] + "  (row " + sheetRow + ")");
      deleteIndices.push(sheetRow);
    } else if (rowsToReclassify[name]) {
      var newCat = rowsToReclassify[name];
      Logger.log("  ~ RECLASSIFY: " + data[i][0] + "  " + data[i][1] + " → " + newCat);
      if (!dryRun) {
        sheet.getRange(sheetRow, 2).setValue(newCat);
      }
      reclassified++;
    }
  }

  // Delete rows in reverse order so indices don't shift mid-deletion
  if (!dryRun) {
    deleteIndices.sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; });
    for (var d = 0; d < deleteIndices.length; d++) {
      sheet.deleteRow(deleteIndices[d]);
    }
  }

  Logger.log("===== " + deleteIndices.length + " row(s) " + (dryRun ? "would be deleted" : "deleted") +
             ", " + reclassified + " " + (dryRun ? "would be reclassified" : "reclassified") + " =====");
  return {
    deleted: deleteIndices.length,
    reclassified: reclassified,
    dryRun: dryRun
  };
}

function initCosignSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Cosign");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Cosign ID","Doc Type","Doc Name","Doc URL","Doc ID",
      "Patient ID","Author Email","Author Role","Site ID",
      "Cosigner Email","Status","Created At","Signed At","Notes"
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1,1,1,14).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// ============================================================
// NOTIFICATION LOG
// Append-only event log for every contact attempt made to a patient, across
// every channel (AI call, SMS, email, manual call). Lets the referral card
// show "last contact" status at a glance and gives the MA a single place to
// see who's been tried, when, via which channel, and what happened.
//
// Schema is designed to be channel- and modality-agnostic so Retell call
// outcomes, Telnyx SMS replies, email confirmations, and manual MA contacts
// all use the same shape. The `relatedData` JSON blob absorbs
// channel-specific extras (call_id, message_sid, shift_id when scheduling
// is wired, extracted scheduled date, etc.) without schema churn.
//
// Append-only semantics: events are logged then updated in-place as status
// transitions (pending → in-progress → completed/escalated/failed). Never
// delete rows — the log is forensic; if a call was tried, it stays recorded.
// ============================================================

function initNotificationLog() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Notification Log");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Event ID",         // 1 Unique ID (NLE-<timestamp>-<rand>)
      "DNC ID",           // 2 Patient DNC ID
      "Patient Name",     // 3 Denormalized for faster digest queries
      "Site ID",          // 4 For filtering by site
      "Type",             // 5 preauth-schedule | d0-reminder | d3-checkin | d10-followup | d20-reminder | d90-reminder | scheduling-callback | other
      "Channel",          // 6 ai-call | sms | email | manual-call | manual-sms
      "Status",           // 7 pending | in-progress | completed | escalated | no-response | failed
      "Initiated At",     // 8 ISO timestamp
      "Completed At",     // 9 ISO, blank until terminal state
      "Actor",            // 10 Retell call_id or user email
      "Outcome",          // 11 Free text ("scheduled 5/12", "voicemail", "wrong number")
      "Related Data",     // 12 JSON blob (scheduled date, shift id, transcript url, message sid)
      "Last Updated"      // 13
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 13).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// Generate a sortable unique event ID. Milliseconds + 4 random alphanumeric
// chars. Sortable by default because the millisecond timestamp is the prefix.
function generateNotificationEventId() {
  var chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
  var rand = "";
  for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) rand += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
  return "NLE-" + Date.now() + "-" + rand;
}

// Append a new notification event to the log. Returns the generated event ID
// so caller can later call updateNotificationEvent(id, patch) to transition
// state (e.g. "pending" → "in-progress" when the Retell call actually starts,
// then "in-progress" → "completed" when the call ends).
//
// Fields:
//   dncId       — required
//   type        — required (see Type column values above)
//   channel     — required
//   status      — required (typically "pending" for initial insert)
//   outcome     — optional, human-readable
//   relatedData — optional object (will be JSON.stringify'd)
//   actor       — optional (who/what initiated: email, retell call_id, system name)
function logNotificationEvent(dncId, type, channel, status, outcome, relatedData, actor) {
  try {
    var sheet = initNotificationLog();
    var eventId = generateNotificationEventId();
    var now = new Date().toISOString();

    // Resolve patient name + site from throughput for denormalization. Best
    // effort — if lookup fails we still log the event with blanks rather
    // than throwing.
    var patientName = "";
    var siteId = "";
    try {
      var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var tpRow = findThroughputRow(tpSheet, dncId);
      if (tpRow > 0) {
        patientName = String(tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 1).getValue() || "");
        siteId = String(tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 19).getValue() || "");
      }
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log("logNotificationEvent: throughput lookup failed for " + dncId + ": " + e);
    }

    // Terminal statuses get Completed At stamped on insert (for events that
    // start already complete — e.g. a one-shot email that was sent successfully).
    // Non-terminal statuses (pending, in-progress) leave it blank.
    var isTerminal = (status === "completed" || status === "escalated" ||
                       status === "no-response" || status === "failed");
    var completedAt = isTerminal ? now : "";

    var relatedJson = "";
    if (relatedData) {
      try { relatedJson = JSON.stringify(relatedData); }
      catch(e) { relatedJson = String(relatedData); }
    }

    sheet.appendRow([
      eventId,
      dncId,
      patientName,
      siteId,
      type,
      channel,
      status,
      now,         // Initiated At
      completedAt, // Completed At
      actor || "",
      outcome || "",
      relatedJson,
      now          // Last Updated
    ]);

    return eventId;
  } catch(err) {
    // Logging failures must never break the flow that called us — return null
    // and let the caller proceed. The event being uncaptured is a monitoring
    // issue, not a user-visible failure.
    Logger.log("logNotificationEvent failed for " + dncId + " (" + type + "/" + channel + "): " + err);
    return null;
  }
}

// Update an existing notification event in place. Use for transitions:
// "pending" → "in-progress" (call started), "in-progress" → "completed"
// (call finished), etc. Only provided fields are written; others untouched.
//
// Patch fields supported: status, outcome, relatedData, actor, completedAt
// (auto-set if status becomes terminal and completedAt not in patch).
function updateNotificationEvent(eventId, patch) {
  if (!eventId || !patch) return false;
  try {
    var sheet = initNotificationLog();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return false;
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    var row = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      if (ids[i][0] === eventId) { row = i + 2; break; }
    }
    if (row < 0) {
      Logger.log("updateNotificationEvent: event not found: " + eventId);
      return false;
    }
    var now = new Date().toISOString();
    if (patch.status !== undefined) sheet.getRange(row, 7).setValue(patch.status);
    if (patch.actor !== undefined) sheet.getRange(row, 10).setValue(patch.actor);
    if (patch.outcome !== undefined) sheet.getRange(row, 11).setValue(patch.outcome);
    if (patch.relatedData !== undefined) {
      var blob = "";
      try { blob = JSON.stringify(patch.relatedData); } catch(e) { blob = String(patch.relatedData); }
      sheet.getRange(row, 12).setValue(blob);
    }
    // Auto-stamp completedAt when transitioning to a terminal status, unless
    // patch explicitly sets it (tests, backdating edge cases).
    var newStatus = patch.status !== undefined ? patch.status : String(sheet.getRange(row, 7).getValue() || "");
    var isTerminal = (newStatus === "completed" || newStatus === "escalated" ||
                       newStatus === "no-response" || newStatus === "failed");
    if (patch.completedAt !== undefined) {
      sheet.getRange(row, 9).setValue(patch.completedAt);
    } else if (isTerminal) {
      var existing = sheet.getRange(row, 9).getValue();
      if (!existing) sheet.getRange(row, 9).setValue(now);
    }
    sheet.getRange(row, 13).setValue(now);
    return true;
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("updateNotificationEvent failed for " + eventId + ": " + err);
    return false;
  }
}

// Returns the most recent notification event for a given DNC ID, or null.
// Used by getReferrals to annotate each referral card with latest contact.
// Scans from bottom of log since events are appended in chronological order
// — most recent is always last match.
function getLatestNotificationEventForPatient(dncId) {
  if (!dncId) return null;
  try {
    var sheet = initNotificationLog();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return null;
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 13).getValues();
    // Walk backwards — most recent event for this patient wins
    for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (String(data[i][1]) === dncId) {
        var related = {};
        if (data[i][11]) {
          try { related = JSON.parse(String(data[i][11])); } catch(e) { related = { raw: String(data[i][11]) }; }
        }
        return {
          eventId: data[i][0],
          dncId: data[i][1],
          patientName: data[i][2],
          siteId: data[i][3],
          type: data[i][4],
          channel: data[i][5],
          status: data[i][6],
          initiatedAt: data[i][7],
          completedAt: data[i][8],
          actor: data[i][9],
          outcome: data[i][10],
          relatedData: related,
          lastUpdated: data[i][12]
        };
      }
    }
    return null;
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("getLatestNotificationEventForPatient failed for " + dncId + ": " + err);
    return null;
  }
}

// Returns ALL notification events for a given DNC ID, most-recent-first.
// Used by the referral-card expand-detail view (future) and by digest
// functions that need to see the full contact history for a patient.
function getNotificationEventsForPatient(dncId) {
  if (!dncId) return [];
  try {
    var sheet = initNotificationLog();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return [];
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 13).getValues();
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][1]) !== dncId) continue;
      var related = {};
      if (data[i][11]) {
        try { related = JSON.parse(String(data[i][11])); } catch(e) { related = { raw: String(data[i][11]) }; }
      }
      out.push({
        eventId: data[i][0],
        dncId: data[i][1],
        patientName: data[i][2],
        siteId: data[i][3],
        type: data[i][4],
        channel: data[i][5],
        status: data[i][6],
        initiatedAt: data[i][7],
        completedAt: data[i][8],
        actor: data[i][9],
        outcome: data[i][10],
        relatedData: related,
        lastUpdated: data[i][12]
      });
    }
    // Most recent first — reverse insertion order
    out.reverse();
    return out;
  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("getNotificationEventsForPatient failed for " + dncId + ": " + err);
    return [];
  }
}

// ============================================================
// PAYER ACTION QUEUE
// ============================================================
// Generic queue for payer-driven actions that need human follow-up:
// peer-to-peer review requests, appeal-required denials, additional-info
// requests. Multi-channel intake — Floyd email, payer fax via Spruce,
// payer voicemail via Spruce — all converge on the same table.
//
// Architecture:
//   - One row per pending action, dedup'd across channels
//   - Sources column is a JSON array; each channel that arrives appends
//     an entry. UI shows "📎 1 source" vs "📎 2 sources" so single-source
//     rows are visible at a glance (the failure case the redundancy catches)
//   - Conflicts between sources surface in Notes as [CONFLICT timestamp]
//     markers, never auto-resolved
//   - Deadline can be null until a PDF arrives with explicit timestamp;
//     UI defaults to action-type SLA when deadline is missing
//
// Dedup priority:
//   1. (dnc_id, payer, action_type, ref_number) when both sides have ref#
//   2. (dnc_id, payer, action_type) within last 30 days when ref# missing
//      — handles voicemail / brief Floyd emails that lack ref#
// ============================================================

var PAYER_ACTION_TYPES = {
  "p2p_request": {
    label: "Peer-to-Peer Request",
    sla_hours: 24,
    deadline_source_default: "explicit_pdf"
  },
  "appeal_required": {
    label: "Appeal Required",
    sla_days: 65,
    deadline_source_default: "notice_date+65d"
  },
  "additional_info_request": {
    label: "Additional Info Requested",
    sla_days: 14,
    deadline_source_default: "explicit_or_manual"
  }
};

function initPayerActionQueue() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Payer Action Queue");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Action ID",        //  1  PAQ-<timestamp>-<rand>
      "DNC ID",           //  2
      "Patient Name",     //  3  denormalized for digest queries
      "Payer",            //  4  e.g. "Humana"
      "Action Type",      //  5  p2p_request | appeal_required | additional_info_request
      "Ref Number",       //  6  payer reference # from PDF (blank if email-only)
      "Member ID",        //  7
      "CPT",              //  8
      "Dx Codes",         //  9
      "Deadline",         // 10  ISO timestamp; blank until PDF parsed
      "Deadline Source",  // 11  explicit_pdf | notice_date+65d | notice_date+72h | manual
      "Status",           // 12  open | resolved | superseded | expired
      "Sources",          // 13  JSON array of {channel, received_at, source_id, parsed_fields}
      "Created At",       // 14
      "Last Updated",     // 15
      "Resolved At",      // 16
      "Notes",            // 17  free text + [CONFLICT] flags
      "Site ID"           // 18  denormalized from throughput at create; "" = admin fallback
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 18).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#fef3c7");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  } else {
    // Backfill header for sheets created before Site ID column existed.
    // Mirrors the migration pattern in initThroughputSheet. Existing data
    // rows return blank for col 18 naturally on read — admin-fallback bucket.
    var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
    if (lastCol < 18) {
      sheet.getRange(1, 18).setValue("Site ID").setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#fef3c7");
    }
  }
  return sheet;
}

function generatePayerActionId() {
  var chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789";
  var rand = "";
  for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) rand += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
  return "PAQ-" + Date.now() + "-" + rand;
}

// Parse Floyd's standardized action-email body. Format confirmed 2026-05-07
// against Bates DNC-GBGV pair (preauth rejected + peer-to-peer request).
// Body shape (both action types use the same template):
//
//   Patient Name:  BATES, SARAH A
//   Insurance: Humana (H55918435)
//   CPT Code: 64555
//   Dx: E11.42, G89.29
//   Provider Name: Dr. Lindsey Nelson
//   POS: Office-11
//
// Returns whatever fields parsed cleanly. Fields are not gated on each other.
function parseFloydActionEmailBody(body) {
  if (!body) return {};
  var result = {};
  var nameM = /Patient Name:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i.exec(body);
  if (nameM) result.patientName = nameM[1].trim();
  // "Insurance: Humana (H55918435)" — payer name + member ID in parens
  var insM = /Insurance:\s*([^(\n]+?)\s*\(([^)]+)\)/i.exec(body);
  if (insM) {
    result.payer = insM[1].trim();
    result.memberId = insM[2].trim();
  } else {
    // Fallback if no parens
    var insBareM = /Insurance:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i.exec(body);
    if (insBareM) result.payer = insBareM[1].trim();
  }
  var cptM = /CPT\s*Code:\s*(\d+)/i.exec(body);
  if (cptM) result.cpt = cptM[1];
  var dxM = /Dx:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i.exec(body);
  if (dxM) result.dx = dxM[1].trim();
  var provM = /Provider Name:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i.exec(body);
  if (provM) result.provider = provM[1].trim();
  var posM = /POS:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i.exec(body);
  if (posM) result.pos = posM[1].trim();
  return result;
}

// Upsert a row into Payer Action Queue. Designed for both email-side
// (Floyd) and PDF-side (Humana fax) writes — either source can arrive
// first; merge fills in what the other source carries.
//
// Dedup key (in priority order):
//   1. Exact match: (dnc_id, payer, action_type, ref_number) when both rows have ref#
//   2. Time-window match: (dnc_id, payer, action_type) within last 30 days
//      when one or both rows lack ref#
//
// On match: append new source to sources array, fill null fields the new
// event provides data for, append [CONFLICT] note when fields disagree.
// On no match: create new row.
//
// Returns { actionId, created, merged } on success, { actionId: null, error }
// on failure. Never throws.
function upsertPayerActionRow(opts) {
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  if (!opts || !opts.dncId || !opts.actionType) {
    return { actionId: null, error: "missing dncId or actionType" };
  }
  if (!PAYER_ACTION_TYPES[opts.actionType]) {
    return { actionId: null, error: "unknown actionType: " + opts.actionType };
  }

  try {
    var sheet = initPayerActionQueue();
    var dncId = String(opts.dncId).toUpperCase();
    var payer = String(opts.payer || "").trim();
    var refNumber = String(opts.refNumber || "").trim();
    var actionType = opts.actionType;

// Resolve patient name + siteId from throughput. Always read — siteId
    // is denormalized onto the queue row so listPayerActionQueue can filter
    // by user's allowed sites without joining throughput per row. Patient
    // name reads cheap as a side effect. Empty siteId (e.g. patient not in
    // throughput yet) becomes the "show to all admins" fallback in list.
    var patientName = opts.patientName || "";
    var siteId = "";
    try {
      var tp = initThroughputSheet();
      var tpRow = findThroughputRow(tp, dncId);
      if (tpRow > 0) {
        if (!patientName) patientName = String(tp.getRange(tpRow, 1).getValue() || "");
        // Site ID is column 19 in throughput (per initThroughputSheet header order)
        siteId = String(tp.getRange(tpRow, 19).getValue() || "").trim();
      }
    } catch(e) { /* best effort — empty siteId is acceptable, lands in admin bucket */ }
    // Dedup search — walk newest-to-oldest, take first match
    var matchRowIdx = -1;
    if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 17).getValues();
      var thirtyDaysAgo = Date.now() - (30 * 86400000);
      for (var i = rows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var rDnc = String(rows[i][1]);
        var rPayer = String(rows[i][3]);
        var rType = String(rows[i][4]);
        var rRef = String(rows[i][5]);
        var rStatus = String(rows[i][11]);
        if (rStatus !== "open") continue;
        if (rDnc !== dncId) continue;
        if (rType !== actionType) continue;
        // Payer match: case-insensitive substring either direction (handles
        // "Humana" vs "Humana Medicare Advantage" drift)
        var pLower = payer.toLowerCase();
        var rpLower = rPayer.toLowerCase();
        var payerMatches = !payer || !rPayer ||
          rpLower.indexOf(pLower) !== -1 ||
          pLower.indexOf(rpLower) !== -1;
        if (!payerMatches) continue;
        // Ref# join when both present; differs → genuinely different actions
        if (refNumber && rRef) {
          if (refNumber === rRef) { matchRowIdx = i + 2; break; }
          continue;
        }
        // No ref# on at least one side: 30-day window match
        var rCreated = rows[i][13] ? new Date(rows[i][13]).getTime() : 0;
        if (rCreated >= thirtyDaysAgo) { matchRowIdx = i + 2; break; }
      }
    }

    var nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
    var sourceEntry = {
      channel: opts.sourceChannel || "unknown",
      received_at: opts.receivedAt || nowIso,
      source_id: opts.sourceId || "",
      parsed_fields: opts.parsedFields || {}
    };

    if (matchRowIdx > 0) {
      // ===== MERGE =====
      var existing = sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 1, 1, 17).getValues()[0];
      var existingSources = [];
      try { existingSources = JSON.parse(String(existing[12] || "[]")); } catch(e) { existingSources = []; }
      existingSources.push(sourceEntry);
      sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 13).setValue(JSON.stringify(existingSources));

      // Fill any null fields the new event provides
      if (!existing[2] && patientName) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 3).setValue(patientName);
      if (!existing[3] && payer) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 4).setValue(payer);
      if (!existing[5] && refNumber) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 6).setValue(refNumber);
      if (!existing[6] && opts.memberId) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 7).setValue(opts.memberId);
      if (!existing[7] && opts.cpt) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 8).setValue(opts.cpt);
      if (!existing[8] && opts.dx) sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 9).setValue(opts.dx);
      if (!existing[9] && opts.deadline) {
        sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 10).setValue(opts.deadline);
        sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 11).setValue(
          opts.deadlineSource || PAYER_ACTION_TYPES[actionType].deadline_source_default
        );
      }
      sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 15).setValue(nowIso);

      // Conflict detection — surface, don't auto-resolve
      var conflicts = [];
      if (existing[6] && opts.memberId && String(existing[6]) !== opts.memberId) conflicts.push("memberId");
      if (existing[7] && opts.cpt && String(existing[7]) !== opts.cpt) conflicts.push("cpt");
      if (existing[5] && refNumber && String(existing[5]) !== refNumber) conflicts.push("refNumber");
      if (conflicts.length > 0) {
        var existingNotes = String(existing[16] || "");
        var conflictNote = "[CONFLICT " + nowIso + "] " + conflicts.join(",") + " differ across sources — review";
        sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 17).setValue(existingNotes ? existingNotes + " | " + conflictNote : conflictNote);
      }

      auditLog({
        action: "upsertPayerActionRow",
        user: "system",
        patientId: dncId,
        outcome: "merged",
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
        detail: "type=" + actionType + " sources=" + existingSources.length +
                (conflicts.length ? " conflicts=" + conflicts.join(",") : "")
      });

      return { actionId: existing[0], created: false, merged: true };
    }

    // ===== CREATE =====
    var actionId = generatePayerActionId();
    sheet.appendRow([
      actionId,                                                                    //  1
      dncId,                                                                       //  2
      patientName,                                                                 //  3
      payer,                                                                       //  4
      actionType,                                                                  //  5
      refNumber,                                                                   //  6
      opts.memberId || "",                                                         //  7
      opts.cpt || "",                                                              //  8
      opts.dx || "",                                                               //  9
      opts.deadline || "",                                                         // 10
      opts.deadline ? (opts.deadlineSource || PAYER_ACTION_TYPES[actionType].deadline_source_default) : "",  // 11
      "open",                                                                      // 12
      JSON.stringify([sourceEntry]),                                               // 13
      nowIso,                                                                      // 14
      nowIso,                                                                      // 15
      "",                                                                          // 16
     "",                                                                          // 17
      siteId                                                                       // 18
    ]);
    auditLog({
      action: "upsertPayerActionRow",
      user: "system",
      patientId: dncId,
      outcome: "created",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "type=" + actionType + " channel=" + sourceEntry.channel
    });

    return { actionId: actionId, created: true, merged: false };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("upsertPayerActionRow failed for " + (opts.dncId || "?") + " (" + (opts.actionType || "?") + "): " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "upsertPayerActionRow",
      user: "system",
      patientId: opts.dncId || "",
      outcome: "error",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
    });
    return { actionId: null, error: String(err) };
  }
}

// ============================================================
// listPayerActionQueue — GET endpoint, returns open queue rows
// filtered to the calling user's assigned sites.
//
// Query params:
//   email        (optional) — user's email; resolves to allowedSites via
//                Users sheet. Empty/missing = admin view (no filter).
//   status       (optional) — filter by status field. Default "open".
//                Pass "all" to return everything.
//   includeStale (optional) — "1" to also include resolved/superseded/expired
//                from the last 30d. Default omits them.
//
// Returns:
//   { status: "ok", count: N, queue: [...] }
//
// Each queue item:
//   { actionId, dncId, patientName, payer, actionType, refNumber,
//     memberId, cpt, dx, deadline, deadlineSource, status,
//     sources (parsed array), sourceCount, hasConflict,
//     createdAt, lastUpdated, resolvedAt, notes, siteId }
//
// Urgency (red/amber/gray) is computed frontend-side because (a) backend
// can't reliably know user TZ, (b) urgency rolls forward while the user
// looks at the page, (c) the SLA-default-vs-explicit color rule is
// presentation logic. Backend just returns raw deadline + source.
//
// Site filter mirrors getReferrals — case-insensitive substring match
// either direction. Empty siteId on a row = admin fallback (always shown
// to admin view, hidden from non-admin views with non-empty allowedSites).
// ============================================================
function listPayerActionQueue(opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  try {
    var sheet = initPayerActionQueue();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { status: "ok", count: 0, queue: [] };
    }

    var email = String(opts.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var statusFilter = String(opts.status || "open").trim().toLowerCase();
    var includeStale = String(opts.includeStale || "") === "1";

    // Resolve user's allowedSites from Users sheet. Empty array = no filter
    // (admin view). Missing email also = admin view, by design — admin tools
    // and cron jobs can call without an email and see everything.
    var allowedSites = [];
    var isAdminView = !email;
    if (email) {
      try {
        var userRow = getUserRowByEmail(email);
        if (userRow && userRow.locations) {
          allowedSites = String(userRow.locations).split(",").map(function(s){
            return s.trim().toLowerCase();
          }).filter(function(s){ return s.length > 0; });
        }
        // User not in Users sheet OR has no locations → treat as admin
        // (don't silently zero the list — surfaces config gaps as visible)
        if (allowedSites.length === 0) isAdminView = true;
      } catch(e) {
        // Users sheet unreachable → admin fallback rather than empty list
        isAdminView = true;
      }
    }

    // Read all 18 cols, walk rows, filter, project
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 18).getValues();
    var thirtyDaysAgo = Date.now() - (30 * 86400000);
    var out = [];

    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      var row = data[i];
      var rowStatus = String(row[11] || "").toLowerCase();

      // Status filter
      if (statusFilter !== "all") {
        if (statusFilter === "open" && rowStatus !== "open") {
          // Allow recent stale rows through if includeStale flag set
          if (!includeStale) continue;
          var resolvedAt = row[15] ? new Date(row[15]).getTime() : 0;
          if (resolvedAt < thirtyDaysAgo) continue;
        } else if (statusFilter !== "open" && rowStatus !== statusFilter) {
          continue;
        }
      }

      // Site filter — admin view sees everything; otherwise canonical-substring
      // match either direction, plus empty siteId rows always pass (admin bucket)
      var rowSiteId = String(row[17] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!isAdminView && rowSiteId) {
        var siteMatched = false;
        for (var s = 0; s < allowedSites.length; s++) {
          var allowed = allowedSites[s];
          if (allowed.indexOf(rowSiteId) !== -1 || rowSiteId.indexOf(allowed) !== -1) {
            siteMatched = true; break;
          }
        }
        if (!siteMatched) continue;
      }
      // If !isAdminView && empty rowSiteId: skip — only admins see unassigned
      if (!isAdminView && !rowSiteId) continue;

      // Parse sources JSON; tolerate corruption
      var sources = [];
      try {
        sources = JSON.parse(String(row[12] || "[]"));
        if (!Array.isArray(sources)) sources = [];
      } catch(e) { sources = []; }

      var notes = String(row[16] || "");
      var hasConflict = notes.indexOf("[CONFLICT") !== -1;

      out.push({
        actionId:       row[0],
        dncId:          row[1],
        patientName:    row[2],
        payer:          row[3],
        actionType:     row[4],
        refNumber:      row[5],
        memberId:       row[6],
        cpt:            row[7],
        dx:             row[8],
        deadline:       row[9] ? new Date(row[9]).toISOString() : "",
        deadlineSource: row[10],
        status:         row[11],
        sources:        sources,
        sourceCount:    sources.length,
        hasConflict:    hasConflict,
        createdAt:      row[13] ? new Date(row[13]).toISOString() : "",
        lastUpdated:    row[14] ? new Date(row[14]).toISOString() : "",
        resolvedAt:     row[15] ? new Date(row[15]).toISOString() : "",
        notes:          notes,
        siteId:         row[17]
      });
    }

    // Sort: open rows by deadline asc (nulls last), resolved rows by
    // resolvedAt desc. Frontend can re-sort, but a sensible default
    // means the UI is useful even before any sort UI ships.
    out.sort(function(a, b) {
      var aOpen = String(a.status).toLowerCase() === "open";
      var bOpen = String(b.status).toLowerCase() === "open";
      if (aOpen !== bOpen) return aOpen ? -1 : 1;
      if (aOpen) {
        if (!a.deadline && !b.deadline) return 0;
        if (!a.deadline) return 1;
        if (!b.deadline) return -1;
        return new Date(a.deadline) - new Date(b.deadline);
      }
      var ar = a.resolvedAt ? new Date(a.resolvedAt).getTime() : 0;
      var br = b.resolvedAt ? new Date(b.resolvedAt).getTime() : 0;
      return br - ar;
    });

    auditLog({
      action: "listPayerActionQueue",
      user: email || "admin",
      outcome: "ok",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "count=" + out.length + " statusFilter=" + statusFilter +
              " adminView=" + isAdminView +
              (allowedSites.length ? " sites=" + allowedSites.join("|") : "")
    });

    return { status: "ok", count: out.length, queue: out };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("listPayerActionQueue failed: " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "listPayerActionQueue",
      user: opts.email || "admin",
      outcome: "error",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
    });
    return { status: "error", message: String(err) };
  }
}

// Smoke test — run from editor after deploy.
// Editor-only smoke test for listPayerActionQueue. Exercises admin view,
// email-filter view, status filter. No deployment required — runs in-process.
// Editor-only test for resolvePayerAction. Reads the most-recent open
// row from the queue, resolves it, then verifies state by re-reading.
// Re-running this test will hit the "already-resolved → re-resolve" path.
// ============================================================
// backfillPayerActionSiteIds — one-shot helper to populate Site ID on
// queue rows that pre-date the schema bump (or that failed throughput
// lookup at create time).
//
// For each row with empty Site ID:
//   1. Look up the patient's row in throughput by DNC ID
//   2. Read site_id from throughput col 19
//   3. Write into queue col 18
//
// Rows where DNC ID isn't in throughput (e.g. test rows like DNC-TEST)
// stay with empty Site ID — they remain in the admin-fallback bucket,
// which is the correct behavior. Don't invent data.
//
// Idempotent. Safe to re-run. Reports how many rows it touched.
// Run from the editor once per environment (dev sheet, prod sheet) after
// deploying the schema bump. Don't put this on a trigger.
// ============================================================
function backfillPayerActionSiteIds() {
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  Logger.log("===== backfillPayerActionSiteIds =====");
  try {
    var sheet = initPayerActionQueue();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      Logger.log("Queue is empty, nothing to backfill.");
      return { status: "ok", scanned: 0, updated: 0, skipped: 0 };
    }

    var tp = initThroughputSheet();
    var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 18).getValues();
    var updated = 0;
    var skipped = 0;

    for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      var row = data[i];
      var existingSite = String(row[17] || "").trim();
      if (existingSite) { continue; } // already populated

      var dncId = String(row[1] || "").trim();
      if (!dncId) { skipped++; continue; }

      var tpRow = findThroughputRow(tp, dncId);
      if (tpRow < 0) { skipped++; continue; } // not in throughput → admin fallback bucket

      var site = String(tp.getRange(tpRow, 19).getValue() || "").trim();
      if (!site) { skipped++; continue; } // throughput row exists but has no site

      sheet.getRange(i + 2, 18).setValue(site);
      updated++;
      Logger.log("  Backfilled " + row[0] + " (" + dncId + ") → site '" + site + "'");
    }

    auditLog({
      action: "backfillPayerActionSiteIds",
      user: "system",
      outcome: "ok",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "scanned=" + data.length + " updated=" + updated + " skipped=" + skipped
    });

    Logger.log("Done. scanned=" + data.length + " updated=" + updated + " skipped=" + skipped);
    return { status: "ok", scanned: data.length, updated: updated, skipped: skipped };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("backfillPayerActionSiteIds failed: " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "backfillPayerActionSiteIds",
      user: "system",
      outcome: "error",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
    });
    return { status: "error", message: String(err) };
  }
}

function testResolvePayerAction() {
  Logger.log("===== resolvePayerAction smoke test =====");

  // Pick a target row — most recent open row
  var listed = listPayerActionQueue({ status: "open" });
  if (!listed.queue || listed.queue.length === 0) {
    Logger.log("No open rows to resolve. Run testPayerActionQueue first to seed.");
    return;
  }
  var target = listed.queue[0];
  Logger.log("Target: " + target.actionId + " (" + target.dncId + " / " + target.actionType + ")");

  // Test 1: invalid resolution
  var r1 = resolvePayerAction({ actionId: target.actionId, resolution: "garbage" });
  Logger.log("Invalid resolution: " + JSON.stringify(r1));

  // Test 2: missing actionId
  var r2 = resolvePayerAction({ resolution: "resolved" });
  Logger.log("Missing actionId: " + JSON.stringify(r2));

  // Test 3: not found
  var r3 = resolvePayerAction({ actionId: "PAQ-DOES-NOT-EXIST", resolution: "resolved" });
  Logger.log("Not found: " + JSON.stringify(r3));

  // Test 4: real resolution
  var r4 = resolvePayerAction({
    actionId: target.actionId,
    resolution: "resolved",
    notes: "smoke test — please ignore",
    email: "test@example.com"
  });
  Logger.log("Real resolve: " + JSON.stringify(r4));

  // Test 5: re-read via list, confirm status flipped
  var afterList = listPayerActionQueue({ status: "resolved" });
  var found = (afterList.queue || []).filter(function(q){ return q.actionId === target.actionId; })[0];
  if (found) {
    Logger.log("Verified: actionId=" + found.actionId +
               " status=" + found.status +
               " resolvedAt=" + found.resolvedAt);
  } else {
    Logger.log("FAIL: resolved row not found in status=resolved list");
  }

  Logger.log("===== done =====");
}
function testListPayerActionQueue() {
  Logger.log("===== listPayerActionQueue smoke test =====");

  // Test 1: admin view (no email) — should return ALL open rows
  var r1 = listPayerActionQueue({});
  Logger.log("Admin view: status=" + r1.status + " count=" + r1.count);
  if (r1.queue && r1.queue.length > 0) {
    var sample = r1.queue[0];
    Logger.log("  First row: " + sample.actionId + " / " + sample.dncId +
               " / " + sample.payer + " / " + sample.actionType +
               " / status=" + sample.status +
               " / sources=" + sample.sourceCount +
               " / siteId='" + sample.siteId + "'" +
               " / conflict=" + sample.hasConflict);
  }

  // Test 2: with email — exercises Users-sheet lookup path
  var r2 = listPayerActionQueue({ email: "evan.leitner@gmail.com" });
  Logger.log("Email-filtered view: status=" + r2.status + " count=" + r2.count);

  // Test 3: status=all — should return everything regardless of state
  var r3 = listPayerActionQueue({ status: "all" });
  Logger.log("Status=all: status=" + r3.status + " count=" + r3.count);

  // Test 4: status=resolved — should currently be 0 since nothing is resolved
  var r4 = listPayerActionQueue({ status: "resolved" });
  Logger.log("Status=resolved: status=" + r4.status + " count=" + r4.count);

  Logger.log("===== done =====");
}
// ============================================================
// resolvePayerAction — POST endpoint, marks a queue row resolved/superseded/expired.
//
// Body params:
//   actionId    (required) — PAQ-... id from the queue row
//   resolution  (required) — "resolved" | "superseded" | "expired"
//   notes       (optional) — free-text note appended to row notes column
//   email       (optional) — actor email for audit trail
//
// Returns:
//   { status: "ok", actionId, newStatus, resolvedAt }
