//   { status: "error", message }
//
// Side effects:
//   - Status col flipped to resolution value
//   - Resolved At col stamped with now()
//   - Last Updated col stamped with now()
//   - Notes col gets timestamped resolution entry appended
//   - logNotificationEvent fires for audit trail (queue history visible
//     alongside other notifications when card detail view ships)
//   - auditLog row written
//
// Idempotent-ish: resolving an already-resolved row updates the resolution
// type and stamps a fresh notes entry. Allows correction of "resolved →
// superseded" without exposing a separate "edit resolution" endpoint.
// ============================================================
function resolvePayerAction(opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  var validResolutions = { "resolved": true, "superseded": true, "expired": true };

  if (!opts.actionId) {
    return { status: "error", message: "actionId required" };
  }
  if (!opts.resolution || !validResolutions[opts.resolution]) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "resolution must be one of: resolved | superseded | expired"
    };
  }

  try {
    var sheet = initPayerActionQueue();
    if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { status: "error", message: "queue is empty" };
    }

    var actionId = String(opts.actionId);
    var resolution = String(opts.resolution).toLowerCase();
    var actorEmail = String(opts.email || "system").toLowerCase();
    var providedNotes = String(opts.notes || "").trim();

    // Find row by actionId — col 1
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    var matchRowIdx = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      if (String(ids[i][0]) === actionId) { matchRowIdx = i + 2; break; }
    }
    if (matchRowIdx < 0) {
      return { status: "error", message: "actionId not found: " + actionId };
    }

    var nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

    // Read existing row to capture context for notification event + notes append
    var existing = sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 1, 1, 18).getValues()[0];
    var dncId = String(existing[1] || "");
    var actionType = String(existing[4] || "");
    var prevStatus = String(existing[11] || "");

    // Build resolution note — timestamped, attributed, includes user-provided text
    var resolutionNote = "[" + nowIso + " " + actorEmail + "] " +
      (prevStatus.toLowerCase() === "open" ? "" : "(was " + prevStatus + ") ") +
      resolution +
      (providedNotes ? " — " + providedNotes : "");
    var existingNotes = String(existing[16] || "");
    var combinedNotes = existingNotes ? existingNotes + " | " + resolutionNote : resolutionNote;

    // Atomic-ish writes — Apps Script doesn't have transactions, but we're
    // writing 4 cells on a single row; collision risk is minimal.
    sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 12).setValue(resolution);   // Status
    sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 15).setValue(nowIso);       // Last Updated
    sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 16).setValue(nowIso);       // Resolved At
    sheet.getRange(matchRowIdx, 17).setValue(combinedNotes); // Notes

    // Audit trail — mirrors the dispatch logging in processBillerEmails so
    // the lifecycle of a payer action is reconstructable from one source.
    try {
      logNotificationEvent(
        dncId,
        actionType === "p2p_request" ? "p2p-request" : "appeal-required",
        "manual",
        "completed",
        "Queue action " + resolution + " by " + actorEmail +
          (providedNotes ? ": " + providedNotes : ""),
        { actionId: actionId, resolution: resolution, prevStatus: prevStatus },
        actorEmail
      );
    } catch(logErr) {
      Logger.log("resolvePayerAction notification log failed: " + logErr);
    }

    auditLog({
      action: "resolvePayerAction",
      user: actorEmail,
      patientId: dncId,
      outcome: "ok",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "actionId=" + actionId + " resolution=" + resolution +
              " prevStatus=" + prevStatus
    });

    return {
      status: "ok",
      actionId: actionId,
      newStatus: resolution,
      resolvedAt: nowIso
    };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("resolvePayerAction failed: " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "resolvePayerAction",
      user: opts.email || "system",
      outcome: "error",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
    });
    return { status: "error", message: String(err) };
  }
}

  Logger.log("===== Payer Action Queue smoke test =====");
  // Test 1: create
  var r1 = upsertPayerActionRow({
    dncId: "DNC-TEST",

}

function getUserRowByEmail(email) {
  if (!email) return null;
  var sheet = initUsersSheet();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase() === String(email).toLowerCase()) {
      return {
        rowIndex: i + 1,
        email: data[i][0],
        displayName: data[i][1],
        credentials: data[i][2],
        role: data[i][3],
        locations: String(data[i][4] || "").split(",").map(normalizeSiteId).filter(Boolean),
        licensedStates: String(data[i][5] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean),
        signatureUrl: data[i][6],
        createdAt: data[i][7],
        updatedAt: data[i][8]
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function getSiteById(siteId) {
  if (!siteId) return null;
  var canonicalLookup = normalizeSiteId(siteId);
  var sheet = initSitesSheet();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    // Compare canonical forms — sheet may have un-normalized rows from before
    // the canonicalization rollout. Normalize the row's siteId at read time.
    if (normalizeSiteId(data[i][0]) === canonicalLookup) {
      return {
        siteId: normalizeSiteId(data[i][0]), name: data[i][1], address: data[i][2], state: data[i][3],
        coveredStates: String(data[i][4] || data[i][3] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim().toUpperCase()}).filter(Boolean)
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// Returns all users (by email) assigned to the given site ID who have a
// clinical role — physicians and CRNAs. Used for preauth-approved "schedule
// D0" notifications so the people who actually book + do the procedure get
// pinged, not the MAs. Case-insensitive role match. Returns empty array if
// no site or no matching users.
function getClinicianEmailsAtSite(siteId) {
  if (!siteId) return [];
  var sheet = initUsersSheet();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var emails = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var role = String(data[i][3] || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    if (role !== "physician" && role !== "crna") continue;
    var locs = String(data[i][4] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean);
    if (locs.indexOf(siteId) !== -1 || locs.length === 0) {
      // locs.length === 0 = admin-style user with no site restriction, include them
      if (data[i][0]) emails.push(String(data[i][0]).trim());
    }
  }
  return emails;
}
// Detect priority signals in a Spruce conversation text field (e.g. Ashley's
// "Dr Thomas requests this patient be high priority"). Conservative — fires
// only on explicit priority keywords to keep SMS noise low. Returns the
// matched signal or null.
function detectPriorityContext(text) {
  if (!text || typeof text !== "string") return null;
  var patterns = [
    { rx: /\bhigh\s+priority\b/i, label: "high priority" },
    { rx: /\burgent\b/i, label: "urgent" },
    { rx: /\bASAP\b/, label: "ASAP" },
    { rx: /\bstat\b/i, label: "stat" },
    { rx: /\bexpedit/i, label: "expedited" },
    { rx: /\brush\b/i, label: "rush" }
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < patterns.length; i++) {
    if (patterns[i].rx.test(text)) {
      return { signal: patterns[i].label, fullText: text };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// List MA emails assigned to a given site. Mirrors getClinicianEmailsAtSite.
// Returns empty array if no site, no matching MAs, or Users sheet missing.
function getMaEmailsAtSite(siteId) {
  if (!siteId) return [];
  try {
    var sheet = initUsersSheet();
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var emails = [];
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      var role = String(data[i][3] || "").toLowerCase().trim();
      if (role !== "ma") continue;
      var locs = String(data[i][4] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean);
      // No site restriction OR site explicitly listed = include
      if (locs.length === 0 || locs.indexOf(siteId) !== -1) {
        if (data[i][0]) emails.push(String(data[i][0]).trim());
      }
    }
    return emails;
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("getMaEmailsAtSite failed: " + e);
    return [];
  }
}

// Fire on new referral auto-created via Spruce inbound path. Email goes to
// Lindsey + site-assigned MAs + Evan (auto-CC via sendClinicianEmail).
// If priority signal detected in conversation text, additionally fires SMS
// to Evan + Lindsey cells (if configured in Script Properties).
//
// Caller passes:
//   parsed         — extracted referral fields
//   sourceLabel    — e.g. "Secure msg via Spruce" or "Fax via Spruce"
//   siteId         — site assignment from throughput col 19 (may be empty)
//   rowIndex       — throughput row number for the new entry
//   priorityNote   — optional Spruce conversation text for priority detection
function sendReferralAddedNotification(parsed, sourceLabel, siteId, rowIndex, priorityNote) {
  try {
    var pName = parsed.name || "Unknown";
    var pDob = parsed.dob || "(unknown)";
    var pPhone = parsed.phone || "(unknown)";
    var pProvider = parsed.provider || "(unknown)";
    var pAddress = [parsed.address, parsed.city, parsed.state, parsed.zip]
                    .filter(Boolean).join(", ") || "(unknown)";

    var priority = detectPriorityContext(priorityNote);
    var subjectPrefix = priority ? "[DNC referral · " + priority.signal.toUpperCase() + "]" : "[DNC referral]";

    // Recipients: Lindsey + site-assigned MAs. Evan auto-CC'd by sendClinicianEmail.
    var toList = [CLINICIAN_EMAIL];
    var siteMas = getMaEmailsAtSite(siteId);
    if (siteMas.length === 0 && MA_EMAIL) {
      siteMas = [MA_EMAIL]; // fallback to hardcoded MA if no site assignments configured
    }
    siteMas.forEach(function(e){
      if (e && toList.indexOf(e) === -1) toList.push(e);
    });

    var bodyLines = [
      "New referral auto-created from " + sourceLabel + ".",
      ""
    ];
    if (priority) {
      bodyLines.push("⚠️ PRIORITY signal: " + priority.signal);
      bodyLines.push("Sender note: " + priority.fullText);
      bodyLines.push("");
    }
    bodyLines.push("Patient: " + pName);
    bodyLines.push("DOB: " + pDob);
    bodyLines.push("Phone: " + pPhone);
    bodyLines.push("Referring provider: " + pProvider);
    bodyLines.push("Address: " + pAddress);
    if (siteId) bodyLines.push("Site: " + siteId);
    bodyLines.push("Throughput row: " + (rowIndex || "?"));
    bodyLines.push("");
    bodyLines.push("Open the app: https://surghome.com/assess/");
    bodyLines.push("");
    bodyLines.push("If the parse looks wrong, edit the row in the Refs UI or throughput sheet.");

    sendClinicianEmail({
      to: toList.join(","),
      subject: subjectPrefix + " " + pName + " — via " + sourceLabel,
      body: bodyLines.join("\n")
    });

    // High-priority SMS path — fires only if priority detected AND cell
    // numbers are configured. Silent no-op otherwise.
    if (priority) {
      var smsBody = "DNC priority referral: " + pName + ". " +
                    "Signal: " + priority.signal + ". " +
                    "Phone: " + pPhone + ". Check app.";
      [EVAN_CELL, LINDSEY_CELL].forEach(function(num) {
        if (!num) return;
        try {
          sendOutboundSms(num, smsBody, {
            patientId: "DNC-NEW-REFERRAL",
            idempotencyKey: "newref-" + (rowIndex || Date.now())
          });
        } catch(e) {
          Logger.log("Priority SMS to " + num + " failed: " + e);
        }
      });
    }
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("sendReferralAddedNotification failed: " + e);
  }
}
// Match a referral to sites based on the referring clinic state (preferred) or patient state (fallback).
// Returns array of matching site IDs. Empty if no match.
function matchReferralToSites(referringClinicState, patientState) {
  var stateToMatch = (referringClinicState || patientState || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!stateToMatch) return [];
  var sheet = initSitesSheet();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var matches = [];
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var siteId = data[i][0];
    if (!siteId) continue;
    // Check covered states column first, fall back to site's own state
    var covered = String(data[i][4] || data[i][3] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim().toUpperCase()}).filter(Boolean);
    if (covered.indexOf(stateToMatch) !== -1) {
      // Always return canonical site IDs even if the sheet has drift
      matches.push(normalizeSiteId(siteId));
    }
  }
  return matches;
}

// Find the right physician to cosign for a given site.
// Rule: physician must (1) have this site in their locations OR (2) be licensed in this site's state.
// Returns email string, or null if nothing matches.
function findCosignerForSite(siteId) {
  var site = getSiteById(siteId);
  if (!site) return null;
  var sheet = initUsersSheet();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();

  var siteMatch = null;
  var stateMatch = null;
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    var role = String(data[i][3] || "").toLowerCase();
    if (role !== "physician") continue;
    var locs = String(data[i][4] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()});
    var states = String(data[i][5] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim().toUpperCase()});
    if (locs.indexOf(siteId) !== -1) { siteMatch = data[i][0]; break; }
    if (!stateMatch && states.indexOf(String(site.state).toUpperCase()) !== -1) {
      stateMatch = data[i][0];
    }
  }
  return siteMatch || stateMatch || null;
}

// Fetch a saved signature PNG as a blob, ready to embed in a Doc.
// Returns null on failure — but logs WHY at each failure point so the issue
// is debuggable. Earlier silent failure was hiding signature appendImage
// errors (URL format drift, permission issues, file deletion).
function fetchSignatureBlob(signatureUrl) {
  if (!signatureUrl) {
    Logger.log("fetchSignatureBlob: no signatureUrl provided");
    return null;
  }
  try {
    // File ID extraction: handles modern Drive URL format
    //   https://drive.google.com/file/d/<25+ char ID>/view
    // and older formats. Match anything 25+ chars of word/dash content.
    var match = signatureUrl.match(/[-\w]{25,}/);
    if (!match) {
      Logger.log("fetchSignatureBlob: no file ID pattern in URL: " + signatureUrl);
      return null;
    }
    var fileId = match[0];
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    if (!file) {
      Logger.log("fetchSignatureBlob: getFileById returned null for " + fileId);
      return null;
    }
    var blob = file.getBlob();
    Logger.log("fetchSignatureBlob: ok — fileId=" + fileId + " size=" + blob.getBytes().length);
    return blob;
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("fetchSignatureBlob: failed for url '" + signatureUrl + "': " + e);
    return null;
  }
}

// Append a signature block (image + name + timestamp) to a Doc body.
// Used by both writeDoc/createDoc paths. Author signature on creation; cosigner appended later.
// Logs success/failure of image append so silent fails surface.
function appendSignatureBlock(body, label, user, sigBlob) {
  body.appendParagraph("");
  body.appendParagraph("_______________________________");
  var caption = body.appendParagraph(
    label + ": " + (user.displayName || user.email) +
    (user.credentials ? ", " + user.credentials : "") +
    "    " + new Date().toLocaleString()
  );
  if (sigBlob) {
    try {
      body.appendImage(sigBlob).setWidth(220);
      Logger.log("appendSignatureBlock: image appended for " + (user.email || "(no email)"));
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log("appendSignatureBlock: appendImage FAILED for " + (user.email || "(no email)") + ": " + e);
    }
  } else {
    Logger.log("appendSignatureBlock: NO BLOB for " + (user.email || "(no email)") + " (signatureUrl=" + (user.signatureUrl || "empty") + ")");
  }
  return caption;
}

// Diagnostic: walk every Users row, fetch their signature, log status.
// Run from editor to identify which users have working/broken signatures.
function diagnoseAllSignatures() {
  Logger.log("===== SIGNATURE DIAGNOSTIC =====");
  var sheet = initUsersSheet();
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) { Logger.log("No users."); return; }
  var rows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 9).getValues();
  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    var email = rows[i][0];
    var sigUrl = rows[i][6];
    if (!email) continue;
    Logger.log("--- " + email + " ---");
    Logger.log("  Signature URL: " + (sigUrl || "(EMPTY)"));
    if (sigUrl) {
      var blob = fetchSignatureBlob(sigUrl);
      if (blob) {
        Logger.log("  Status: OK (blob size " + blob.getBytes().length + " bytes, type " + blob.getContentType() + ")");
      } else {
        Logger.log("  Status: FETCH FAILED — see prior log lines for reason");
      }
    } else {
      Logger.log("  Status: NO SIGNATURE SAVED");
    }
  }
  Logger.log("===== END SIGNATURE DIAGNOSTIC =====");
}

// Get or create a named subfolder within a parent folder.
function getOrCreateSubfolder(parentFolder, name) {
  var existing = parentFolder.getFoldersByName(name);
  if (existing.hasNext()) return existing.next();
  return parentFolder.createFolder(name);
}

// Create a standalone "Insurance Cards" doc containing only the insurance card images.
// Returns { id, url, name } or null if nothing to create.
function createInsuranceCardsDoc(parentFolder, photos, datePrefix) {
  if (!photos || photos.length === 0) return null;
  var cardPhotos = photos.filter(function(p) {
    return p.type && p.type.indexOf("insurance_") === 0;
  });
  if (cardPhotos.length === 0) return null;

  var docName = (datePrefix ? datePrefix + " - " : "") + "Insurance Cards";
  var doc = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.clear();
  var st = {};
  st[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_FAMILY] = "Calibri";
  st[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_SIZE] = 11;

  var title = body.appendParagraph("INSURANCE CARDS");
  if (title && title.setAttributes) {
    title.setAttributes(st);
    title.editAsText().setBold(true).setFontSize(14);
  }
  body.appendParagraph("Captured: " + new Date().toLocaleString());
  body.appendParagraph("");

  // Order the photos: primary front, primary back, secondary front, secondary back
  var order = ["insurance_primary_front","insurance_primary_back","insurance_secondary_front","insurance_secondary_back"];
  cardPhotos.sort(function(a,b) { return order.indexOf(a.type) - order.indexOf(b.type); });

  var labels = {
    "insurance_primary_front": "Primary Insurance — Front",
    "insurance_primary_back": "Primary Insurance — Back",
    "insurance_secondary_front": "Secondary Insurance — Front",
    "insurance_secondary_back": "Secondary Insurance — Back"
  };

  for (var i = 0; i < cardPhotos.length; i++) {
    var photo = cardPhotos[i];
    var lbl = body.appendParagraph(labels[photo.type] || photo.type);
    if (lbl && lbl.setAttributes) {
      lbl.setAttributes(st);
      lbl.editAsText().setBold(true);
    }
    try {
      var b64 = photo.data.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
      var decoded = Utilities.base64Decode(b64);
      var blob = Utilities.newBlob(decoded, "image/jpeg", photo.type + ".jpg");
      body.appendImage(blob).setWidth(400);
    } catch(imgErr) {
      body.appendParagraph("[Image failed: " + imgErr.toString() + "]");
    }
    body.appendParagraph("");
  }

  doc.saveAndClose();
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  parentFolder.addFile(file);
  DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file);
  return { id: doc.getId(), url: doc.getUrl(), name: docName };
}

// Send SMS to patient asking for insurance card photos via Spruce API.
// Requires SPRUCE_API_TOKEN in Script Properties. Run setupSpruceIntegration() once
// to cache the phone endpoint ID. Returns { ok: boolean, message: string }.
function sendInsuranceCardSms(toPhone, patientName) {
  if (!toPhone) return { ok: false, message: "No patient phone number" };

  // Normalize phone to E.164 (+1XXXXXXXXXX)
  var digits = String(toPhone).replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 10) digits = "1" + digits;
  if (digits.length !== 11) return { ok: false, message: "Invalid phone format: " + toPhone };
  var e164 = "+" + digits;

  var greeting = patientName ? ("Hi " + String(patientName).split(",")[0].split(" ")[0] + ", ") : "Hi, ";
  var text = greeting +
    "this is the Diabetic Nerve Clinic following up on your phone intake. " +
    "To finish your insurance verification, please text us photos of the FRONT and BACK of your primary insurance card. " +
    "If you have secondary insurance, please send the front and back of that card too. Thank you!";

  try {
    var phoneEndpointId = getSpruceEndpointIdByChannel("phone");
    // Message body per Spruce docs is a list of message elements with type=text
    var body = {
      destination: { smsOrEmailEndpoint: e164 },
      body: [{ type: "text", text: text }]
    };
    var result = spruceFetch("POST", "/internalendpoints/" + phoneEndpointId + "/conversations", body);
    var reqId = (result && (result.requestID || result.RequestID || result.requestId)) || "";
    return { ok: true, message: "SMS sent to " + e164 + (reqId ? " (req " + reqId + ")" : "") };
  } catch(e) {
    return { ok: false, message: "Spruce SMS failed: " + e.toString() };
  }
}
// ============================================================
// OUTBOUND SMS — via dnc-spruce-sender Cloud Run service
// ============================================================
// Two-layer auth:
//   1. Cloud Run IAM invoker: OIDC ID token minted as dnc-apps-script-caller@
//      service account (audience = service URL). Validated by Google's frontend
//      before request reaches the container.
//   2. App-layer X-Internal-Token: shared secret in DNC_INTERNAL_TOKEN script
//      property, matched against dnc-internal-token in Secret Manager by the
//      Cloud Run service itself.
// Both must pass. The IAM layer is what required the serviceAccountTokenCreator
// grant on dnc-apps-script-caller@ for the Apps Script executing identity.

var SPRUCE_SENDER_URL = "https://dnc-spruce-sender-3bgbug5gma-uk.a.run.app";
var SPRUCE_SENDER_SA = "dnc-apps-script-caller@core-waters-493614-n3.iam.gserviceaccount.com";

/**
 * Mint a short-lived OIDC ID token impersonating the Cloud Run invoker SA.
 * Uses the Apps Script executing identity's OAuth token to call IAM
 * Credentials API, which returns an ID token signed by Google with audience
 * = the Cloud Run service URL. Cloud Run validates that audience and the
 * SA's invoker permission before letting the request through.
 *
 * Tokens are valid ~1 hour; we don't cache here because Apps Script
 * function invocations are short-lived. If SMS volume scales, add
 * CacheService caching keyed on audience.
 */
function mintCloudRunIdToken_(audience) {
  var url = "https://iamcredentials.googleapis.com/v1/projects/-/serviceAccounts/" +
            encodeURIComponent(SPRUCE_SENDER_SA) + ":generateIdToken";
  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
    method: "post",
    contentType: "application/json",
    headers: { "Authorization": "Bearer " + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() },
    muteHttpExceptions: true,
    payload: JSON.stringify({ audience: audience, includeEmail: true })
  });
  var code = resp.getResponseCode();
  var body = resp.getContentText();
  if (code < 200 || code >= 300) {
    throw new Error("generateIdToken " + code + ": " + body.substring(0, 300));
  }
  var parsed = JSON.parse(body);
  if (!parsed.token) throw new Error("generateIdToken returned no token: " + body.substring(0, 200));
  return parsed.token;
}

/**
 * Send an SMS to a patient via the dnc-spruce-sender Cloud Run service.
 *
 * @param {string} toPhone        - patient phone (any common format; service normalizes)
 * @param {string} messageBody    - text content (max 1600 chars per Spruce limit)
 * @param {object} [opts]         - optional. Recognized:
 *                                  patientId: "DNC-XXXX" for audit log
 *                                  idempotencyKey: dedup key passed to Spruce
 *                                                  (use when retries are possible)
 * @returns {object} { ok, conversationId?, requestId?, error?, status? }
 *                   Never throws. Caller branches on result.ok.
 */
function sendOutboundSms(toPhone, messageBody, opts) {
  var startedAt = Date.now();
  opts = opts || {};

  try {
    if (!toPhone) return { ok: false, error: "missing toPhone" };
    if (!messageBody) return { ok: false, error: "missing messageBody" };
    if (String(messageBody).length > 1600) {
      return { ok: false, error: "messageBody too long (" + messageBody.length + " > 1600)" };
    }

    var internalToken = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("DNC_INTERNAL_TOKEN");
    if (!internalToken) {
      return { ok: false, error: "DNC_INTERNAL_TOKEN not set in Script Properties" };
    }

    // Mint Cloud Run ID token (layer 1 auth)
    var idToken;
    try {
      idToken = mintCloudRunIdToken_(SPRUCE_SENDER_URL);
    } catch(tokenErr) {
      Logger.log("sendOutboundSms: ID token mint failed: " + tokenErr);
      auditLog({
        action: "sendOutboundSms",
        user: "system",
        patientId: opts.patientId || "",
        outcome: "id_token_mint_failed",
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
        detail: String(tokenErr).substring(0, 200)
      });
      return { ok: false, error: "id token mint: " + String(tokenErr) };
    }

    var payload = {
      phone: String(toPhone),
      body: String(messageBody)
    };
    if (opts.idempotencyKey) payload.idempotencyKey = String(opts.idempotencyKey);

    var resp;
    try {
      resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(SPRUCE_SENDER_URL, {
        method: "post",
        contentType: "application/json",
        headers: {
          "Authorization": "Bearer " + idToken,        // layer 1: Cloud Run IAM
          "X-Internal-Token": internalToken            // layer 2: app-level secret
        },
        muteHttpExceptions: true,
        payload: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
    } catch(fetchErr) {
      Logger.log("sendOutboundSms fetch failed: " + fetchErr);
      auditLog({
        action: "sendOutboundSms",
        user: "system",
        patientId: opts.patientId || "",
        outcome: "network_error",
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
        detail: String(fetchErr).substring(0, 200)
      });
      return { ok: false, error: "network: " + String(fetchErr) };
    }

    var status = resp.getResponseCode();
    var body = resp.getContentText();

    if (status < 200 || status >= 300) {
      Logger.log("sendOutboundSms non-2xx: " + status + " body=" + body.substring(0, 300));
      auditLog({
        action: "sendOutboundSms",
        user: "system",
        patientId: opts.patientId || "",
        outcome: "http_" + status,
        latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
        detail: body.substring(0, 200)
      });
      return { ok: false, status: status, error: "http " + status, body: body.substring(0, 300) };
    }

    var parsed = {};
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(body); } catch(e) { /* fall through */ }

    auditLog({
      action: "sendOutboundSms",
      user: "system",
      patientId: opts.patientId || "",
      outcome: "ok",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: "convId=" + (parsed.conversationId || "")
    });

    return {
      ok: true,
      conversationId: parsed.conversationId || "",
      requestId: parsed.requestId || ""
    };

  } catch(err) {
    Logger.log("sendOutboundSms unexpected error: " + err);
    auditLog({
      action: "sendOutboundSms",
      user: "system",
      patientId: opts.patientId || "",
      outcome: "exception",
      latencyMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      detail: String(err).substring(0, 200)
    });
    return { ok: false, error: String(err) };
  }
}

function testSendOutboundSms() {
  var TEST_PHONE = "+17349729930";  // ← put your own cell here
  var result = sendOutboundSms(TEST_PHONE, "DNC test message - safe to ignore.", {
    patientId: "DNC-TEST",
    idempotencyKey: "smoke-" + Date.now()
  });
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  return result;
}
// ============================================================
// SPRUCE FAX WEBHOOK — inbound referral auto-parse
// ============================================================

// Queue a Spruce webhook event into the Spruce Events sheet for async processing.
// Returns quickly so the webhook POST can respond <5s.
// ============================================================
// SPRUCE CONVERSATION BACKFILL (one-shot)
// ============================================================
// Pull recent inbound items with attachments from a specific Spruce
// conversation and run them through the same classify→route pipeline
// the live webhook uses. Use case: catching up on Ashley's secure
// messages (or any thread) sent before the webhook went live.
//
// Usage from editor:
//   backfillSpruceConversation("t_2NQDJISOVSG00", 48)
//     where the first arg is the Spruce conversation ID
//     (find it in the Spruce app URL: app.sprucehealth.com/.../thread/{id})
//     and the second arg is hours-back from now (default 24)
//
// Idempotency: relies on createReferralFromParsed's name+phone dedup
// against non-Complete throughput rows. Safe to run twice on the same
// thread — second run will see "duplicate" status and skip.
function backfillSpruceConversation(conversationId, hoursBack) {
  if (!conversationId) {
    Logger.log("Pass a Spruce conversation ID (e.g. 't_2NQDJISOVSG00') as the first argument.");
    return;
  }
  var hours = hoursBack || 24;
  var cutoffMs = Date.now() - hours * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  Logger.log("Backfill: conversation=" + conversationId + " hoursBack=" + hours +
             " (cutoff=" + new Date(cutoffMs).toISOString() + ")");

  // Step 1: list items in this conversation. Endpoint and response shape
  // unverified — log full first response so we can adapt if needed.
  var listResult;
  try {
    listResult = spruceFetch("GET", "/conversations/" + conversationId + "/items");
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("Failed to list items for " + conversationId + ": " + e);
    Logger.log("If this is a 404, the endpoint shape may differ. Try /conversationItems?conversationId=" + conversationId);
    return;
  }
  Logger.log("List response (first 1500 chars): " + JSON.stringify(listResult).substring(0, 1500));

  // Unwrap defensively — Spruce APIs sometimes wrap in {data:[...]}, sometimes return raw array
  var items = Array.isArray(listResult) ? listResult :
              (listResult && Array.isArray(listResult.data)) ? listResult.data :
              (listResult && Array.isArray(listResult.items)) ? listResult.items :
              (listResult && Array.isArray(listResult.conversationItems)) ? listResult.conversationItems :
              [];
  Logger.log("Found " + items.length + " items total in conversation");

  var processed = 0, skipped = 0, errored = 0, eligible = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    var item = items[i];
    var createdAt = item.createdAt ? new Date(item.createdAt).getTime() : 0;
    var isInbound = String(item.direction || "").toLowerCase() === "inbound";
    var hasAttachment = item.attachments && Array.isArray(item.attachments) && item.attachments.length > 0;

    if (createdAt < cutoffMs) { skipped++; continue; }
    if (!isInbound) { skipped++; continue; }
    if (!hasAttachment) { skipped++; continue; }

    eligible++;
    Logger.log("Eligible item " + (item.id || "?") + " from " + item.createdAt);

    // Items from the list endpoint may be abbreviated and lack a fresh
    // signedUrl. Refetch the full item to get a downloadable URL.
    var fullItem = item;
    if (item.id && (!item.attachments[0].data || !item.attachments[0].data.signedUrl)) {
      try {
        var full = spruceFetch("GET", "/conversationItems/" + item.id);
        fullItem = (full && (full.data || full.object)) || full;
        Logger.log("  Refetched full item for " + item.id);
      } catch(e) {
        Logger.log("  Refetch failed for " + item.id + ": " + e);
        errored++;
        continue;
      }
    }

    // Reuse the live webhook handler by constructing a synthetic event
    var syntheticEvent = {
      type: "conversationItem.created",
      data: { object: fullItem }
    };
    try {
      var handled = handleSpruceEvent(syntheticEvent);
      Logger.log("  → handled=" + handled);
      processed++;
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log("  → handler threw: " + e);
      errored++;
    }
  }

  Logger.log("Backfill complete. eligible=" + eligible +
             " processed=" + processed +
             " skipped=" + skipped +
             " errored=" + errored);
  Logger.log("Check Refs tab for new referrals and 'Unclassified Inbound Faxes' for non-Referral parks.");
}

function queueSpruceEvent(rawBody) {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Spruce Events");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sheet.appendRow(["Received At", "Event Type", "Status", "Payload", "Processed At", "Error"]);
    sheet.getRange(1,1,1,6).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#e5e7eb");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  var eventType = "unknown";
  try {
    var parsed = JSON.parse(rawBody || "{}");
    eventType = parsed.type || "unknown";
  } catch(e) {}
  sheet.appendRow([new Date(), eventType, "pending", rawBody || "", "", ""]);
}
// Park a non-Referral fax (or a Referral that failed parse) in a triage sheet.
// Surfaces the signed URL so the human can open the PDF and decide what to do.
// Signed URLs expire 15 minutes after the original webhook fired, so triage
// must happen quickly — or use the Spruce Conversation ID to find it in the
// Spruce inbox directly (the ID is logged so you can search Spruce's app).
function logUnclassifiedFax(item, classification, downloadUrl, note) {
  try {
    var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Unclassified Inbound Faxes");
    if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
      sheet.appendRow([
        "Received At",
        "Spruce Conversation ID",
        "Sender",
        "Document Type",
        "Confidence",
        "Reasoning",
        "Fax PDF (signed URL — 15 min expiry)",
        "Notes",
        "Status"
      ]);
      sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 9).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#fef3c7");
      sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
    var sender = (item.author && item.author.displayName) ||
                 (item.conversation &&
                  item.conversation.externalParticipants &&
                  item.conversation.externalParticipants[0] &&
                  item.conversation.externalParticipants[0].displayName) ||
                 "unknown";
    sheet.appendRow([
      new Date(),
      (item.conversation && item.conversation.id) || "",
      sender,
      classification.docType || "Other",
      classification.confidence || "low",
      classification.reasoning || "",
      downloadUrl || "",
      note || "",
      "pending_triage"
    ]);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("logUnclassifiedFax write failed: " + e);
  }
}
// Process any pending Spruce events. Run via a time-driven trigger (every 1 minute).
// Add the trigger manually: Apps Script editor > Triggers > Add Trigger > processSpruceEvents > Time-driven > Minutes timer > Every minute.
function processSpruceEvents() {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Spruce Events");
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) return; // another run is processing, skip
  try {
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][2]) !== "pending") continue;
      try {
        var event = JSON.parse(data[i][3] || "{}");
        var handled = handleSpruceEvent(event);
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 3).setValue(handled ? "done" : "skipped");
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 5).setValue(new Date());
      } catch(err) {
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 3).setValue("error");
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 5).setValue(new Date());
        sheet.getRange(i+1, 6).setValue(String(err).substring(0, 500));
      }
    }
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// Handle a single Spruce event. Returns true if the event was processed as a referral,
// false if it was filtered out (not a fax, not inbound, etc).
// NOTE: Exact field paths here are defensive guesses — verify against real payload in Logger
// after first webhook fires (check the Spruce Events sheet).
function handleSpruceEvent(event) {
  if (!event || event.type !== "conversationItem.created") return false;

  var item = event.data && event.data.object;
  if (!item) return false;

  Logger.log("Spruce webhook item: " + JSON.stringify(item).substring(0, 2000));

  // Filter: inbound only. Attachment check below screens chat-only messages.
  // Classifier downstream screens content quality.
  var channel = (item.conversation && item.conversation.type) ||
                (item.internalEndpoint && item.internalEndpoint.channel) ||
                item.channel || "";
  var direction = item.direction || (item.isInbound ? "inbound" : "");
  if (String(direction).toLowerCase() !== "inbound") {
    Logger.log("Skipping non-inbound event — channel=" + channel + " direction=" + direction);
    return false;
  }

  var channelLower = String(channel).toLowerCase();
  var sourceLabel = channelLower.indexOf("fax") !== -1 ? "Fax via Spruce" :
                    channelLower === "secure" || channelLower === "thread" ? "Secure msg via Spruce" :
                    channelLower === "phone" || channelLower === "sms" ? "Text via Spruce" :
                    "Spruce (" + channel + ")";

  // Find attachment
  var attachments = item.attachments || [];
  if (!Array.isArray(attachments) || attachments.length === 0) {
    Logger.log("No attachments on item " + (item.id || "?") + " — skipping");
    return false;
  }
  var att = attachments[0];
  var downloadUrl = (att.data && att.data.signedUrl && att.data.signedUrl.url) ||
                    att.url || att.downloadUrl || att.href ||
                    (att.links && att.links.download);
  if (!downloadUrl) {
    Logger.log("No download URL on attachment: " + JSON.stringify(att).substring(0, 500));
    return false;
  }

  // Download (signed URL pre-authed — no bearer token)
  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(downloadUrl, { muteHttpExceptions: true });
  var code = resp.getResponseCode();
  if (code < 200 || code >= 300) {
    throw new Error("Attachment download failed: " + code + " " + resp.getContentText().substring(0, 200));
  }
  var blob = resp.getBlob();
  var mediaType = blob.getContentType() || (att.data && att.data.mimetype) || "application/pdf";
  var base64 = Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());

  // STEP 1 — Haiku triage
  var classification = classifyInboundDoc(base64, mediaType);
  Logger.log("Classified: " + classification.docType + " (" + classification.confidence + ") — " + classification.reasoning);

  // STEP 2 — Route by type. Only Referral goes to Refs.
  if (classification.docType === "Referral") {
    var result;
    try {
      result = parseReferralImageBase64(base64, mediaType);
    } catch(e) {
      Logger.log("Referral parse threw: " + e);
      logUnclassifiedFax(item, classification, downloadUrl, "Sonnet parse error: " + String(e).substring(0, 150));
      return false;
    }
    var parsedFields = result && result.parsed;
    if (!parsedFields) {
      Logger.log("Referral parse returned no .parsed object");
      logUnclassifiedFax(item, classification, downloadUrl, "Parse returned no fields despite Referral classification");
      return false;
    }
   var created = createReferralFromParsed(parsedFields, sourceLabel);
    if (!created || created.status !== "ok") {
      var skipReason = created ? created.status : "null";
      Logger.log("createReferralFromParsed → " + skipReason + ". Routing to triage.");
      logUnclassifiedFax(item, classification, downloadUrl, "Classified Referral but row not created: " + skipReason);
      return false;
    }
    Logger.log("Referral row created (" + sourceLabel + "): " + JSON.stringify(created));

    // Non-blocking notification — Lindsey + site MAs + Evan via email;
    // priority signals additionally fire SMS to Evan + Lindsey.
    try {
      var refSiteId = "";
      try {
        var tSheet = initThroughputSheet();
        refSiteId = String(tSheet.getRange(created.rowIndex, 19).getValue() || "");
      } catch(siteErr) { /* siteId optional */ }
      sendReferralAddedNotification(parsedFields, sourceLabel, refSiteId,
                                    created.rowIndex, item.text || "");
    } catch(notifyErr) {
      Logger.log("Referral notification failed (non-blocking): " + notifyErr);
    }

    return true;
  }

  // Non-Referral: park in triage sheet
  logUnclassifiedFax(item, classification, downloadUrl, "Non-referral document type — manual triage required");
  return true;
}

// Shared tool definition for referral extraction. Both parse passes use this same tool,
// which constrains the model to emit schema-valid JSON in a tool_use block. Unlike
// prompt-based JSON extraction, the model cannot return markdown backticks or malformed output.
var REFERRAL_EXTRACTION_TOOL = {
  name: "extract_referral",
  description: "Extract patient referral information from a scanned referral document.",
  input_schema: {
    type: "object",
    properties: {
      name: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Patient full name as printed on the document. Prefer 'Last, First' format. Empty string if not legible."
      },
      dob: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Patient date of birth in MM/DD/YYYY format. Empty string if not legible."
      },
      phone: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Patient's own phone number, exactly as printed. NOT the referring office phone. Empty string if not present."
      },
      address: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Patient street address (number and street), empty if not present."
      },
      city: { type: "string", description: "Patient city" },
      state: { type: "string", description: "Patient state as 2-letter code (e.g. DE, MD, VA)" },
      zip: { type: "string", description: "Patient ZIP code, 5 or 9 digits" },
      provider: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Referring provider — the clinician who SIGNED the referral at the bottom of the document. NEVER use anyone from 'To:', 'Attn:', 'Recipient:' headers. Empty string if not legible."
      },
      orderDate: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Date the referral was written, signed, or sent. MM/DD/YYYY format. Empty string if not present."
      },
      insurancePrimary: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Primary insurance plan name. PRESERVE the full carrier + plan designation. If the document says 'Medicare Advantage', 'MA Plan', 'HMO', 'PPO', or 'Replacement', this is a Medicare Advantage plan and you MUST include the private carrier name (CareFirst, Humana, Aetna, BCBS, etc.) plus the words 'Medicare Advantage'. Do NOT collapse MA plans to just 'Medicare'. Examples: 'Medicare Part B', 'Traditional Medicare', 'CareFirst BlueCross BlueShield Medicare Advantage PPO', 'Humana Medicare Advantage HMO', 'Aetna Better Health'."
      },
      memberIdPrimary: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Primary member ID exactly as printed. For Traditional Medicare, this is an 11-character alphanumeric MBI (like 1EG4-TE5-MK73). For Medicare Advantage and commercial plans, this is whatever the document labels as Member ID or Subscriber ID. Read carefully: common OCR confusions are 0/O, 1/I/l, 5/S, 8/B. If a character is genuinely illegible, use '?' in that position rather than guessing."
      },
      insuranceSecondary: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Secondary insurance plan name. ONLY populate this if the patient has Traditional Medicare AND a separate supplement/Medigap/secondary plan. LEAVE EMPTY if the patient has Medicare Advantage — MA is a single replacement plan, not Medicare + supplement. LEAVE EMPTY if only one insurance is listed."
      },
      memberIdSecondary: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Secondary member ID. A different number from the primary ID. Empty if no secondary insurance."
      },
      contraindications: {
        type: "array",
        items: { type: "string" },
        description: "Implanted devices or contraindicating conditions explicitly documented as patient conditions. Use exact labels: pacemaker, defibrillator, loop recorder, spinal cord stimulator, deep brain stimulator, bladder stimulator, pregnancy, hemophilia, cognitive impairment. Do NOT flag 'ICD-10' or 'ICD-9' (coding systems) as ICD (defibrillator device) — those are unrelated. Empty array if none detected."
      },
      notes: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Reason for referral, diagnosis, and any other clinical context. Empty string if none."
      },
      referringClinicName: {
        type: "string",
        description: "Name of the referring clinic or practice — usually in the letterhead at the top of the referral. E.g. 'Chesapeake Primary Care', 'Peninsula Regional Medical Center'. Empty if not visible."
      },
      referringClinicCity: {
        type: "string",
        description: "City of the referring clinic — from the letterhead address, NOT the patient address. Empty if not visible."
      },
      referringClinicState: {
        type: "string",
        description: "State of the referring clinic as 2-letter code — from the letterhead address. This is critical for routing and is usually more reliable than the patient state. Empty if not visible."
      }
    },
    required: ["name", "dob", "phone", "provider", "insurancePrimary", "contraindications"]
  }
};

// ============================================================
// LLM CALL CHOKEPOINT — Vertex AI (BAA-covered)
// ============================================================
// Every LLM call goes through this function. Routes to Anthropic models on
// Vertex AI for HIPAA/BAA coverage (Anthropic API direct does not have a
// BAA available at our usage volume; Google Cloud's BAA covers Vertex).
//
// Auth: ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() returns a token bound to the script
// owner's Google identity (executeAs: USER_DEPLOYING in appsscript.json).
// That identity has aiplatform.user on project 575474053150 by virtue
// of being project owner.
//
// Returns: parsed response body (Messages API shape — same as direct
// Anthropic API, so call sites don't need to change).
// Throws on non-2xx response.
//
// Config in Script Properties (override defaults if needed):
//   VERTEX_PROJECT_ID — defaults to "575474053150"
//   VERTEX_REGION     — defaults to "us-east5"
function callLLM(model, payload) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var projectId = props.getProperty("VERTEX_PROJECT_ID") || "575474053150";
  var region = props.getProperty("VERTEX_REGION") || "us-east5";
  var token = ScriptApp.getOAuthToken();
  var url = "https://" + region + "-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/" + projectId +
            "/locations/" + region + "/publishers/anthropic/models/" + model + ":rawPredict";
  // Vertex requires anthropic_version IN THE BODY (not a header), and uses
  // "vertex-2023-10-16" — distinct from the public API's "2023-06-01" header.
  // Model goes in the URL path, NOT the body — strip it if caller included it.
  var body = Object.assign({}, payload, { anthropic_version: "vertex-2023-10-16" });
  delete body.model;
  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
    method: "post",
    contentType: "application/json",
    headers: { "Authorization": "Bearer " + token },
    muteHttpExceptions: true,
    payload: JSON.stringify(body)
  });
  var code = resp.getResponseCode();
  var text = resp.getContentText();
  if (code < 200 || code >= 300) {
    throw new Error("Vertex LLM " + code + " (" + model + "): " + text.substring(0, 400));
  }
  return JSON.parse(text);
}
// Make one tool-use extraction call. Returns the args object from the tool_use block.
// Throws on API error or missing tool_use response.
function extractReferralWithTool(base64, mediaType, systemPrompt, model) {
  var contentBlock;
  if (String(mediaType).toLowerCase().indexOf("pdf") !== -1) {
    contentBlock = { type: "document", source: { type: "base64", media_type: "application/pdf", data: base64 } };
  } else {
    contentBlock = { type: "image", source: { type: "base64", media_type: mediaType, data: base64 } };
  }
  var apiData = callLLM(model || "claude-sonnet-4-6", {
    max_tokens: 2000,
    system: systemPrompt,
    tools: [REFERRAL_EXTRACTION_TOOL],
    tool_choice: { type: "tool", name: "extract_referral" },
    messages: [{
      role: "user",
      content: [
        contentBlock,
        { type: "text", text: "Call extract_referral with every field you can read from this document. Use empty strings for fields you cannot read confidently — do not guess." }
      ]
    }]
  });
  if (!apiData.content || !Array.isArray(apiData.content)) {
    throw new Error("Claude returned no content");
  }
  for (var i = 0; i < apiData.content.length; i++) {
    if (apiData.content[i].type === "tool_use" && apiData.content[i].name === "extract_referral") {
      return apiData.content[i].input || {};
    }
  }
  throw new Error("No tool_use block in response");
}
// ============================================================
// INBOUND DOC CLASSIFIER (Haiku triage)
// ============================================================
// Called on every Spruce fax BEFORE we attempt referral extraction.
// Purpose: keep junk (P2P requests, denials, lab results, etc.) out of
// the Refs queue. Only Referrals route to parseReferralImageBase64.
// Everything else gets parked in "Unclassified Inbound Faxes" for manual
// triage until per-type handlers are built.
//
// Cost: ~$0.005/fax on Vertex Haiku. Trivial vs. pollution risk.
// Failure mode: returns docType="Other" so faxes park rather than drop.
function classifyInboundDoc(base64, mediaType) {
  var systemPrompt =
    "You classify scanned medical documents into one of these categories:\n" +
    "- Referral: A document explicitly referring a patient for clinical consultation, evaluation, or procedure. Has a referring provider signature. Often titled 'Referral', 'Consult Request', or 'Order'. Contains diagnostic codes and a clinical reason for the referral.\n" +
    "- P2P Request: Peer-to-peer review request from a payer (Humana, BCBS, Aetna, UHC, etc.) asking a physician to call to discuss a denied authorization. Usually has 'Peer to Peer' or 'P2P' in the document.\n" +
    "- Auth Approval: Insurance prior authorization approval letter.\n" +
    "- Auth Denial: Insurance prior authorization denial letter or adverse determination.\n" +
    "- Eligibility Verification: Insurance eligibility printout or verification document.\n" +
    "- Medicare Card: Image of a Medicare insurance card (front or back).\n" +
    "- Insurance Card: Image of a non-Medicare insurance card.\n" +
    "- Lab Result: Laboratory test results.\n" +
    "- Clinical Note: Progress note, H&P, op note, or other clinical documentation.\n" +
    "- Other: Doesn't fit any category above, or document is blank/illegible.\n\n" +
    "A document containing a patient name and DOB is NOT automatically a Referral. Auth letters, denials, P2P requests, lab results, and clinical notes ALL contain patient demographic info. Look at document type, header, and purpose — not just the presence of patient data.\n\n" +
    "Use the classify_document tool to return your answer.";

  var tool = {
    name: "classify_document",
    description: "Classify a scanned medical document.",
    input_schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        documentType: {
          type: "string",
          enum: ["Referral", "P2P Request", "Auth Approval", "Auth Denial",
                 "Eligibility Verification", "Medicare Card", "Insurance Card",
                 "Lab Result", "Clinical Note", "Other"]
        },
        confidence: { type: "string", enum: ["high", "medium", "low"] },
        reasoning: { type: "string", description: "One-sentence rationale citing what you saw in the document." }
      },
      required: ["documentType", "confidence"]
    }
  };

  var contentBlock;
  if (String(mediaType).toLowerCase().indexOf("pdf") !== -1) {
    contentBlock = { type: "document", source: { type: "base64", media_type: "application/pdf", data: base64 } };
  } else {
    contentBlock = { type: "image", source: { type: "base64", media_type: mediaType, data: base64 } };
  }

  try {
    var apiData = callLLM("claude-haiku-4-5", {
      max_tokens: 500,
      system: systemPrompt,
      tools: [tool],
      tool_choice: { type: "tool", name: "classify_document" },
      messages: [{
        role: "user",
        content: [contentBlock, { type: "text", text: "Classify this document." }]
      }]
    });

    var toolUseBlock = null;
    if (apiData && Array.isArray(apiData.content)) {
      for (var i = 0; i < apiData.content.length; i++) {
        if (apiData.content[i].type === "tool_use" && apiData.content[i].name === "classify_document") {
          toolUseBlock = apiData.content[i];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!toolUseBlock) {
      Logger.log("classifyInboundDoc: no tool_use block in response");
      return { docType: "Other", confidence: "low", reasoning: "no tool_use block in classifier response" };
    }
    return {
      docType: toolUseBlock.input.documentType || "Other",
      confidence: toolUseBlock.input.confidence || "low",
      reasoning: toolUseBlock.input.reasoning || ""
    };
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("classifyInboundDoc threw: " + e);
    return { docType: "Other", confidence: "low", reasoning: "classifier error: " + String(e).substring(0, 150) };
  }
}
function testVertexCallLLM() {
  try {
    var result = callLLM("claude-sonnet-4-6", {
      max_tokens: 100,
      messages: [{ role: "user", content: "Say hello in 5 words." }]
    });
    Logger.log("SUCCESS");
    Logger.log("Response content: " + JSON.stringify(result.content));
    Logger.log("Stop reason: " + result.stop_reason);
    Logger.log("Usage: " + JSON.stringify(result.usage));
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("FAILURE: " + e.toString());
  }
}
// Format validators. Return { ok: boolean, reason: string }.
// Used after extraction to flag fields that may be wrong even if both passes agreed.
var REFERRAL_VALIDATORS = {
  phone: function(v) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" }; // empty is fine, caught by missing check elsewhere
    var digits = String(v).replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (digits.length === 10 || digits.length === 11) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    return { ok: false, reason: "phone should be 10 or 11 digits, got " + digits.length };
  },
  dob: function(v) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    var m = /^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{4})$/.exec(String(v).trim());
    if (!m) return { ok: false, reason: "DOB not in MM/DD/YYYY format" };
    var mm = parseInt(m[1], 10), dd = parseInt(m[2], 10), yyyy = parseInt(m[3], 10);
    if (mm < 1 || mm > 12 || dd < 1 || dd > 31) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid month/day" };
    var now = new Date().getFullYear();
    if (yyyy < 1900 || yyyy > now) return { ok: false, reason: "year " + yyyy + " out of range" };
    var age = now - yyyy;
    if (age < 18 || age > 110) return { ok: false, reason: "age " + age + " unusual for DPN clinic" };
    return { ok: true, reason: "" };
  },
  zip: function(v) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    if (/^\d{5}(-\d{4})?$/.test(String(v).trim())) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    return { ok: false, reason: "ZIP should be 5 or 9 digits" };
  },
  state: function(v) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    if (/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(String(v).trim().toUpperCase())) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    return { ok: false, reason: "state should be 2-letter code" };
  },
  memberIdPrimary: function(v, allFields) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    if (String(v).indexOf("?") >= 0) return { ok: false, reason: "contains unreadable characters" };
    var insName = String(allFields && allFields.insurancePrimary || "").toLowerCase();
    var isTraditionalMedicare = /\bmedicare\b/.test(insName) && !/advantage|ma\s*plan|hmo|ppo/.test(insName);
    if (isTraditionalMedicare) {
      var stripped = String(v).replace(/[\s-]/g, "");
      if (stripped.length !== 11) return { ok: false, reason: "Traditional Medicare MBI should be 11 chars, got " + stripped.length };
      if (!/^[0-9A-Z]+$/i.test(stripped)) return { ok: false, reason: "MBI has non-alphanumeric characters" };
    } else if (String(v).length < 3) {
      return { ok: false, reason: "member ID too short" };
    }
    return { ok: true, reason: "" };
  },
  memberIdSecondary: function(v) {
    if (!v) return { ok: true, reason: "" };
    if (String(v).indexOf("?") >= 0) return { ok: false, reason: "contains unreadable characters" };
    return { ok: true, reason: "" };
  }
};

// Compare two extraction passes and tag each field with a confidence level.
// Modifies pass1 in place (filling in blanks from pass2) and returns the confidence map.
function reconcileReferralPasses(pass1, pass2) {
  var fieldsToCheck = [
    "name", "dob", "phone", "address", "city", "state", "zip",
    "provider", "orderDate",
    "insurancePrimary", "memberIdPrimary",
    "insuranceSecondary", "memberIdSecondary"
  ];
  var confidence = {};
  var disagreements = [];
  var warnings = [];

  function norm(v) {
    if (v === null || v === undefined) return "";
    return String(v).toLowerCase().replace(/[\s\-().#]/g, "").trim();
  }

  for (var i = 0; i < fieldsToCheck.length; i++) {
    var f = fieldsToCheck[i];
    var v1 = pass1[f];
    var v2 = pass2[f];
    var n1 = norm(v1);
    var n2 = norm(v2);

    // Both blank → missing
    if (!n1 && !n2) {
      confidence[f] = "missing";
      continue;
    }

    // Run format validator if present (uses pass1 values for cross-field context)
    var validator = REFERRAL_VALIDATORS[f];
    var formatOk = true;
    var formatReason = "";
    if (validator && n1) {
      var check = validator(v1, pass1);
      if (!check.ok) {
        formatOk = false;
        formatReason = check.reason;
        warnings.push(f + ": " + check.reason);
      }
    }

    // Only one pass returned a value
    if (n1 && !n2) {
      // Pass 1 has it, pass 2 blank — suspect (disagreement)
      confidence[f] = formatOk ? "suspect_single_pass" : "format_error";
      disagreements.push({ field: f, pass1: v1, pass2: "(blank)", reason: formatReason });
      continue;
    }
    if (!n1 && n2) {
      // Pass 2 has it, pass 1 blank — fill from pass 2 and mark suspect
      pass1[f] = v2;
      if (validator) {
        var check2 = validator(v2, pass1);
        if (!check2.ok) {
          confidence[f] = "format_error";
          warnings.push(f + ": " + check2.reason + " (from pass 2)");
        } else {
          confidence[f] = "suspect_single_pass";
        }
      } else {
        confidence[f] = "suspect_single_pass";
      }
      disagreements.push({ field: f, pass1: "(blank)", pass2: v2 });
      continue;
    }

    // Both returned values — compare
    if (n1 === n2) {
      confidence[f] = formatOk ? "confirmed" : "format_error";
      continue;
    }

    // Substring tolerance for insurance names (one may be more verbose than the other)
    var isSubstring = (n1.indexOf(n2) !== -1) || (n2.indexOf(n1) !== -1);
    if (isSubstring && (f === "insurancePrimary" || f === "insuranceSecondary" || f === "provider" || f === "name")) {
      // Prefer the longer version for these fields
      if (String(v2 || "").length > String(v1 || "").length) {
        pass1[f] = v2;
      }
      confidence[f] = formatOk ? "confirmed" : "format_error";
      continue;
    }

    // Real disagreement
    confidence[f] = "suspect_disagreement";
    disagreements.push({ field: f, pass1: v1, pass2: v2, reason: formatReason });
  }

  return {
    merged: pass1,
    confidence: confidence,
    disagreements: disagreements,
    warnings: warnings
  };
}

// Shared helper: two-pass referral extraction pipeline using tool-use structured outputs.
// Used by both the manual parseReferralPhoto action and the Spruce fax webhook processor.
// Returns { parsed, confidence, disagreements, warnings }. Throws on total failure.
function parseReferralImageBase64(base64, mediaType) {
  // Pass 1: comprehensive extraction with full instruction context
  var pass1System =
    "You are extracting patient referral information from a clinical referral document (fax, PDF, or photo). " +
    "Read the entire document carefully. Use the extract_referral tool to return structured data.\n\n" +
    "INSURANCE CLASSIFICATION — this is the most common error source:\n" +
    "PATTERN A (Traditional Medicare): Document shows 'Medicare', 'Medicare Part B', or 'Traditional Medicare' with NO 'Advantage', 'MA Plan', 'HMO', 'PPO', or 'Replacement' markings. Primary ID is an 11-char MBI. Often has a Medigap/supplement secondary.\n" +
    "PATTERN B (Medicare Advantage): Document shows 'Medicare Advantage', 'MA Plan', 'Medicare Replacement', OR shows a private carrier (CareFirst, BCBS, Humana, Aetna, UnitedHealthcare, Wellcare) WITH the word 'Medicare', often with PPO/HMO markings or CMS contract numbers. This is ONE plan, not two. insurancePrimary MUST preserve the full carrier name plus 'Medicare Advantage'. insuranceSecondary MUST be empty.\n" +
    "PATTERN C (Commercial/Other): No Medicare anywhere.\n\n" +
    "DECISION RULE: If you see 'Advantage', 'MA Plan', or 'Replacement' on an insurance line, it is Pattern B. Preserve the carrier name. Never collapse to just 'Medicare'.\n\n" +
    "REFERRING PROVIDER: The provider is whoever SIGNED the referral at the bottom. NEVER use anyone in 'To:', 'Attn:', or 'Recipient:' headers — those are the receiving clinic.\n\n" +
    "CONTRAINDICATIONS: Only flag explicit implanted devices or patient conditions. 'ICD-10' and 'ICD-9' are coding systems, not defibrillator devices — do not confuse them with 'ICD' (implantable cardioverter defibrillator).\n\n" +
    "ACCURACY: Read each character of member IDs carefully. Common OCR confusions: 0/O, 1/I/l, 5/S, 8/B. If a character is genuinely illegible, use '?' in that position rather than guessing. Leave any field blank that you cannot read confidently — a blank field is better than a guess.";

  // Pass 2: narrower, different framing to reduce shared bias between passes
  var pass2System =
    "You are a medical billing specialist doing data entry verification on a referral document. " +
    "Your job is to transcribe the patient's identity and insurance information with extreme care, because a single transcription error causes claim rejection.\n\n" +
    "Focus especially on these high-stakes fields: patient legal name, DOB, phone number, full address, primary insurance plan name including the administering carrier, and primary member ID. Read each character deliberately.\n\n" +
    "For the insurance plan name: transcribe it as it literally appears on the document. If the document shows 'CareFirst BlueCross BlueShield Medicare Advantage PPO', write that full string — do not abbreviate to 'Medicare' or 'BCBS'. If the document shows 'Medicare Part B' with no private carrier, write 'Medicare Part B'.\n\n" +
    "For the member ID: if any character is unreadable, mark it with '?' rather than guessing. Common confusions: 0 vs O, 1 vs I vs l, 5 vs S, 8 vs B.\n\n" +
    "The referring provider is the clinician who signed the referral, typically at the bottom of the document near their title (MD, DO, NP, PA). Never use names from the To/Attn/Recipient header.\n\n" +
    "Use the extract_referral tool to return what you read. Leave fields blank if you cannot read them — do not guess.";

  var pass1, pass2;
  try {
    pass1 = extractReferralWithTool(base64, mediaType, pass1System, "claude-sonnet-4-6");
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("Pass 1 failed: " + e);
    throw e; // hard fail — no point in pass 2 if pass 1 broke
  }

  try {
    pass2 = extractReferralWithTool(base64, mediaType, pass2System, "claude-sonnet-4-6");
  } catch(e) {
    // Pass 2 failed — return pass 1 with no verification, mark everything as suspect
    Logger.log("Pass 2 failed, returning pass 1 unverified: " + e);
    var fallbackConfidence = {};
    var fallbackFields = ["name","dob","phone","address","city","state","zip","provider","orderDate","insurancePrimary","memberIdPrimary","insuranceSecondary","memberIdSecondary"];
    for (var fi = 0; fi < fallbackFields.length; fi++) {
      fallbackConfidence[fallbackFields[fi]] = pass1[fallbackFields[fi]] ? "suspect_single_pass" : "missing";
    }
    return {
      parsed: pass1,
      confidence: fallbackConfidence,
      disagreements: [],
      warnings: ["Verification pass failed — all fields marked suspect"]
    };
  }

  var reconciled = reconcileReferralPasses(pass1, pass2);
  return {
    parsed: reconciled.merged,
    confidence: reconciled.confidence,
    disagreements: reconciled.disagreements,
    warnings: reconciled.warnings
  };
}

// Shared helper: create a referral throughput row from a parsed referral object.
// Applies the same name+phone dedup as the manual addReferral action.
function createReferralFromParsed(parsed, sourceLabel) {
  if (!parsed || !parsed.name || !parsed.phone) {
    Logger.log("Skipping referral — missing name or phone: " + JSON.stringify(parsed));
    return null;
  }
  var sheet = initThroughputSheet();

  function normName(s) { return String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); }
  function normPhone(s) { var d = String(s || "").replace(/\D/g, ""); return d.slice(-10); }
  var newName = normName(parsed.name);
  var newPhone = normPhone(parsed.phone);

  // Dedup against existing non-Complete rows
  if (newName && newPhone && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    var existing = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 18).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < existing.length; i++) {
      if (normName(existing[i][0]) === newName && normPhone(existing[i][2]) === newPhone) {
        var status = existing[i][15] || "";
        if (status !== "Complete" && String(status).indexOf("Removed") !== 0) {
          Logger.log("Dedup skip: " + parsed.name + " already in row " + (i+2));
          return { status: "duplicate", rowIndex: i + 2 };
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Build notes in same format as manual addReferral
  var parts = [];
  if (parsed.provider) parts.push("Ref: " + parsed.provider);
  if (Array.isArray(parsed.contraindications) && parsed.contraindications.length) {
    parts.push("CONTRA: " + parsed.contraindications.join(", "));
  }
  if (parsed.insurancePrimary || parsed.memberIdPrimary) {
    parts.push("Ins: " + (parsed.insurancePrimary || "Primary") + (parsed.memberIdPrimary ? " #" + parsed.memberIdPrimary : ""));
  }
  if (parsed.insuranceSecondary || parsed.memberIdSecondary) {
    parts.push("Sec: " + (parsed.insuranceSecondary || "Secondary") + (parsed.memberIdSecondary ? " #" + parsed.memberIdSecondary : ""));
  }
  if (parsed.notes) parts.push(parsed.notes);
  if (sourceLabel) parts.push("[" + sourceLabel + "]");
  var notesField = parts.join(" | ");

  // Use order date if parsed, else now
  var refDate = new Date().toISOString();
  if (parsed.orderDate) {
    var d = new Date(parsed.orderDate);
    if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) refDate = d.toISOString();
  }

  var dobString = parsed.dob ? "'" + String(parsed.dob).replace(/^'/, "") : "";
  // Auto-tag site from referring clinic state (falls back to patient state)
  var fSiteTag = matchReferralToSites(parsed.referringClinicState || "", parsed.state || "").join(",");
  sheet.appendRow([
    parsed.name, dobString, parsed.phone, "",
    refDate, "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "",
    "Referral Received", new Date().toISOString(), notesField,
    fSiteTag, 0, "", "", ""
  ]);
  return { status: "ok", rowIndex: sheet.getLastRow() };
}

// One-time setup: call this from the Apps Script editor AFTER setting SPRUCE_API_TOKEN.
// - Lists internal endpoints and caches phone + fax IDs
// - Registers the fax webhook pointing at this deployed web app
// You'll need to pass your deployed web app URL as the argument.
// Example: setupSpruceIntegration("https://script.google.com/macros/s/.../exec")
function setupSpruceIntegration(deployedWebAppUrl) {
  if (!deployedWebAppUrl) {
    Logger.log("Pass your deployed web app URL as the first argument.");
    return;
  }
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  // Clear any stale cached IDs so we re-fetch
  props.deleteProperty("SPRUCE_PHONE_ENDPOINT_ID");
  props.deleteProperty("SPRUCE_FAX_ENDPOINT_ID");

  // Cache phone and fax endpoint IDs (look at Logger output to verify response shape)
  try {
    var phoneId = getSpruceEndpointIdByChannel("phone");
    Logger.log("Cached SPRUCE_PHONE_ENDPOINT_ID: " + phoneId);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("Phone endpoint lookup failed: " + e);
  }
  try {
    var faxId = getSpruceEndpointIdByChannel("fax");
    Logger.log("Cached SPRUCE_FAX_ENDPOINT_ID: " + faxId);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("Fax endpoint lookup failed: " + e);
  }

  // Generate a webhook secret if we don't have one
  var webhookSecret = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  if (!webhookSecret) {
    webhookSecret = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
    props.setProperty("SPRUCE_WEBHOOK_SECRET", webhookSecret);
  }

  // Register the webhook. URL format: {deployed}?_action=spruceWebhook&secret=XYZ
  var webhookUrl = deployedWebAppUrl + (deployedWebAppUrl.indexOf("?") === -1 ? "?" : "&") +
                   "_action=spruceWebhook&secret=" + webhookSecret;
  Logger.log("Registering webhook URL: " + webhookUrl);

  try {
    var result = spruceFetch("POST", "/webhooks/endpoints", {
      name: "DNC App Fax Referrals",
      url: webhookUrl
    });
    Logger.log("Webhook registered: " + JSON.stringify(result));
    if (result && result.secretKey) {
      // Spruce's HMAC secret — we can't use it from Apps Script (no header access)
      // but store it for reference
      props.setProperty("SPRUCE_HMAC_SECRET", result.secretKey);
    }
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("Webhook registration failed: " + e);
  }

  Logger.log("Setup complete. Next steps:");
  Logger.log("1. Verify phone and fax endpoint IDs above");
  Logger.log("2. Add a time-driven trigger for processSpruceEvents (every 1 minute)");
  Logger.log("3. Test with testSpruceSms() to confirm SMS pipeline works");
}

// Test harness: send a test SMS via Spruce.
function testSpruceSms() {
  var result = sendInsuranceCardSms("+15555550123", "Test, Patient");
  Logger.log("testSpruceSms: " + JSON.stringify(result));
}

// Create a Cosign queue row for a doc that needs a physician signature.
// If no qualifying cosigner, falls back to FALLBACK_COSIGNER_EMAIL and notes the fallback.

// ============================================================
// SPRUCE HEALTH API
// ============================================================
// Auth: SPRUCE_API_TOKEN in Script Properties
// First-time setup: run setupSpruceIntegration() from the Apps Script editor
// to cache endpoint IDs and register the fax webhook.

var SPRUCE_API_BASE = "https://api.sprucehealth.com/v1";

// Generic Spruce API request wrapper. Throws on non-2XX.
function spruceFetch(method, path, body) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var token = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_API_TOKEN");
  if (!token) throw new Error("SPRUCE_API_TOKEN not set in Script Properties");
  var url = SPRUCE_API_BASE + path;
  var options = {
    method: method,
    headers: {
      "Authorization": "Bearer " + token,
      "Accept": "application/json"
    },
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  };
  if (body) {
    options.contentType = "application/json";
    options.payload = JSON.stringify(body);
  }
  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, options);
  var code = resp.getResponseCode();
  var text = resp.getContentText();
  if (code < 200 || code >= 300) {
    throw new Error("Spruce API " + method + " " + path + " -> " + code + ": " + text);
  }
  if (!text) return null;
  try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch(e) { return text; }
}

// Cached lookup: find the internal endpoint ID for a given channel (phone, fax, email, secure).
// First call hits the API; subsequent calls read from Script Properties.
// NOTE: The exact response shape for /internalendpoints needs verification on first run.
// This function handles a few common shapes defensively.
function getSpruceEndpointIdByChannel(channel) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var cacheKey = "SPRUCE_" + String(channel).toUpperCase() + "_ENDPOINT_ID";
  var cached = props.getProperty(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return cached;

  var result = spruceFetch("GET", "/internalendpoints");
  var endpoints = [];
  if (Array.isArray(result)) endpoints = result;
  else if (result && Array.isArray(result.data)) endpoints = result.data;
  else if (result && Array.isArray(result.internalEndpoints)) endpoints = result.internalEndpoints;
  else if (result && Array.isArray(result.endpoints)) endpoints = result.endpoints;

  Logger.log("Spruce internal endpoints: " + JSON.stringify(endpoints));

  for (var i = 0; i < endpoints.length; i++) {
    var ep = endpoints[i];
    var epChannel = ep.channel || (ep.endpoint && ep.endpoint.channel) || ep.type;
    if (String(epChannel).toLowerCase() === String(channel).toLowerCase()) {
      var id = ep.id || (ep.endpoint && ep.endpoint.id);
      if (id) {
        props.setProperty(cacheKey, id);
        return id;
      }
    }
  }
  throw new Error("No Spruce endpoint found for channel: " + channel + ". Check Logger output for actual response shape.");
}

// Create a Cosign queue row for a doc that needs a physician signature.
// If no qualifying cosigner, falls back to FALLBACK_COSIGNER_EMAIL and notes the fallback.
function createCosignEntry(opts) {
  var sheet = initCosignSheet();
  var matched = findCosignerForSite(opts.siteId);
  var cosignerEmail = matched || FALLBACK_COSIGNER_EMAIL;
  var fellBack = !matched;
  var cosignId = "CS-" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  sheet.appendRow([
    cosignId, opts.docType, opts.docName, opts.docUrl, opts.docId,
    opts.patientId, opts.authorEmail, opts.authorRole, opts.siteId,
    cosignerEmail, "pending", new Date(), "", fellBack ? "FALLBACK - no qualifying physician for site" : ""
  ]);
  // Advance throughput to "Awaiting Cosign" so the referral card reflects where
  // the work is stuck. Only advance if we're not already further along — don't
  // regress from Preauth Submitted back to Awaiting Cosign if someone belatedly
  // creates a new cosign entry on an old patient.
  try {
    if (opts.patientId) {
      var tpForCosign = initThroughputSheet();
      var rowForCosign = findThroughputRow(tpForCosign, opts.patientId);
      if (rowForCosign > 0) {
        var curStatus = String(tpForCosign.getRange(rowForCosign, 16).getValue() || "");
        var blockedByDownstream = curStatus === "Preauth Submitted" ||
                                  curStatus === "Preauth In Review" ||
                                  curStatus === "Authorized" ||
                                  curStatus.indexOf("Preauth Rejected") === 0 ||
                                  curStatus.indexOf("D0") === 0 ||
                                  curStatus.indexOf("D20") === 0 ||
                                  curStatus.indexOf("D90") === 0 ||
                                  curStatus.indexOf("Trial ") === 0 ||
                                  curStatus === "Reimbursed";
        if (!blockedByDownstream) {
          tpForCosign.getRange(rowForCosign, 16).setValue("Awaiting Cosign");
          tpForCosign.getRange(rowForCosign, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("createCosignEntry: throughput status update failed for " + opts.patientId + ": " + e);
  }
  // Notify cosigner. CC Evan on all cosign notifications so he can spot stale
  // queue items even when the actual cosigner is slow.
  try {
    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: cosignerEmail,
      cc: EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL,
      subject: "DNC Cosign needed: " + opts.docType + " - " + (opts.patientId || ""),
      body: "A document needs your cosignature.\n\n" +
            "Type: " + opts.docType + "\n" +
            "Patient: " + (opts.patientId || "") + "\n" +
            "Author: " + opts.authorEmail + " (" + opts.authorRole + ")\n" +
            "Site: " + opts.siteId + "\n\n" +
            "Document: " + opts.docUrl + "\n\n" +
            "Open the DNC app and go to the Cosign tab to sign." +
            (fellBack ? "\n\n[NOTE] You're receiving this as the fallback cosigner because no physician is mapped to site '" + opts.siteId + "'." : "")
    });
  } catch(e) { Logger.log("Cosign notify failed: " + e); }
  return { cosignId: cosignId, cosignerEmail: cosignerEmail, fellBack: fellBack };
}

// Manual backfill — call from the editor when a patient's preauth generated
// without its cosign entries (e.g. the Velez case where userEmail was missing
// from the payload and author lookup silently failed).
//
// Usage from Apps Script editor:
//   backfillCosignForPatient("DNC-XXXX", "bradley.burdick@huntington.com");
//
// Walks their patient folder in Drive, finds any Preauth H&P and LOMN docs,
// and creates a Cosign row for each. Uses the supplied authorEmail to look up
// author role + map to the right physician cosigner via site assignment.
// Safe to re-run — will create duplicate cosign rows if called twice for same
// patient, so check the Cosign sheet before running.
function backfillCosignForPatient(patientId, authorEmail) {
  if (!patientId) { Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: patientId required"); return; }
  if (!authorEmail) { Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: authorEmail required"); return; }

  var authorProfile = getUserRowByEmail(authorEmail);
  if (!authorProfile) {
    Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: no Users row for " + authorEmail);
    return;
  }
  if (String(authorProfile.role).toLowerCase() === "physician") {
    Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: " + authorEmail + " is a physician — no cosign needed");
    return;
  }

  // Find patient folder in Drive. Patient folder name format from the
  // preauth flow is "<patientName> - <patientId>" or just "<patientId>".
  var rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(NOTES_FOLDER_ID);
  var patientFolder = null;
  var folderIter = rootFolder.getFolders();
  while (folderIter.hasNext()) {
    var f = folderIter.next();
    if (f.getName().indexOf(patientId) !== -1) { patientFolder = f; break; }
  }
  if (!patientFolder) {
    Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: no Drive folder found for " + patientId);
    return;
  }

  // Find Preauth subfolder and scan for H&P / LOMN files
