  var preauthFolders = patientFolder.getFoldersByName("Preauth");
  if (!preauthFolders.hasNext()) {
    Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: no Preauth subfolder in " + patientFolder.getName());
    return;
  }
  var preauthFolder = preauthFolders.next();
  var docs = preauthFolder.getFiles();
  var siteId = (authorProfile.locations && authorProfile.locations[0]) || "";
  var created = 0;

  while (docs.hasNext()) {
    var doc = docs.next();
    var name = doc.getName();
    var docType = null;
    if (name.indexOf("H&P") !== -1 || name.indexOf("HnP") !== -1 || name.toLowerCase().indexOf("h&p") !== -1) {
      docType = "Preauth H&P";
    } else if (name.toLowerCase().indexOf("lomn") !== -1) {
      docType = "LOMN";
    }
    if (!docType) continue;

    createCosignEntry({
      docType: docType,
      docName: name,
      docUrl: doc.getUrl(),
      docId: doc.getId(),
      patientId: patientId,
      authorEmail: authorEmail,
      authorRole: authorProfile.role,
      siteId: siteId
    });
    created++;
    Logger.log("Backfilled cosign: " + docType + " for " + patientId);
  }

  Logger.log("backfillCosignForPatient: done. Created " + created + " cosign row(s) for " + patientId);
}

// ============================================================
// CLINIC THROUGHPUT TRACKING
// ============================================================

// ============================================================
// STATUS BACKFILL — recompute throughput statuses from actual data
// ============================================================
// Run from Apps Script editor: backfillThroughputStatuses()
// Run with dryRun=false to commit; dryRun=true (default) just logs what
// it WOULD change so you can review first.
//
// What it does: walks every Throughput row and recomputes what its status
// SHOULD be based on facts in adjacent data — Cosign sheet, Drafts sheet,
// presence of D0/D20/D90 timestamps. Fixes legacy rows that say
// "Intake Complete" with no actual content, or got stuck mid-pipeline,
// or never got the cosign-status update because they predate the wiring.
//
// Decision tree (top-down, first match wins — never regresses):
//   1. Has D90 Complete timestamp           → leave (downstream truth)
//   2. Has D20 Complete timestamp           → at least "D20 Complete"
//   3. Has D0 Complete timestamp            → at least "D0 Complete"
//   4. Has Scheduled D0 timestamp           → at least "D0 Scheduled"
//   5. Status already says Authorized       → leave (Floyd email truth)
//   6. Status already says Preauth Rejected → leave (Floyd email truth)
//   7. Status already says Preauth Submitted/In Review → leave
//   8. Cosign rows exist for this DNC ID:
//        all signed → "Cosigned"
//        any pending → "Awaiting Cosign"
//   9. Drafts row exists with substantive content → "Intake In Progress"
//  10. Default → "Referral Received"
//
// Does NOT touch: rows where actual data shows progress beyond what the
// status currently says. We can ALWAYS advance toward truth, never reverse.
function backfillThroughputStatuses(dryRun) {
  if (dryRun === undefined) dryRun = true; // safe default

  Logger.log("===== STATUS BACKFILL " + (dryRun ? "(DRY RUN)" : "(COMMIT)") + " =====");

  var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
  var lastRow = tpSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) {
    Logger.log("No throughput rows to process.");
    return { processed: 0, changed: 0, dryRun: dryRun };
  }
  // Read 23 cols (full width including comm pref). Defensive clamp.
  var widthAvail = Math.min(tpSheet.getLastColumn(), 23);
  var tpData = tpSheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, widthAvail).getValues();

  // Pre-load Cosign sheet, indexed by patient ID for O(1) lookup
  var cosignByPatient = {};
  try {
    var coSheet = initCosignSheet();
    if (coSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      var coRows = coSheet.getRange(2, 1, coSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 14).getValues();
      for (var ci = 0; ci < coRows.length; ci++) {
        var pid = String(coRows[ci][5] || ""); // col 6 = Patient ID
        if (!pid) continue;
        if (!cosignByPatient[pid]) cosignByPatient[pid] = [];
        cosignByPatient[pid].push({
          status: String(coRows[ci][10] || "").toLowerCase(), // col 11 = Status
          docType: String(coRows[ci][1] || "")
        });
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { Logger.log("Cosign sheet load failed: " + e); }

  // Pre-load Drafts sheet, indexed by patient ID — we'll check for substantive
  // content per patient. "Substantive" = at least one field that came from the
  // patient conversation, not just hydrated referral data.
  var draftsByPatient = {};
  try {
    var drSheet = getOrCreateSheet("Drafts");
    if (drSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      var drRows = drSheet.getRange(2, 1, drSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 3).getValues();
      for (var di = 0; di < drRows.length; di++) {
        var dpid = String(drRows[di][0] || "");
        if (!dpid) continue;
        var formJson = String(drRows[di][2] || "");
        if (!formJson) continue;
        try {
          var formData = JSON.parse(formJson);
          // Substantive content check (mirrors saveDraft logic)
          var substantive =
            (formData.painRest && String(formData.painRest).trim()) ||
            (formData.painBest && String(formData.painBest).trim()) ||
            (formData.painWorst && String(formData.painWorst).trim()) ||
            (formData.medications && String(formData.medications).trim()) ||
            (Array.isArray(formData.priorTx) && formData.priorTx.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(formData.painDescriptors) && formData.painDescriptors.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(formData.pmhx) && formData.pmhx.length > 0);
          draftsByPatient[dpid] = { substantive: !!substantive, hasDraft: true };
        } catch(e) {
          draftsByPatient[dpid] = { substantive: false, hasDraft: true };
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { Logger.log("Drafts sheet load failed: " + e); }

  var changes = [];
  var processed = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < tpData.length; i++) {
    var row = tpData[i];
    var rowNum = i + 2; // 1-indexed sheet row (header is row 1)
    var dncId = String(row[3] || ""); // col 4
    if (!dncId) continue;
    processed++;

    var currentStatus = String(row[15] || "").trim(); // col 16
    // First check: is this a known legacy alias? If so, translate immediately.
    // This handles "Intake Complete" → "Intake In Progress" without needing
    // content inspection. Aliases live in STATUS_TAXONOMY at top of file.
    var aliasResolved = translateLegacyStatus(currentStatus);
    if (aliasResolved !== currentStatus) {
      changes.push({
        row: rowNum,
        dncId: dncId,
        name: String(row[0] || ""),
        from: currentStatus + " (legacy alias)",
        to: aliasResolved
      });
      if (!dryRun) {
        tpSheet.getRange(rowNum, 16).setValue(aliasResolved);
        tpSheet.getRange(rowNum, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
      }
      currentStatus = aliasResolved; // continue processing with the new status
    }

    var scheduledD0 = row[7];        // col 8
    var d0Complete = row[8];         // col 9
    var scheduledD20 = row[11];      // col 12
    var d20Complete = row[12];       // col 13
    var scheduledD90 = row[13];      // col 14
    var d90Complete = row[14];       // col 15

    // Compute the status this row SHOULD have
    var newStatus = null;

    // Downstream truth: visit timestamps win over anything earlier
    if (d90Complete) {
      // Leave whatever it currently says past D90 — too varied to backfill
      continue;
    } else if (d20Complete) {
      if (currentStatus !== "D20 Complete" &&
          currentStatus.indexOf("Trial") !== 0 &&
          currentStatus !== "Reimbursed" &&
          currentStatus.indexOf("D90") !== 0) {
        newStatus = "D20 Complete";
      }
    } else if (d0Complete) {
      if (currentStatus !== "D0 Complete" &&
          currentStatus.indexOf("D20") !== 0 &&
          !d20Complete && !scheduledD20) {
        newStatus = "D0 Complete";
      }
    } else if (scheduledD0) {
      if (currentStatus !== "D0 Scheduled" &&
          currentStatus.indexOf("D0") !== 0 &&
          currentStatus !== "Authorized") {
        newStatus = "D0 Scheduled";
      }
    }

    // Don't touch rows that are at billing/payer-truth states
    var isAtBillerTruth = currentStatus === "Authorized" ||
                          currentStatus === "Preauth Submitted" ||
                          currentStatus === "Preauth In Review" ||
                          currentStatus.indexOf("Preauth Rejected") === 0 ||
                          currentStatus.indexOf("Trial") === 0 ||
                          currentStatus === "Reimbursed";
    if (!newStatus && isAtBillerTruth) continue;

    // Cosign-aware: rebuild status based on cosign rows
    if (!newStatus) {
      var cosigns = cosignByPatient[dncId];
      if (cosigns && cosigns.length > 0) {
        var anyPending = false;
        for (var k = 0; k < cosigns.length; k++) {
          if (cosigns[k].status === "pending") { anyPending = true; break; }
        }
        // Only assign if it would actually change the row
        var cosignTarget = anyPending ? "Awaiting Cosign" : "Cosigned";
        if (currentStatus !== cosignTarget) {
          newStatus = cosignTarget;
        }
      }
    }

    // Intake In Progress vs Referral Received
    // Specifically targets the legacy "Intake Complete" with empty drafts
    // bug — those rows get downgraded if no real content exists.
    if (!newStatus) {
      var draft = draftsByPatient[dncId];
      if (draft && draft.substantive) {
        if (currentStatus !== "Intake In Progress" &&
            currentStatus !== "Awaiting Cosign" &&
            currentStatus !== "Cosigned") {
          newStatus = "Intake In Progress";
        }
      } else {
        // No substantive draft — should be Referral Received
        // BUT: if currentStatus is already "Referral Received" or blank,
        // skip. If it's the legacy "Intake Complete" with no real content,
        // downgrade it. This is the rule that fixes the user's complaint.
        if (currentStatus === "Intake Complete" || currentStatus === "Intake In Progress") {
          newStatus = "Referral Received";
        }
      }
    }

    if (newStatus && newStatus !== currentStatus) {
      changes.push({
        row: rowNum,
        dncId: dncId,
        name: String(row[0] || ""),
        from: currentStatus || "(blank)",
        to: newStatus
      });
      if (!dryRun) {
        tpSheet.getRange(rowNum, 16).setValue(newStatus);
        tpSheet.getRange(rowNum, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
      }
    }
  }

  Logger.log("Processed " + processed + " rows, " + changes.length + " status corrections" +
             (dryRun ? " (NOT WRITTEN)" : " (WRITTEN)"));
  for (var ch = 0; ch < changes.length; ch++) {
    var c = changes[ch];
    Logger.log("  Row " + c.row + " " + c.dncId + " (" + c.name + "): \"" + c.from + "\" → \"" + c.to + "\"");
  }

  return {
    processed: processed,
    changed: changes.length,
    changes: changes,
    dryRun: dryRun
  };
}


function initThroughputSheet() {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Clinic Throughput");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
    var headers = [
      "Patient Name", "DOB", "Phone", "DNC ID",
      "Referral Date", "Called", "Intake Done",
      "Scheduled D0", "D0 Complete",
      "Medicare Submitted", "Reimbursement Collected",
      "Scheduled D20", "D20 Complete",
      "Scheduled D90", "D90 Complete",
      "Status", "Last Updated", "Notes", "Site ID",
      "Call Attempts", "Last Call At", "Call Log",
      "Comm Preference"
    ];
    sheet.appendRow(headers);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight("bold");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  } else {
    // Backfill header row for sheets created before new columns existed.
    // Only appends missing column headers; existing data rows get blanks
    // naturally on read. Safe to run repeatedly.
    //   Cols 20-22 = call tracking (added previous session)
    //   Col 23    = comm preference (added this session)
    var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
    if (lastCol < 23) {
      var addHeaders = [];
      if (lastCol < 20) addHeaders.push("Call Attempts");
      if (lastCol < 21) addHeaders.push("Last Call At");
      if (lastCol < 22) addHeaders.push("Call Log");
      if (lastCol < 23) addHeaders.push("Comm Preference");
      sheet.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, addHeaders.length)
        .setValues([addHeaders])
        .setFontWeight("bold");
    }
  }
  return sheet;
}

function findThroughputRow(sheet, patientId) {
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return -1;
  var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 4, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
    if (ids[i][0] === patientId) return i + 2;
  }
  return -1;
}

// Ensure a throughput row exists for this patient. If missing, create it.
// Returns the row number (always > 0).
function ensureThroughputRow(sheet, patientId, patientName) {
  var existing = findThroughputRow(sheet, patientId);
  if (existing > 0) return existing;
  // Create new row with patient name (if known) and DNC ID
  sheet.appendRow([
    patientName || "",  // 1 Name
    "",                 // 2 DOB
    "",                 // 3 Phone
    patientId,          // 4 DNC ID
    new Date().toISOString(), // 5 Referral Date (best-guess)
    "", "", "", "",     // 6-9 Called, Intake Done, Sched D0, D0 Complete
    "", "",             // 10-11 Medicare Submitted, Reimbursement
    "", "", "", "",     // 12-15 Sched D20, D20 Complete, Sched D90, D90 Complete
    "Referral Received", // 16 Status — canonical taxonomy. Was previously "New - Direct Entry"
                          // for auto-created rows but that flagged non-canonical. Auto-created
                          // rows are still functionally referrals at the Referral Received stage,
                          // they just lacked a real referral document.
    new Date().toISOString(), // 17 Last Updated
    "",                 // 18 Notes
    "",                 // 19 Site ID
    0,                  // 20 Call Attempts
    "",                 // 21 Last Call At
    "",                 // 22 Call Log
    ""                  // 23 Comm Preference
  ]);
  return sheet.getLastRow();
}

function updateThroughputField(patientId, colIndex, value) {
  var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
  var row = findThroughputRow(sheet, patientId);
  if (row > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(row, colIndex).setValue(value);
    sheet.getRange(row, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); // Last Updated
  }
}

// Column index map for Throughput:
// 1=Name, 2=DOB, 3=Phone, 4=DNC ID,
// 5=Referral Date, 6=Called, 7=Intake Done,
// 8=Scheduled D0, 9=D0 Complete,
// 10=Medicare Submitted, 11=Reimbursement Collected,
// 12=Scheduled D20, 13=D20 Complete,
// 14=Scheduled D90, 15=D90 Complete,
// 16=Status, 17=Last Updated, 18=Notes

// ============================================================
// DAILY NOTIFICATION TRIGGER
// ============================================================
// To set up: In Apps Script, go to Triggers (clock icon on left)
// Click "+ Add Trigger" > checkStalePatients > Time-driven >
// Day timer > 8am to 9am > Save
// ============================================================

function checkStalePatients() {
  var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;

  var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 18).getValues();
  var now = new Date();
  var maAlerts = [];
  var clinicianAlerts = [];
  var billerAlerts = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    var status = String(data[i][15] || "");
    if (status === "Complete") continue; // fully cycled, no more alerts needed

    var name = data[i][0];
    var id = data[i][3];
    var referralDate = data[i][4] ? new Date(data[i][4]) : null;
    var called = data[i][5];
    var intakeDone = data[i][6];
    var scheduledD0 = data[i][7];
    var d0Complete = data[i][8];
    var medicareSubmitted = data[i][9];
    var scheduledD20 = data[i][11];
    var d20Complete = data[i][12];
    var scheduledD90 = data[i][13];
    var d90Complete = data[i][14];

    var daysSinceReferral = referralDate ? Math.floor((now - referralDate) / 86400000) : 0;

    // MA alerts
    if (referralDate && !called && daysSinceReferral >= 1) {
      maAlerts.push(id + ": Referral " + daysSinceReferral + " days ago, not yet called");
    }
    if (called && intakeDone && !scheduledD0) {
      maAlerts.push(id + ": Intake complete, not yet scheduled for D0");
    }
    if (d0Complete && !scheduledD20) {
      // Check if D0 was recent enough that D20 should be scheduled
      var d0Date = new Date(d0Complete);
      var daysSinceD0 = Math.floor((now - d0Date) / 86400000);
      if (daysSinceD0 >= 1) {
        maAlerts.push(id + ": D0 complete, D20 not scheduled (day " + daysSinceD0 + " of 20)");
      }
    }
    if (d20Complete && !scheduledD90) {
      maAlerts.push(id + ": D20 complete, D90 follow-up not scheduled");
    }

    // Clinician alerts
    if (intakeDone && scheduledD0 && !d0Complete) {
      var apptDate = new Date(scheduledD0);
      var daysUntil = Math.floor((apptDate - now) / 86400000);
      if (daysUntil <= 1 && daysUntil >= 0) {
        clinicianAlerts.push(id + ": D0 appointment tomorrow or today, review draft");
      }
    }

    // Biller alerts — only for patients that genuinely need attention.
    // Skip if status indicates the biller has already acted (Submitted, Reimbursed, Complete, Removed).
    var statusLower = String(status).toLowerCase();
    var billerDone = medicareSubmitted || statusLower.indexOf("submitted") >= 0 ||
      statusLower.indexOf("reimbursed") >= 0 || statusLower === "complete" ||
      statusLower.indexOf("removed") === 0 || statusLower.indexOf("denied") >= 0 ||
      statusLower.indexOf("closed") >= 0;
    if (d0Complete && !billerDone) {
      var d0CompDate = new Date(d0Complete);
      var daysSinceD0Comp = Math.floor((now - d0CompDate) / 86400000);
      if (daysSinceD0Comp >= 3) {
        billerAlerts.push(id + ": D0 note submitted " + daysSinceD0Comp + " days ago, Medicare not yet submitted");
      }
    }
  }

  // Send emails (each in its own try so one failure doesn't lose the others)
  if (maAlerts.length > 0 && MA_EMAIL) {
    try {
      MailApp.sendEmail({
        to: MA_EMAIL,
        subject: "DNC Action Items - " + maAlerts.length + " patients need attention",
        body: "Good morning,\n\nThe following patients need action:\n\n" + maAlerts.join("\n") + "\n\nPlease address today.\n\nDiabetic Nerve Clinic"
      });
    } catch(e) { Logger.log("MA digest mail failed: " + e.toString()); }
  }
  if (clinicianAlerts.length > 0 && CLINICIAN_EMAIL) {
    sendClinicianEmail({
      subject: "DNC - Upcoming appointments, drafts to review",
      body: "The following patients have appointments coming up:\n\n" + clinicianAlerts.join("\n") + "\n\nReview drafts before the visit."
    });
  }
  if (billerAlerts.length > 0 && BILLER_EMAIL) {
    try {
      MailApp.sendEmail({
        to: BILLER_EMAIL,
        subject: "DNC - Medicare submissions pending",
        body: "The following patients need Medicare submission:\n\n" + billerAlerts.join("\n") + "\n\nPlease complete."
      });
    } catch(e) { Logger.log("Biller digest mail failed: " + e.toString()); }
  }
}

// ============================================================
// BILLER EMAIL PARSER
// ============================================================
// Time-driven trigger (every 5-10 min) polls the evan@leitneranesthesia.com inbox
// for emails from the biller matching the subject template:
//   DNC-XXXX: preauth submitted
//   DNC-XXXX: authorized
//   DNC-XXXX: implant billing submitted
//   DNC-XXXX: reimbursed
//   DNC-XXXX: denied [reason]
// When biller parses "preauth approved", email the people who need to act on
// that: Lindsey (always, program owner), the affiliated clinicians at the
// patient's site (from Users sheet), and Bradley (MA, will actually make the
// call). The bridge between billing-side events and clinical-side action —
// without this the approval sits silently in a spreadsheet cell and nobody
// knows to contact the patient. This is the pre-GCP version; once real
// workflow engine is live, this becomes a task queue entry instead of an email.
function sendPreauthApprovedNotification(rowIdx, dncId) {
  try {
    var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
    var widthAvailable = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 23);
    var row = sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 1, 1, widthAvailable).getValues()[0];
    var patientName = row[0] || "(unknown name)";
    var phone = row[2] || "(no phone on file)";
    var siteId = row[18] || "";
    var commPref = row[22] || "";  // col 23 (0-indexed 22) — Comm Preference

    // Build recipient list: Lindsey + site clinicians + Bradley, deduped
    var recipients = {};
    if (CLINICIAN_EMAIL) recipients[CLINICIAN_EMAIL.toLowerCase()] = true;
    if (MA_EMAIL) recipients[MA_EMAIL.toLowerCase()] = true;
    var siteClinicians = getClinicianEmailsAtSite(siteId);
    for (var i = 0; i < siteClinicians.length; i++) {
      if (siteClinicians[i]) recipients[siteClinicians[i].toLowerCase()] = true;
    }
    var toList = Object.keys(recipients).join(",");
    if (!toList) {
      Logger.log("sendPreauthApprovedNotification: no recipients for " + dncId);
      return;
    }

    var site = siteId ? getSiteById(siteId) : null;
    var siteLabel = site ? (site.name + " (" + siteId + ")") : (siteId || "unassigned");

    var prefLine = commPref
      ? "Patient-stated contact preference: " + commPref
      : "Patient-stated contact preference: NOT CAPTURED (ask at next contact)";

    var body =
      "Preauth approved for " + patientName + " (" + dncId + ").\n\n" +
      "Site: " + siteLabel + "\n" +
      "Phone: " + phone + "\n" +
      prefLine + "\n\n" +
      "Next step: contact patient to schedule D0 (device placement) visit.\n\n" +
      "— Automated notification from DNC platform (biller email parser)";

    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: toList,
      subject: "Schedule D0: " + patientName + " (" + dncId + ")",
      body: body
    });
    Logger.log("Preauth-approved notification sent for " + dncId + " to " + toList);

    // Log to Notification Log so the referral card surfaces "staff notified"
    // at a glance. Status is "completed" because the email went out — this
    // is terminal for the email action itself. A follow-on scheduling attempt
    // (AI call, SMS, manual call) will create its own event when initiated.
    logNotificationEvent(
      dncId,
      "preauth-schedule",
      "email",
      "completed",
      "Staff notified to schedule D0: " + toList,
      { recipients: toList.split(","), commPref: commPref, siteId: siteId },
      "system:biller-parser"
    );
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log("sendPreauthApprovedNotification failed for " + dncId + ": " + e);
  }
}

// Polls evan@leitneranesthesia.com inbox for Floyd's biller status emails and
// updates the corresponding throughput row's status. Subject template confirmed
// with Floyd 4/20/2026:
//   DNC-XXXX: preauth in review
//   DNC-XXXX: preauth submitted
//   DNC-XXXX: preauth approved
//   DNC-XXXX: preauth rejected
//   DNC-XXXX: trial billing submitted
//   DNC-XXXX: trial billing approved
//   DNC-XXXX: trial billing rejected
//   DNC-XXXX: trial reimbursed
//
// Run: Apps Script editor → Triggers → Add → processBillerEmails → Time-driven → Every 5 min
// Pure function: extracts DNC ID + matched status rule from a biller email's
// subject + body. Returns { dncId, statusRaw, matched, reason } where:
//   - dncId is null if no DNC-XXXX in subject
//   - matched is null if no rule matched
//   - statusRaw is the keyword substring that triggered the match
//   - reason describes why if either dncId or matched is null
//
// Pure means: no Gmail / sheet / logger calls. Just string in, struct out.
// This makes the parser testable. processBillerEmails calls this then
// handles IO based on the returned values.
function parseBillerEmail(subject, body) {
  if (!subject) return { dncId: null, matched: null, statusRaw: "", reason: "empty subject" };
  var rules = STATUS_TAXONOMY.rules;

  // 1. DNC ID extraction — match anywhere in subject (handles "Re:" prefixes)
  var dncIdMatch = /\b(DNC-[A-Z0-9]+)\b/i.exec(subject);
  if (!dncIdMatch) {
    return { dncId: null, matched: null, statusRaw: "", reason: "no DNC-XXXX in subject" };
  }
  var dncId = dncIdMatch[1].toUpperCase();

  // 2. Pattern A — Floyd-initiated email "DNC-XXXX: <status>"
  var statusRaw = "";
  var patternAMatch = /DNC-[A-Z0-9]+\s*:\s*(.+)$/i.exec(subject);
  if (patternAMatch && patternAMatch[1]) {
    statusRaw = patternAMatch[1].trim().toLowerCase();
    // Edge: subject contains system-generated phrase, not Floyd's status.
    // Discard and fall through to Pattern B (body parse).
    var systemPhrases = ["packet ready", "needs attention", "schedule d0",
                         "schedule d20", "schedule d90", "submissions pending",
                         "cosign needed", "upcoming appointments",
                         "your doc has been cosigned"];
    for (var sp = 0; sp < systemPhrases.length; sp++) {
      if (statusRaw.indexOf(systemPhrases[sp]) !== -1) {
        statusRaw = "";
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Pattern B — body fallback for replies. Scan first 500 chars for
  //    any status keyword from STATUS_TAXONOMY.rules.
  if (!statusRaw && body) {
    var bodyHead = String(body).substring(0, 500).toLowerCase();
    for (var r2 = 0; r2 < rules.length; r2++) {
      if (bodyHead.indexOf(rules[r2].match) !== -1) {
        statusRaw = rules[r2].match;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!statusRaw) {
    return { dncId: dncId, matched: null, statusRaw: "", reason: "no status keyword in subject or body" };
  }

  // 4. Find matching rule (longest-prefix-first via list order)
  for (var r = 0; r < rules.length; r++) {
    if (statusRaw.indexOf(rules[r].match) === 0) {
      return { dncId: dncId, matched: rules[r], statusRaw: statusRaw, reason: "ok" };
    }
  }

  return { dncId: dncId, matched: null, statusRaw: statusRaw, reason: "status keyword '" + statusRaw + "' has no rule" };
}

// Test harness for parseBillerEmail. Run from Apps Script editor:
//   testBillerParser()
// Logs PASS/FAIL per case, summary at end. Add new cases as you discover
// edge cases in production. Do this BEFORE modifying parser logic.
function testBillerParser() {
  var cases = [
    // Pattern A — Floyd initiates
    { name: "Pattern A: clean approved",
      subject: "DNC-3YJF: preauth approved", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-3YJF", expectStatus: "Authorized" },
    { name: "Pattern A: clean rejected",
      subject: "DNC-VELZ: preauth rejected", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-VELZ", expectStatus: "Preauth Rejected" },
    { name: "Pattern A: 'denied' alias",
      subject: "DNC-ABCD: preauth denied", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-ABCD", expectStatus: "Preauth Rejected" },
    { name: "Pattern A: trial reimbursed",
      subject: "DNC-XYZW: trial reimbursed", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-XYZW", expectStatus: "Reimbursed" },
    // Order-dependence: longer-prefix must win
    { name: "Order: 'trial billing rejected' beats 'trial billing'",
      subject: "DNC-AAAA: trial billing rejected", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-AAAA", expectStatus: "Trial Billing Rejected" },
    { name: "Order: 'preauth approved' beats 'preauth'",
      subject: "DNC-BBBB: preauth approved", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-BBBB", expectStatus: "Authorized" },
    // Pattern B — Floyd replies to system email
    { name: "Pattern B: reply to 'Preauth packet ready'",
      subject: "Re: Preauth packet ready - DNC-CCCC",
      body: "preauth approved\n\nNotes: clean run.",
      expectDnc: "DNC-CCCC", expectStatus: "Authorized" },
    { name: "Pattern B: reply to 'Medicare packet ready'",
      subject: "Re: DNC: Medicare packet ready - DNC-DDDD",
      body: "trial reimbursed $1247.50",
      expectDnc: "DNC-DDDD", expectStatus: "Reimbursed" },
    { name: "Pattern B: reply with status mid-body",
      subject: "Re: Preauth packet ready - DNC-EEEE",
      body: "Hi Evan,\n\nQuick update — preauth submitted today. They said 7-10 days.",
      expectDnc: "DNC-EEEE", expectStatus: "Preauth Submitted" },
    // Edge cases
    { name: "Edge: no DNC ID in subject",
      subject: "Re: question about Velez", body: "preauth approved",
      expectDnc: null, expectStatus: null },
    { name: "Edge: DNC ID but no status anywhere",
      subject: "Re: DNC-FFFF status check", body: "Calling you tomorrow about this one.",
      expectDnc: "DNC-FFFF", expectStatus: null },
    { name: "Edge: lowercase DNC in subject",
      subject: "dnc-gggg: preauth approved", body: "",
      expectDnc: "DNC-GGGG", expectStatus: "Authorized" }
  ];

  var passed = 0, failed = 0;
  Logger.log("===== testBillerParser =====");
  for (var i = 0; i < cases.length; i++) {
    var c = cases[i];
    var result = parseBillerEmail(c.subject, c.body);
    var actualDnc = result.dncId;
    var actualStatus = result.matched ? result.matched.status : null;
    var dncOk = actualDnc === c.expectDnc;
    var statusOk = actualStatus === c.expectStatus;
    if (dncOk && statusOk) {
      passed++;
      Logger.log("  ✓ PASS: " + c.name);
    } else {
      failed++;
      Logger.log("  ✗ FAIL: " + c.name);
      Logger.log("    expected: dncId=" + c.expectDnc + " status=" + c.expectStatus);
      Logger.log("    actual:   dncId=" + actualDnc + " status=" + actualStatus + " (reason: " + result.reason + ")");
    }
  }
  Logger.log("===== " + passed + " passed, " + failed + " failed =====");
  return { passed: passed, failed: failed, total: cases.length };
}

function processBillerEmails() {
  var BILLER_FROM = "floyd@thesproutmax.com";
  var sheet = initThroughputSheet();

  // Search for unread emails from Floyd. Three patterns to catch:
  //  1. "DNC-XXXX: ..."           - Floyd initiates (clean)
  //  2. "Re: Preauth packet ..."  - Floyd replies to system email (has DNC-XXXX in subject)
  //  3. "Re: DNC: Medicare ..."   - same, different system subject pattern
  // Search all unread emails from Floyd; the parser will discard anything
  // without a DNC-XXXX ID in the subject. This is wider than the old
  // "subject:DNC-" filter, but Floyd's volume is low enough this is fine.
  var threads = GmailApp.search("from:" + BILLER_FROM + " is:unread", 0, 30);
  if (!threads.length) return;

  // Status keyword → throughput updates. Listed LONGEST-FIRST so that
  // "preauth approved" matches before "preauth", "trial billing rejected"
  // matches before "trial billing" or "trial". Order matters — do not alphabetize.
  //   col10 = Medicare Submitted timestamp (set when packet first goes out)
  //   col11 = Reimbursement Collected (amount string)
  //   col16 = Status (display string)
  // Notes:
  //   - "preauth in review" and "preauth submitted" both stamp col10 — either
  //     means the packet has been sent to Medicare.
  //   - "preauth approved" → status "Authorized" matches existing downstream
  //     status filters elsewhere in the codebase.
  //   - "trial reimbursed" triggers amount extraction from the email body.
  // Status keyword → throughput updates. Listed LONGEST-FIRST so that
  // "preauth approved" matches before "preauth", "trial billing rejected"
  // matches before "trial billing" or "trial". Order matters — do not alphabetize.
  //   col10 = Medicare Submitted timestamp (set when packet first goes out)
  //   col11 = Reimbursement Collected (amount string)
  //   col16 = Status (display string)
  // Notes:
  //   - "preauth in review" and "preauth submitted" both stamp col10 — either
  //     means the packet has been sent to Medicare.
  //   - "preauth approved" → status "Authorized" matches existing downstream
  //     status filters elsewhere in the codebase.
  //   - "trial reimbursed" triggers amount extraction from the email body.
  //   - Both "rejected" and "denied" accepted — Floyd uses "Denied" in practice
  //     (as of 4/22/2026 confirmed emails) though the original spec said
  //     "rejected". Either word maps to the same status.
  // Status keyword rules now sourced from the central STATUS_TAXONOMY at the
  // top of this file. Edit there to change parser behavior.
  var STATUS_RULES = STATUS_TAXONOMY.rules;

  // Track unmatched emails — forward to clinician for manual review so drift
  // in Floyd's wording doesn't silently lose status updates.
  var unmatchedSubjects = [];

  for (var t = 0; t < threads.length; t++) {
    var msgs = threads[t].getMessages();
    for (var m = 0; m < msgs.length; m++) {
      if (!msgs[m].isUnread()) continue;
      var subject = msgs[m].getSubject() || "";
      var body = msgs[m].getPlainBody() || "";

      // Delegate to pure parser. Returns { dncId, statusRaw, matched, reason }.
      // See parseBillerEmail() above for the full extraction logic + edge cases.
      // Test cases live in testBillerParser() — run that BEFORE modifying parser.
      var parseResult = parseBillerEmail(subject, body);
      if (!parseResult.dncId) {
        Logger.log("Biller email skipped: " + parseResult.reason + " — subject: " + subject);
        unmatchedSubjects.push(subject);
        msgs[m].markRead();
        continue;
      }
      var dncId = parseResult.dncId;
      if (!parseResult.matched) {
        Logger.log("Biller email for " + dncId + " unmatched: " + parseResult.reason);
        unmatchedSubjects.push(subject + " (" + dncId + ", " + parseResult.reason + ")");
        msgs[m].markRead();
        continue;
      }
      var statusRaw = parseResult.statusRaw;
      var matched = parseResult.matched;

      // Find the throughput row for this DNC ID
      var rowIdx = findThroughputRow(sheet, dncId);
      if (rowIdx < 0) {
        Logger.log("Biller email: no throughput row for " + dncId);
        unmatchedSubjects.push(subject + " (no throughput row)");
        msgs[m].markRead();
        continue;
      }

      // For rejections, preserve any reason text after the keyword:
      // "preauth rejected — missing SCS trial doc" → status = "Preauth Rejected: missing SCS trial doc"
      var statusValue = matched.status;
      if (matched.isRejection) {
        var afterKeyword = statusRaw.substring(matched.match.length).trim();
        afterKeyword = afterKeyword.replace(/^[\s:\-—–]+/, "").trim();
        if (afterKeyword) statusValue += ": " + afterKeyword;
      }

       // Write status (col 16) and last-updated (col 17). Skip status write
      // for rules where status is null (e.g. p2p_request) — those are queue-
      // only actions that don't represent a throughput phase change.
      if (matched.status !== null && matched.status !== undefined) {
        sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 16).setValue(statusValue);
      }
      sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());

      // Stamp Medicare Submitted (col 10) on first preauth event
      if (matched.stampSubmitted) {
        var existingSubmit = sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 10).getValue();
        if (!existingSubmit) sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 10).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
      }

      // Extract reimbursement amount from body for trial reimbursed
      if (matched.extractAmount) {
        var amtMatch = /\$[\d,]+(?:\.\d{2})?/.exec(body);
        if (amtMatch) sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 11).setValue(amtMatch[0]);
      }

      // Notify staff on preauth approved — bridges billing → clinical scheduling
      if (matched.notifyPreauthApproved) {
        sendPreauthApprovedNotification(rowIdx, dncId);
      }

      // For rejections (preauth or trial billing): log a notification event so
      // the referral card shows the status with a red indicator. Don't send a
      // duplicate email — Floyd's rejection email already CCs Lindsey, Bradley,
      // and the clinical team directly, so they see the peer-to-peer callback
      // details in the original email body. Adding a Claude-generated email on
      // top would just be noise.
      if (matched.isRejection) {
        try {
          logNotificationEvent(
            dncId,
            matched.notifyPreauthRejected ? "preauth-rejection" : "billing-rejection",
            "email",
            "escalated",
            matched.status + " — see Floyd's email for peer-to-peer / reason",
            { billerSubject: subj, rejectionType: matched.status },
            "system:biller-parser"
          );
        } catch(e) {
          Logger.log("Rejection notification log failed for " + dncId + ": " + e);
        }
      }

      auditLog({ action: "billerEmailParsed", user: BILLER_FROM, patientId: dncId, outcome: "ok", detail: matched.match });
      Logger.log("Biller email processed: " + dncId + " → " + matched.status);

      // ---- QUEUE ACTION DISPATCH ----
      // If this rule has a queueAction (e.g. p2p_request, appeal_required),
      // upsert a row into the Payer Action Queue. Email is one source among
      // potentially several (the Humana fax PDF, a Carillon voicemail);
      // dedup logic in upsertPayerActionRow merges them on (dnc, payer, type).
      if (matched.queueAction) {
        try {
          var floydParsed = parseFloydActionEmailBody(body);
          var queueResult = upsertPayerActionRow({
            dncId: dncId,
            actionType: matched.queueAction,
            payer: floydParsed.payer || "",
            patientName: floydParsed.patientName || "",
            memberId: floydParsed.memberId || "",
            cpt: floydParsed.cpt || "",
            dx: floydParsed.dx || "",
            sourceChannel: "email",
            sourceId: msgs[m].getId ? msgs[m].getId() : ("floyd-" + Date.now()),
            receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            parsedFields: floydParsed
          });
          Logger.log("Queue dispatch for " + dncId + " (" + matched.queueAction + "): " + JSON.stringify(queueResult));
          // Log a notification event so the referral card can surface
          // "📋 P2P queued" or "📋 Appeal required" at a glance.
          try {
            logNotificationEvent(
              dncId,
              matched.queueAction === "p2p_request" ? "p2p-request" : "appeal-required",
              "email",
              "completed",
              "Queued from Floyd email: " + (queueResult.created ? "new" : "merged"),
              { actionId: queueResult.actionId, queueAction: matched.queueAction },
              "system:biller-parser"
            );
          } catch(logErr) {
            Logger.log("Queue notification log failed: " + logErr);
          }
        } catch(qErr) {
          Logger.log("Queue dispatch FAILED for " + dncId + ": " + qErr);
        }
      }

// ---- FLOYD PARSE SUMMARY WEBHOOK ----
      // Per-parse visibility: every Floyd email that goes through the parser
      // generates a summary email to admin. Confidence levels reflect HOW
      // the parser arrived at the match, not whether it was right:
      //   high   = Pattern A (subject "DNC-XXXX: <status>", clean keyword match)
      //   medium = Pattern B (status keyword found in body fallback)
      //   low    = matched but extraction was unusual (rejection with no reason text,
      //            keyword-followed-by-additional-text that may indicate drift)
      // Volume is intentionally not throttled — Floyd sends ~5-10/day, real-time
      // drift detection beats inbox tidiness. If volume becomes unmanageable
      // later, add a confidence threshold here.
      try {
        var parseConfidence;
        var parseConfidenceReason;
        // Determine which pattern matched. Pattern A leaves "DNC-XXXX: keyword"
        // in the subject; the parser sets statusRaw from that. Pattern B falls
        // back to body, so subject won't have the colon-prefixed status.
        var subjectHadStatus = /DNC-[A-Z0-9]+\s*:\s*\S+/i.test(subject);
        if (subjectHadStatus) {
          // Pattern A. Now check whether the keyword extraction was clean.
          var statusAfterKeyword = statusRaw.substring(matched.match.length).trim().replace(/^[\s:\-—–]+/, "").trim();
          if (matched.isRejection && !statusAfterKeyword) {
            // Rejection without reason text — Floyd usually includes one.
            // Either he forgot, or the parser cut it off, or the format
            // drifted. Worth flagging.
            parseConfidence = "low";
            parseConfidenceReason = "rejection matched but no reason text after keyword — verify Floyd's email";
          } else if (statusAfterKeyword && !matched.isRejection) {
            // Non-rejection match but trailing text exists. Could be benign
            // ("preauth approved — clean run, ID 12345") or a sign of drift.
            // Surface it for review.
            parseConfidence = "medium";
            parseConfidenceReason = "match clean but trailing text in subject: '" + statusAfterKeyword.substring(0, 80) + "'";
          } else {
            parseConfidence = "high";
            parseConfidenceReason = "Pattern A clean match";
          }
        } else {
          // Pattern B — body fallback. Worked but the path is fuzzier.
          parseConfidence = "medium";
          parseConfidenceReason = "Pattern B (status keyword found in body, not subject)";
        }

        // Pull patient name + site from the row we just updated for the email body
        var summaryRowVals = sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 1, 1, 23).getValues()[0];
        var summaryName = String(summaryRowVals[0] || "");
        var summarySite = String(summaryRowVals[18] || "");

        // Body excerpt — first 400 chars, enough to spot drift without flooding the inbox
        var bodyExcerpt = body ? String(body).substring(0, 400).replace(/\n+/g, " ").trim() : "(empty body)";

        var summaryBody = [
          "Floyd email parsed.",
          "",
          "Patient: " + summaryName + " (" + dncId + ")",
          "Site: " + (summarySite || "—"),
          "",
          "Original subject: " + subject,
          "Body excerpt: " + bodyExcerpt + (body && body.length > 400 ? " ..." : ""),
          "",
          "Parser results:",
         "  Status set to: " + (statusValue || "(no status change — queue action only)"),
          "  Matched keyword: " + matched.match,
          "  Confidence: " + parseConfidence,
          "  Reason: " + parseConfidenceReason,
          (matched.stampSubmitted ? "  Stamped 'Medicare Submitted' timestamp on first preauth event." : ""),
          (matched.notifyPreauthApproved ? "  → Preauth-approved notification sent to clinical team for D0 scheduling." : ""),
          (matched.isRejection ? "  → Rejection logged to notification log; Floyd's email already CCs the team." : ""),
          (matched.extractAmount ? "  Extracted reimbursement amount from body." : ""),
          "",
          "If anything here looks wrong, this is the email to investigate before status drift compounds."
        ];

        sendClinicianEmail({
          to: EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL,
          subject: "[DNC parse] " + dncId + " → " + statusValue + " (" + parseConfidence + ")",
          body: summaryBody.filter(function(s){ return s !== ""; }).join("\n"),
          skipEvanCc: true
        });
      } catch(summaryErr) {
        Logger.log("Floyd parse summary webhook failed: " + summaryErr);
      }
      // Outcome vs. expected classification — asymmetric handling.
      //
      // Default state: payer is in Payer Reference table with a category.
      // Each Floyd email is either EXPECTED (matches prior precedent) or
      // UNEXPECTED. Unexpected splits two ways with very different meanings:
      //
      //   FAVORABLE unexpected: payer was preauth-required (or unknown) but
      //     we got Authorized. This is precedent-setting — payer may have
      //     loosened criteria, or they fundamentally cover this CPT and our
      //     prior categorization was overly cautious. Default action when
      //     resolved: promote payer category up.
      //
      //   UNFAVORABLE unexpected: payer was auto-approved but we got
      //     Rejected. The realistic explanations, in order of likelihood:
      //       1. Patient-specific (documentation gaps, contraindications) —
      //          most common. Floyd's email body explains why.
      //       2. Plan-specific drift (sub-plan with different rules).
      //       3. Genuine payer policy change — rare, usually formal notice.
      //     Default action when resolved: KEEP current classification. The
      //     UI defaults the primary CTA to "Keep current" and only offers
      //     "Downgrade" as a secondary action.
      //
      // We forward Floyd's full email body verbatim because his rejection
      // language varies too much for reliable extraction. Humans read the
      // body, machines just route the signal.
      try {
        var conflictRowIdx = findThroughputRow(sheet, dncId);
        if (conflictRowIdx > 0) {
          var conflictNotes = String(sheet.getRange(conflictRowIdx, 18).getValue() || "");
          var conflictInsMatch = /\|\s*Ins:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(conflictNotes);
          var conflictInsurance = conflictInsMatch ? conflictInsMatch[1].trim() : "";

          if (conflictInsurance) {
            var existingClassification = lookupPayerCategory(conflictInsurance);
            var existingCat = existingClassification.category;
            var conflictType = null; // "favorable" | "unfavorable" | null

            // Favorable surprise: authorized for a payer we'd flagged as
            // preauth-required or unknown
            if (matched.status === "Authorized" &&
                (existingCat === "preauth-required" || existingCat === "unknown-needs-verification")) {
              conflictType = "favorable";
            }
            // Unfavorable surprise: rejected for a payer we'd flagged as
            // auto-approved (or unknown). For unknown-needs-verification we
            // still flag — first signal is high value.
            else if (matched.isRejection &&
                     (existingCat === "auto-approved" || existingCat === "unknown-needs-verification")) {
              conflictType = "unfavorable";
            }

            if (conflictType === "favorable") {
              Logger.log("FAVORABLE PAYER PRECEDENT for " + dncId + ": '" +
                         conflictInsurance + "' was '" + existingCat + "', got Authorized");
              queuePayerUpdate(
                conflictInsurance,
                "promote-precedent",
                dncId,
                subject + " (was: " + existingCat + ", got Authorized — precedent set)"
              );
              if (CLINICIAN_EMAIL) {
                sendClinicianEmail({
                  subject: "DNC: Authorization precedent set — " + conflictInsurance,
                  body: "Good news: " + conflictInsurance + " authorized treatment for patient " + dncId + ".\n\n" +
                        "Our Payer Reference table previously classified this insurance as: " + existingCat + "\n\n" +
                        "This is the first authorization we've recorded from this payer. Going forward, future " +
                        "patients with this insurance can likely be treated as auto-approved — saving preauth " +
                        "delay and reducing rejection risk.\n\n" +
                        "Review pending in the gear menu (Pending Payer Updates) — primary action is " +
                        "'Promote to auto-approved'.\n\n" +
                        "Floyd's full message:\n" +
                        "Subject: " + (subject || "") + "\n\n" +
                        (body ? body.substring(0, 2000) : "(no body)") + "\n"
                });
              }
            } else if (conflictType === "unfavorable") {
              Logger.log("UNFAVORABLE PAYER OUTCOME for " + dncId + ": '" +
                         conflictInsurance + "' was '" + existingCat + "', got " + matched.status);
              queuePayerUpdate(
                conflictInsurance,
                "needs-reverification",
                dncId,
                subject + " (was: " + existingCat + ", got " + matched.status + ")"
              );
              if (CLINICIAN_EMAIL) {
                sendClinicianEmail({
                  subject: "DNC: Unexpected rejection from " + conflictInsurance + " — review needed",
                  body: "Patient " + dncId + " was rejected by " + conflictInsurance + ".\n\n" +
                        "Our Payer Reference table currently classifies this insurance as: " + existingCat + "\n\n" +
                        "Most likely explanations (in order):\n" +
                        "  1. Patient-specific rejection — documentation gaps, missing prior therapy, contraindication.\n" +
                        "     Probably the case here. Re-review the H&P/LOMN, fix gaps, resubmit.\n" +
                        "  2. Plan-specific drift — patient on sub-plan (HMO variant, employer-specific) with different rules.\n" +
                        "     Add a separate row in Payer Reference for the specific plan.\n" +
                        "  3. Genuine payer policy change — rare, usually accompanied by a formal letter from payer.\n" +
                        "     If Floyd's reason mentions LCD/LMRP, criteria change, or coverage drop, this may apply.\n\n" +
                        "Default action: keep current classification. Only downgrade if Floyd's explanation indicates " +
                        "(2) or (3) — the gear menu UI primary action is 'Keep current'.\n\n" +
                        "Floyd's full message — read this to understand which case applies:\n" +
                        "Subject: " + (subject || "") + "\n\n" +
                        (body ? body.substring(0, 2000) : "(no body)") + "\n"
                });
              }
            }
            // else: expected outcome, no extra signal needed.
            // (Could refresh "last verified date" here in the future.)
          }
        }
      } catch(conflictErr) {
        Logger.log("Payer outcome check failed for " + dncId + ": " + conflictErr);
      }

      // Body-level eligibility detection: Floyd's emails often contain
      // "INSURANCE NAME: <payer>" + "Authorization required" or
      // "Authorization not required" — which is exactly what we need to
      // accumulate into the Payer Reference table over time. We don't write
      // directly; we queue a pending update for human review (hybrid model).
      try {
        var insuranceMatch = body.match(/insurance\s*name\s*:\s*([^\n\r]+)/i);
        if (insuranceMatch && insuranceMatch[1]) {
          var detectedPayer = insuranceMatch[1].trim();
          // Strip trailing parenthetical IDs etc. — keep it short for matching
          detectedPayer = detectedPayer.replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$/, "").trim();
          if (detectedPayer && detectedPayer.length < 80) {
            var bodyLower = body.toLowerCase();
            var suggestedCategory = null;
            // Order matters: check "not required" before "required" — substring overlap
            if (bodyLower.indexOf("authorization not required") !== -1 ||
                bodyLower.indexOf("auth not required") !== -1 ||
                bodyLower.indexOf("no authorization required") !== -1) {
              suggestedCategory = "auto-approved";
            } else if (bodyLower.indexOf("authorization required") !== -1 ||
                       bodyLower.indexOf("auth required") !== -1 ||
                       bodyLower.indexOf("preauth required") !== -1) {
              suggestedCategory = "preauth-required";
            }

            if (suggestedCategory) {
              // Only queue if this payer isn't already in the main reference
              // table at the same category — prevents noise from re-detection.
              var existing = lookupPayerCategory(detectedPayer);
              if (existing.category !== suggestedCategory ||
                  existing.category === "unknown-needs-verification") {
                queuePayerUpdate(detectedPayer, suggestedCategory, dncId, subject);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      } catch(payerParseErr) {
        Logger.log("Payer body parse failed for " + dncId + ": " + payerParseErr);
      }

      msgs[m].markRead();
    }
  }

  // Backstop: forward any unmatched emails to clinician for manual review.
  // This catches Floyd's wording drift (e.g. "preauth ok" instead of "approved")
  // before status updates are silently lost. Bundled into one digest email
  // so we don't spam on a bad day.
  if (unmatchedSubjects.length > 0 && CLINICIAN_EMAIL) {
    sendClinicianEmail({
      subject: "DNC: " + unmatchedSubjects.length + " biller email(s) need review",
      body: "These biller emails from Floyd were marked read but did not match any known status template. " +
            "Update STATUS_RULES in processBillerEmails or contact Floyd to align template:\n\n" +
            unmatchedSubjects.map(function(s) { return "  • " + s; }).join("\n")
    });
  }
}
    
// Handle GET requests (load drafts)
function doGet(e) {
  var __getAuditStart = Date.now();
  try {
    // Convention: in doGet, request inputs come from `params` (the URL query
    // string). Never reference `data` here — that name is reserved for
    // doPost's JSON body. Mixing them was the bug that hid Brad's cosign
    // note: a handler referenced data.email in doGet where `data` was
    // either undefined or shadowed by a different handler's local var.
    var params = e.parameter || {};
    var action = params.action;
    // Audit every read. Get requests don't carry user identity (GETs are anonymous
    // from Apps Script's perspective), but we still log action + outcome for trace.
    auditLog({ action: "GET:" + (action || "unknown"), user: "", patientId: params.id || "", outcome: "ok" });

    // ---- CHECK ACCESS ----
    // Returns whether a given email is in the Users sheet (i.e. authorized to access the app).
    // This is the data-driven replacement for the hardcoded ALLOWED_EMAILS frontend allowlist.
    // Frontend calls this after Firebase auth completes, with the authenticated email.
    // If allowed=true, also returns the user's profile so the frontend can skip a second call.
    if (action === "checkAccess") {
      var emailToCheck = (params.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!emailToCheck) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "ok", allowed: false, reason: "no email provided"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var profile = getUserRowByEmail(emailToCheck);
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        allowed: !!profile,
        profile: profile || null
     })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    if (action === "loadDraft") {
      var patientId = params.id;
      if (!patientId) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "No patient ID" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      // Multi-source merge cascade (May 2026 fallback layer). Always pulls
      // from all 3 layers; merges non-empty values with priority
      // Drafts > Sheet1 > Throughput. Drafts is only treated as authoritative
      // if it has ≥3 meaningful fields (curated user-entered fields, NOT
      // counting defaultState defaults like visitType/dxCodes/prognosis).
      // Sparse Drafts rows from contamination events get correctly ignored
      // and Sheet1+Throughput merge fills the form for clinical reference.
      var fromDrafts = loadDraftFromDrafts_(patientId);
      var fromSheet1 = loadDraftFromSheet1_(patientId);
      var fromThroughput = loadDraftFromThroughput_(patientId);

      var draftsRichness = countMeaningfulFields_(fromDrafts);
      var sheet1Richness = countMeaningfulFields_(fromSheet1);
      var draftsAuthoritative = draftsRichness >= 3;

      var merged = {};
      mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromThroughput);
      mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromSheet1);
      if (draftsAuthoritative) mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromDrafts);

      if (Object.keys(merged).length === 0) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "not_found" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      var sources = [];
      if (draftsAuthoritative) sources.push("drafts");
      if (fromSheet1) sources.push("sheet1");
      if (fromThroughput) sources.push("throughput");
      var skippedSparseDrafts = (fromDrafts && !draftsAuthoritative);

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        draft: merged,
        sources: sources,
        source: sources[0] || "none",
        draftsRichness: draftsRichness,
        sheet1Richness: sheet1Richness,
        skippedSparseDrafts: skippedSparseDrafts
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
    

    if (action === "getReferrals") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", referrals: [] }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // Read up to 23 columns. Defensive clamp to actual sheet width so older
      // sheets missing the last columns don't throw on getRange.
      //   20-22 = call tracking, 23 = comm preference
      var widthAvailable = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 23);
      var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, widthAvailable).getValues();

      // Site filtering: look up the requesting user's sites from their profile.
      // If no userEmail provided or user has no profile/sites, show everything (admin fallback).
      // Site filter uses canonical equality. With normalizeSiteId applied at
      // both write time (Users sheet save, throughput row creation) and as a
      // defensive read on user locations, we can now use strict equality
      // instead of substring tolerance. Old data still works because the
      // canonical form is computed at read time on each side.
      var userEmail = params.userEmail || "";
      var userSites = [];
      if (userEmail) {
        var userRow = getUserRowByEmail(userEmail);
        if (userRow && userRow.locations && userRow.locations.length > 0) {
          // userRow.locations is already normalized (getUserRowByEmail applies
          // normalizeSiteId on read). No further transform needed here.
          userSites = userRow.locations;
        }
      }

      var referrals = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
        var status = data[i][15] || "Referral Received";
        if (status === "Complete") continue; // hide completed patients
        if (String(status).indexOf("Removed") === 0) continue; // hide removed/withdrawn referrals

        // Site filter: if user has sites, only show referrals tagged to those sites.
        // Untagged referrals (no site ID) show to everyone — they need manual assignment.
        // Normalize each row's siteIds to handle pre-canonicalization data.
        var refSiteIds = String(data[i][18] || "")
          .split(",")
          .map(normalizeSiteId)
          .filter(Boolean);
        if (userSites.length > 0 && refSiteIds.length > 0) {
          var hasMatch = false;
          for (var si = 0; si < userSites.length; si++) {
            if (refSiteIds.indexOf(userSites[si]) !== -1) {
              hasMatch = true;
              break;
            }
          }
          if (!hasMatch) continue; // skip — not this user's site
        }

        // Notes column contains "Ref: [provider] | Ins: ... | Sec: ... | [reason]"
        var notesCol = data[i][17] || "";
        var providerMatch = /^Ref:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(String(notesCol));
        var parsedProvider = providerMatch ? providerMatch[1].trim() : "";
        // Extract primary + secondary insurance from notes for payer lookup
        var insPrimaryMatch = /\|\s*Ins:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(String(notesCol));
        var insPrimary = insPrimaryMatch ? insPrimaryMatch[1].trim() : "";
        var insSecondaryMatch = /\|\s*Sec:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(String(notesCol));
        var insSecondary = insSecondaryMatch ? insSecondaryMatch[1].trim() : "";
        // Look up payer category for the primary insurance — accumulating
        // institutional knowledge so green/yellow/red triage is informed by
        // verified facts, not just heuristic guesses.
        var payerLookup = lookupPayerCategory(insPrimary);
        referrals.push({
          rowIndex: i + 2,
          name: data[i][0] || "",
          dob: formatDobString(data[i][1]),
          phone: data[i][2] || "",
          dncId: data[i][3] || "",
          referralDate: data[i][4] || "",
          called: data[i][5] || "",
          intakeDone: data[i][6] || "",
          scheduledD0: data[i][7] || "",
          d0Complete: data[i][8] || "",
          medicareSubmitted: data[i][9] || "",
          reimbursement: data[i][10] || "",
          scheduledD20: data[i][11] || "",
          d20Complete: data[i][12] || "",
          scheduledD90: data[i][13] || "",
          d90Complete: data[i][14] || "",
          status: status,
          provider: parsedProvider,
          notes: notesCol,
          siteId: data[i][18] || "",
          callAttempts: parseInt(data[i][19], 10) || 0,
          lastCallAt: data[i][20] || "",
          callLog: data[i][21] || "",
          commPref: data[i][22] || "",
          insurancePrimary: insPrimary,
          insuranceSecondary: insSecondary,
          payerLookup: payerLookup
        });
      }
      // Sort: uncalled first (oldest referral first), then by status
      referrals.sort(function(a, b) {
        if (!a.called && b.called) return -1;
        if (a.called && !b.called) return 1;
        if (a.referralDate && b.referralDate) return new Date(a.referralDate) - new Date(b.referralDate);
        return 0;
      });

      // Annotate each referral with its latest notification event so the
      // referral card can show "Last contact: AI call · just now · scheduled 5/12"
      // without a per-card round-trip. Single batch-read of the Notification
      // Log, map by DNC ID, walk each referral once. Latest event wins because
      // we iterate from bottom (chronological tail) of the log.
      try {
        var nLogSheet = initNotificationLog();
        if (nLogSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
          var nLogData = nLogSheet.getRange(2, 1, nLogSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 13).getValues();
          var latestByDnc = {};
          for (var ni = nLogData.length - 1; ni >= 0; ni--) {
            var dId = String(nLogData[ni][1]);
            if (!dId || latestByDnc[dId]) continue; // first seen walking backward = latest
            var rel = {};
            if (nLogData[ni][11]) {
              try { rel = JSON.parse(String(nLogData[ni][11])); } catch(e) { rel = {}; }
            }
            latestByDnc[dId] = {
              eventId: nLogData[ni][0],
              type: nLogData[ni][4],
              channel: nLogData[ni][5],
              status: nLogData[ni][6],
              initiatedAt: nLogData[ni][7],
              completedAt: nLogData[ni][8],
              outcome: nLogData[ni][10],
              relatedData: rel
            };
          }
          for (var ri = 0; ri < referrals.length; ri++) {
            var dncKey = referrals[ri].dncId;
            if (dncKey && latestByDnc[dncKey]) {
              referrals[ri].latestEvent = latestByDnc[dncKey];
            }
          }
        }
      } catch(e) {
        // Don't fail getReferrals on log annotation — fall back to no events
        Logger.log("getReferrals: notification log annotation failed: " + e);
      }

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", referrals: referrals }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    if (action === "loadDraft") {
      var patientId = params.id;
      if (!patientId) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "No patient ID" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      // Multi-source merge cascade (May 2026 fallback layer). Always pulls
      // from all 3 layers; merges non-empty values with priority
      // Drafts > Sheet1 > Throughput. Drafts is only treated as authoritative
      // if it has ≥3 meaningful fields (curated user-entered fields, NOT
      // counting defaultState defaults like visitType/dxCodes/prognosis).
      // Sparse Drafts rows from contamination events get correctly ignored
      // and Sheet1+Throughput merge fills the form for clinical reference.
      var fromDrafts = loadDraftFromDrafts_(patientId);
      var fromSheet1 = loadDraftFromSheet1_(patientId);
      var fromThroughput = loadDraftFromThroughput_(patientId);

      var draftsRichness = countMeaningfulFields_(fromDrafts);
      var sheet1Richness = countMeaningfulFields_(fromSheet1);
      var draftsAuthoritative = draftsRichness >= 3;

      var merged = {};
      mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromThroughput);
      mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromSheet1);
      if (draftsAuthoritative) mergeNonEmpty_(merged, fromDrafts);

      if (Object.keys(merged).length === 0) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "not_found" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      var sources = [];
      if (draftsAuthoritative) sources.push("drafts");
      if (fromSheet1) sources.push("sheet1");
      if (fromThroughput) sources.push("throughput");
      var skippedSparseDrafts = (fromDrafts && !draftsAuthoritative);

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        draft: merged,
        sources: sources,
        source: sources[0] || "none",
        draftsRichness: draftsRichness,
        sheet1Richness: sheet1Richness,
        skippedSparseDrafts: skippedSparseDrafts
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- GET STATUS TAXONOMY ----
    // Frontend reads canonical statuses + aliases + prefixes from here so
    // that the "?" non-canonical indicator is consistent with backend rules.
    // Single source of truth: STATUS_TAXONOMY at top of this file.
    if (action === "listPayerActionQueue") {
      var result = listPayerActionQueue({
        email: e.parameter.email || "",
        status: e.parameter.status || "open",
        includeStale: e.parameter.includeStale || ""
      });
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(result))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
    if (action === "backfillPayerActionSiteIds") {
      var result = backfillPayerActionSiteIds();
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(result))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
    if (action === "getStatusTaxonomy") {
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        canonical: Object.keys(STATUS_TAXONOMY.canonical),
        aliases: STATUS_TAXONOMY.aliases,
        canonicalPrefixes: STATUS_TAXONOMY.canonicalPrefixes
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- LIST PENDING PAYER UPDATES ----
    // For admin/Lindsey review of Floyd-detected payer eligibility info
    // before promoting to the main Payer Reference table.
    if (action === "listPendingPayerUpdates") {
      var pSheet = initPayerUpdatesQueue();
      var pendingUpdates = [];
      if (pSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
        var pRows = pSheet.getRange(2, 1, pSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 9).getValues();
        for (var pi = 0; pi < pRows.length; pi++) {
          if (String(pRows[pi][5]).toLowerCase() !== "pending") continue;
          pendingUpdates.push({
            updateId: pRows[pi][0], payerName: pRows[pi][1],
            suggestedCategory: pRows[pi][2], detectedFrom: pRows[pi][3],
            detectedAt: pRows[pi][4]
          });
        }
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", pendingUpdates: pendingUpdates
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- LIST COSIGN QUEUE ----
    // GET. Reads cosigner email from URL query (params.email). Returns the
    // pending docs for that user. Was incorrectly placed in doPost — that
    // meant the frontend's GET fetch never matched, returning "Unknown action"
    // and showing an empty queue even when rows existed in the sheet.
    if (action === "listCosignQueue") {
      var csSheet = initCosignSheet();
      var csRows = csSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var csEmail = String(params.email || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!csEmail) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "email parameter required"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var queue = [];
      for (var ci = 1; ci < csRows.length; ci++) {
        // Tolerant compare: cosigner email column may drift in casing
        // (device-typed entries vs system-set rows). Normalize before match.
        var rowCosigner = String(csRows[ci][9] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (rowCosigner !== csEmail) continue;
        if (String(csRows[ci][10]) !== "pending") continue;
        queue.push({
          cosignId: csRows[ci][0], docType: csRows[ci][1], docName: csRows[ci][2],
          docUrl: csRows[ci][3], docId: csRows[ci][4], patientId: csRows[ci][5],
          authorEmail: csRows[ci][6], authorRole: csRows[ci][7], siteId: csRows[ci][8],
          createdAt: csRows[ci][11], notes: csRows[ci][13]
        });
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", queue: queue
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Unknown action" }))
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);

  } catch (err) {
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: err.toString() }))
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
  }
}
// ============================================================
// LOAD DRAFT MULTI-SOURCE HELPERS (May 2026 fallback layer)
// ============================================================
// loadDraft cascade:
//   1. Drafts row (highest authority IF it has ≥3 meaningful fields)
//   2. Sheet1 most recent row (durable, structured)
//   3. Throughput row (demographics + insurance parsed from notes)
// Merge non-empty values with priority: drafts > sheet1 > throughput.
// "Meaningful" = a curated list of user-entered fields, NOT defaults like
// visitType/dxCodes/prognosis which defaultState always populates.
// Without this richness check, an empty Drafts row still has 20+ "non-empty"
// fields from defaults and would be treated as authoritative — which was
// the exact failure mode behind Miller/Buchanon loading blank.

function countMeaningfulFields_(obj) {
  if (!obj) return 0;
  var stringFields = [
    "patientName", "age", "sex", "dob", "phone", "address", "city", "zip",
    "painRest", "painBest", "painWorst", "medications", "a1c",
    "referringProvider", "insuranceName", "memberId", "painDuration",
    "subjective", "subjectivePre", "subjectivePost", "plan",
    "deviceSerial", "procedureDaySubjective"
  ];
  var arrayFields = ["pmhx", "priorTx", "painDescriptors", "functionalLimitations"];
  var count = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < stringFields.length; i++) {
    var v = obj[stringFields[i]];
    if (v && typeof v === "string" && v.trim() !== "") count++;
  }
  for (var j = 0; j < arrayFields.length; j++) {
    var arr = obj[arrayFields[j]];
    if (Array.isArray(arr) && arr.length > 0) count++;
  }
  return count;
}

function mergeNonEmpty_(target, source) {
  if (!source) return target;
  for (var k in source) {
    var v = source[k];
    if (v == null) continue;
    if (typeof v === "string" && v === "") continue;
    if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 0) continue;
    target[k] = v;
  }
  return target;
}

function loadDraftFromDrafts_(patientId) {
  var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Drafts");
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return null;
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] === patientId) {
      try { return JSON.parse(data[i][2]); }
      catch(e) { return null; }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function loadDraftFromSheet1_(patientId) {
  var sheet1 = getOrCreateSheet("Sheet1");
  if (sheet1.getLastRow() < 2) return null;
  var data = sheet1.getDataRange().getValues();
  var headers = data[0];
  var mostRecentRow = null;
  for (var j = 1; j < data.length; j++) {
    if (data[j][1] === patientId) mostRecentRow = data[j];
  }
  if (!mostRecentRow) return null;
  return reconstructFromSheet1Row_(mostRecentRow, headers);
}

function reconstructFromSheet1Row_(row, headers) {
  var result = {};
  // Comprehensive field map: every editable string/numeric column in Sheet1
  // → corresponding form state key. Updated May 2026 to fix the incomplete
  // map that caused Miller/Buchanon to load blank.
  var fieldMap = {
    "Patient ID": "patientId", "Date": "date", "Visit Type": "visitType",
    "Age": "age", "Sex": "sex", "DM Type": "diabetesType",
    "DM Duration (yrs)": "diabetesDuration",
    "Referring Provider": "referringProvider", "A1C": "a1c",
    "Medications": "medications",
    "Pain Notes": "painNotes", "Functional Notes": "functionalNotes",
    "PMHx Notes": "pmhxNotes", "Therapy Notes": "txNotes",
    "Social Notes": "socialNotes", "Lab Notes": "labNotes",
    "Pain Rest": "painRest", "Pain Best": "painBest", "Pain Worst": "painWorst",
    "Glucose Control": "glucoseControl",
    "Pain Duration": "painDuration", "Pain Duration Unit": "painDurationUnit",
    "Prognosis": "prognosis",
    "Neuropathy Type": "neuropathyType",
    "Neuropathy Distribution": "neuropathyDistribution",
    "Device Notes": "deviceNotes",
    "Subjective": "subjective", "Subjective Pre-Explant": "subjectivePre",
    "Subjective Post-Explant": "subjectivePost",
    "Plan": "plan", "Notes": "notes",
    // Sensory exam (NEW in this expansion)
    "NRS Pain Pre": "nrsPainPre", "NRS Pain Post": "nrsPainPost",
    "RMSSD Pre (ms)": "rmssdPre", "RMSSD Post (ms)": "rmssdPost",
    "Quarter Pre": "coinQuarterPre", "Quarter Post": "coinQuarterPost",
    "Dime Pre": "coinDimePre", "Dime Post": "coinDimePost",
    "Penny Pre": "coinPennyPre", "Penny Post": "coinPennyPost",
    "SLS Right Sec Pre": "slsRightSecPre", "SLS Right Sec Post": "slsRightSecPost",
    "SLS Right Fall Pre": "slsRightFallPre", "SLS Right Fall Post": "slsRightFallPost",
    "SLS Left Sec Pre": "slsLeftSecPre", "SLS Left Sec Post": "slsLeftSecPost",
    "SLS Left Fall Pre": "slsLeftFallPre", "SLS Left Fall Post": "slsLeftFallPost",
    "10m Walk Pre (s)": "walkTimePre", "10m Walk Post (s)": "walkTimePost",
    // Explant (NEW)
    "Explant Site Clean": "explantSiteClean",
    "Explant Induration": "explantInduration",
    "Explant Erythema": "explantErythema",
    "Explant Tolerated": "explantTolerated",
    "Explant Complication": "explantComplication",
    "Explant Complication Detail": "explantComplicationDetail",
    "Explant Notes": "explantNotes"
  };
  // Array fields stored as "; "-joined strings
  var arrayFieldMap = {
    "PMHx": "pmhx", "Prior Therapies": "priorTx",
    "Pain Descriptors": "painDescriptors",
    "Dx Codes": "dxCodes", "Contraindications": "contraindications"
  };
  // Foot grid counts: Sheet1 stores summed counts only ("8/12"), not the
  // 12-bool array. Site-level detail is unrecoverable from Sheet1 alone —
  // op note on Drive has it. For Phase 1 we expose the count in a separate
  // field the frontend can display as reference. Form's editable foot grid
  // arrays stay empty for new exam entry on follow-up visits.
  var countFieldMap = {
    "Left Foot Pre (/12)": "leftFootPreCount",
    "Left Foot Post (/12)": "leftFootPostCount",
    "Right Foot Pre (/12)": "rightFootPreCount",
    "Right Foot Post (/12)": "rightFootPostCount"
  };
  for (var k = 0; k < headers.length; k++) {
    var h = headers[k];
    var val = row[k];
    if (fieldMap[h]) {
      result[fieldMap[h]] = val == null ? "" : String(val);
    } else if (arrayFieldMap[h]) {
      var s = val == null ? "" : String(val);
      result[arrayFieldMap[h]] = s ? s.split(/;\s*/).filter(function(x){return x;}) : [];
    } else if (countFieldMap[h]) {
      result[countFieldMap[h]] = val === "" || val == null ? null : Number(val);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

function loadDraftFromThroughput_(patientId) {
  var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
  var tpRow = findThroughputRow(tpSheet, patientId);
  if (tpRow < 1) return null;
  var rowData = tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 1, 1, 18).getValues()[0];
  var notes = String(rowData[17] || "");
  var result = {
    patientId: patientId,
    patientName: rowData[0] || "",
    dob: formatDobString(rowData[1]),
    phone: rowData[2] || ""
  };
  // Parse insurance from notes string. Frontend writes these as
  // "Ins: <name> #<id>" and "Sec: <name> #<id>" via parseReferralPhoto +
  // updateReferralCorrections, so format is stable.
  var insMatch = /\bIns:\s*([^|#]+?)(?:\s*#([^|]+))?(?:\s*\||$)/i.exec(notes);
  var secMatch = /\bSec:\s*([^|#]+?)(?:\s*#([^|]+))?(?:\s*\||$)/i.exec(notes);
  if (insMatch) {
    result.insuranceName = insMatch[1].trim();
    if (insMatch[2]) result.memberId = insMatch[2].trim();
  }
  if (secMatch) {
    result.insuranceName2 = secMatch[1].trim();
    if (secMatch[2]) result.memberId2 = secMatch[2].trim();
  }
  // Parse referring provider from "Ref: <name>" prefix
  var refMatch = /^Ref:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(notes);
  if (refMatch) result.referringProvider = refMatch[1].trim();
  return result;
}
// Handle POST requests (submit data or save drafts)
function doPost(e) {
  try {
    var data = {};
    try { data = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents); } catch(parseErr) { data = {}; }
    // Action can come from query params (for webhooks with foreign JSON bodies)
    // or from the JSON body (for internal app calls)
    var action = (e.parameter && e.parameter._action) || data._action || "submit";

    // HIPAA audit: record every request that touches this endpoint, bracketing the handler
    // so we capture both the inbound action and the eventual outcome. The wrapper approach
    // (instead of sprinkling audit calls everywhere) ensures no action can forget to log.
    var __auditStart = Date.now();
    var __auditUser = (data && data.userEmail) || "";
    var __auditPatientId = (data && (data.patientId || data._patientId)) || "";
    // Normalize patient ID to DNC-XXXX pseudonym only; never log names
    if (!/^DNC-/.test(String(__auditPatientId))) __auditPatientId = "";
    // Capture the original return path so we can stamp audit on exit
    var __origCreate = ContentService.createTextOutput;
    var __auditWrite = function(outcome, detail) {
      auditLog({
        action: action,
        user: __auditUser,
        patientId: __auditPatientId,
        outcome: outcome,
        latencyMs: Date.now() - __auditStart,
        detail: detail || ""
      });
    };

    // ---- LOG CALL ATTEMPT ----
    // Records a call that did NOT result in reaching the patient. Increments
    // callAttempts counter, stamps lastCallAt, and appends a structured entry
