    // to the call log ("4/20 2:45pm — No answer"). Status is unchanged — the
    // referral stays in "Referral Received" so the card doesn't falsely advance.
    // When patient IS reached, the frontend uses the existing Start Intake
    // flow (generateId + form load) — this endpoint is only for unreached calls.
    if (action === "logCallAttempt") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var rowIndex = data.rowIndex;
      var outcome = data.outcome || "No answer";
      var reason = data.reason || "";  // free-text for "Other"
      var followupNote = data.followupNote || "";  // optional "call back Thu 7pm" style forward-looking note
      if (!rowIndex) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Missing rowIndex" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // Read current attempts + log (defensive: old sheets may have blank cells)
      var currentAttempts = parseInt(sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 20).getValue(), 10) || 0;
      var currentLog = String(sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 22).getValue() || "");
      var newAttempts = currentAttempts + 1;

      // Format timestamp short: "4/20 2:45pm" — human-readable for tally display
      var now = new Date();
      var tsShort = (now.getMonth() + 1) + "/" + now.getDate() + " " +
        ((now.getHours() % 12 || 12)) + ":" + (now.getMinutes() < 10 ? "0" : "") + now.getMinutes() +
        (now.getHours() >= 12 ? "pm" : "am");

      // Entry format: "4/23 11:45am — No answer (other-reason) | 'call back Thu 7pm' [bradley]"
      // followupNote is quoted inline so it's visually distinct from the outcome
      // and the "Other" reason.  Both note fields are optional.
      var actorEmail = (typeof Session !== "undefined" && Session.getActiveUser) ? Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() : "";
      var entryText = tsShort + " — " + outcome +
                      (reason ? " (" + reason + ")" : "") +
                      (followupNote ? " | \"" + followupNote + "\"" : "") +
                      (actorEmail ? " [" + actorEmail.split("@")[0] + "]" : "");
      var newLog = currentLog ? (currentLog + "\n" + entryText) : entryText;

      // Write: 20=Call Attempts, 21=Last Call At (ISO), 22=Call Log (text), 17=Last Updated
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 20).setValue(newAttempts);
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 21).setValue(now.toISOString());
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 22).setValue(newLog);
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 17).setValue(now.toISOString());
// ---- WRONG NUMBER WEBHOOK ----
      // Fire one email to admin when an MA logs "Wrong number". Suppression
      // is anchored on the verification marker: walk the call log backwards
      // (newest entries last in the string), and if we find a "✓ phone
      // verified" marker before any prior "Wrong number" entry, suppress —
      // admin already confirmed this number after the last problem. If we
      // find a prior "Wrong number" first (or no markers at all), this is
      // the first hit since the last verification (or ever), so fire.
      // Self-resets each time admin verifies via the green-check UI.
      if (outcome === "Wrong number") {
        var logLines = currentLog ? currentLog.split("\n") : [];
        var shouldFire = true;
        // Walk backwards: most recent entry = highest index
        for (var li = logLines.length - 1; li >= 0; li--) {
          var line = logLines[li];
          if (line.indexOf("✓ phone verified") === 0) {
            // Most recent relevant marker is a verification — admin signed off
            // on this number after the prior problem. Suppress this email.
            shouldFire = false;
            break;
          }
          if (line.indexOf("Wrong number") !== -1 && line.indexOf("✓") !== 0) {
            // Most recent relevant marker is a prior wrong-number entry that
            // hasn't been verified away. Admin already got an email for this
            // incident; suppress.
            shouldFire = false;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (shouldFire) {
          try {
            var rowVals = sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, 22).getValues()[0];
            // Column map: 1=Date, 2=DOB, 3=Phone, 4=DNC ID, 5=Referral Date, ...
            // Wait — actually: 1=Name, 2=DOB, 3=Phone, 4=DNC ID, 5=Referral Date,
            // 6=Called, 7=Intake Done, ... 16=Status, 17=Last Updated, 18=Notes,
            // 19=Site ID, 20=Call Attempts, 21=Last Call At, 22=Call Log
            var pName = String(rowVals[0] || "Unknown");
            var pDob = String(rowVals[1] || "");
            var pPhone = String(rowVals[2] || "");
            var pDncId = String(rowVals[3] || "");
            var pStatus = String(rowVals[15] || "");
            var pSite = String(rowVals[18] || "");
            var pNotes = String(rowVals[17] || "");
            // Pull referring provider out of notes for context
            var providerMatch = /^Ref:\s*([^|]+?)\s*(?:\||$)/i.exec(pNotes);
            var pProvider = providerMatch ? providerMatch[1].trim() : "(not parsed)";
            var ssId = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getId();
            var ssUrl = "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/" + ssId +
                        "/edit#gid=" + sheet.getSheetId() + "&range=C" + rowIndex;
            var bodyParts = [
              "An MA flagged this number as wrong. Either correct the phone field in the throughput sheet, or — if the existing number is right — tap the red X on the referral card to mark it verified.",
              "",
              "Patient: " + pName + (pDncId ? " (" + pDncId + ")" : ""),
              "DOB: " + (pDob || "—"),
              "Phone (current, suspect wrong): " + (pPhone || "—"),
              "Referring: " + pProvider,
              "Site: " + (pSite || "—"),
              "Status: " + (pStatus || "—"),
              "",
              "Logged by: " + (actorEmail || "unknown"),
              "When: " + tsShort,
              (reason ? "MA note: " + reason : ""),
              (followupNote ? "Follow-up note: " + followupNote : ""),
              "",
              "Direct link to row:",
              ssUrl,
              "",
              "Prior call log:",
              (currentLog || "(none)"),
              "",
              "You'll only get one email per data-quality incident. Once you tap the green check on the card (or correct the phone and someone re-verifies), future wrong-number hits will fire a new email."
            ];
            sendClinicianEmail({
              to: EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL,
              subject: "[DNC] Wrong number flagged: " + pName + (pDncId ? " (" + pDncId + ")" : ""),
              body: bodyParts.filter(function(s){ return s !== ""; }).join("\n").replace(/\n\n+/g, "\n\n"),
              skipEvanCc: true
            });
          } catch(e) {
            Logger.log("Wrong number webhook failed: " + e);
          }
        }
      }
      // TODO: when user decides on 3-attempt escalation behavior, hook here.
      // Options under consideration: email CLINICIAN_EMAIL when newAttempts === 3,
      // or auto-flip status to "Unreachable - review", or both. Left as a no-op
      // for now per explicit user decision to defer this choice.

      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        callAttempts: newAttempts,
        lastCallAt: now.toISOString(),
        callLog: newLog
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
// ---- MARK PHONE VERIFIED ----
    // Admin/clinician confirms the phone number on a referral row is correct.
    // Appends a verification marker to the call log. The marker is the anchor
    // the wrong-number webhook uses to decide whether to suppress emails — any
    // "Wrong number" entry AFTER the most recent verification fires a fresh
    // email; entries before are silent. Self-resetting per data-quality
    // incident, no separate flag column needed.
    if (action === "markPhoneVerified") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var rowIndex = data.rowIndex;
      if (!rowIndex) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Missing rowIndex" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var actorEmail = data.userEmail || "";
      var currentLog = String(sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 22).getValue() || "");
      var now = new Date();
      var tsShort = (now.getMonth() + 1) + "/" + now.getDate() + " " +
        ((now.getHours() % 12 || 12)) + ":" + (now.getMinutes() < 10 ? "0" : "") + now.getMinutes() +
        (now.getHours() >= 12 ? "pm" : "am");
      // Marker format intentionally distinct from call attempts ("✓" prefix,
      // no outcome word) so the webhook's regex match is unambiguous.
      var marker = "✓ phone verified " + tsShort +
                   (actorEmail ? " [" + actorEmail.split("@")[0] + "]" : "");
      var newLog = currentLog ? (currentLog + "\n" + marker) : marker;
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 22).setValue(newLog);
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 17).setValue(now.toISOString());
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        callLog: newLog
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
    // ---- SEARCH PATIENTS BY NAME ----
    // Powers the inline "Find by name" input on the Assess tab. Returns a
    // ranked list of patients whose Name column contains the query substring,
    // filtered to the requesting user's assigned sites (if any). Used to let
    // an MA / clinician type a partial name and get the DNC ID + basic context
    // without knowing the ID ahead of time. Cross-site lookup is intentionally
    // NOT supported — privacy minimization for per-site staff. Admin-style
    // access (user with no sites configured) gets unfiltered results.
    if (action === "searchPatients") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", results: [] }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var rawQuery = String(data.query || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (rawQuery.length < 2) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", results: [] }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      // Site filter uses canonical equality. normalizeSiteId is applied at
      // both write paths and as defensive read on user locations + each row's
      // siteId. Strict equality is correct here.
      var searchUserEmail = data.userEmail || "";
      var allowedSites = [];
      if (searchUserEmail) {
        var searchUserRow = getUserRowByEmail(searchUserEmail);
        if (searchUserRow && searchUserRow.locations && searchUserRow.locations.length > 0) {
          // Already normalized by getUserRowByEmail
          allowedSites = searchUserRow.locations;
        }
      }

      var widthS = Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 22);
      var allRows = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, widthS).getValues();
      var matches = [];
      var filteredBySite = 0; // diagnostic counter
      for (var ri = 0; ri < allRows.length; ri++) {
        var rowName = String(allRows[ri][0] || "").toLowerCase();
        if (!rowName || rowName.indexOf(rawQuery) === -1) continue;

        // Site filter: skip rows the user doesn't have access to.
        // Normalize each row's site IDs (handles pre-canonicalization data).
        var rowSites = String(allRows[ri][18] || "")
          .split(",")
          .map(normalizeSiteId)
          .filter(Boolean);
        if (allowedSites.length > 0 && rowSites.length > 0) {
          var sHit = false;
          for (var sj = 0; sj < allowedSites.length; sj++) {
            if (rowSites.indexOf(allowedSites[sj]) !== -1) { sHit = true; break; }
          }
          if (!sHit) { filteredBySite++; continue; }
        }

        matches.push({
          rowIndex: ri + 2,
          name: allRows[ri][0] || "",
          dob: formatDobString(allRows[ri][1]),
          phone: allRows[ri][2] || "",
          dncId: allRows[ri][3] || "",
          status: allRows[ri][15] || "",
          siteId: allRows[ri][18] || ""
        });
      }
      // Log diagnostic info so you can check Apps Script Executions if search
      // stops working again. No PHI logged — just counts and non-PHI fields.
      Logger.log("searchPatients: query='" + rawQuery + "' user=" + searchUserEmail +
                 " allowedSites=[" + allowedSites.join(",") + "]" +
                 " matched=" + matches.length +
                 " filteredBySite=" + filteredBySite);
      // Rank: exact prefix matches first, then substring. Within each bucket,
      // most-recent-activity first (reverse insertion order approximates this).
      matches.sort(function(a, b) {
        var aPre = a.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(rawQuery) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
        var bPre = b.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(rawQuery) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
        if (aPre !== bPre) return aPre - bPre;
        return b.rowIndex - a.rowIndex;
      });
      // Return top 10 — a dropdown isn't useful past that
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok",
        results: matches.slice(0, 10)
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- UPDATE REFERRAL STATUS ----
    if (action === "updateReferral") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var rowIndex = data.rowIndex;
      var field = data.field;
      var value = data.value;
      if (!rowIndex || !field) {
        __auditWrite("error", "missing rowIndex or field");
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Missing rowIndex or field" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // Map field names to column indices
      var fieldMap = {
        "patientName": 1, "dob": 2, "phone": 3,
        "dncId": 4, "called": 6, "intakeDone": 7,
        "scheduledD0": 8, "d0Complete": 9,
        "medicareSubmitted": 10, "reimbursement": 11,
        "scheduledD20": 12, "d20Complete": 13,
        "scheduledD90": 14, "d90Complete": 15,
        "status": 16, "notes": 18, "siteId": 19,
        "commPref": 23
      };
      var col = fieldMap[field];
      if (!col) {
        __auditWrite("error", "unknown field: " + field);
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Unknown field: " + field }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // DOB needs the apostrophe prefix to prevent Sheets auto-date-parse
      if (field === "dob" && value) {
        value = "'" + String(value).replace(/^'/, "");
      }
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, col).setValue(value);
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); // Last Updated
      __auditWrite("ok", "field=" + field);
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- UPDATE REFERRAL CORRECTIONS ----
    // Reverse-sync from the intake form back to the referral row when the MA corrects
    // any of: name, DOB, phone, referring provider, insurance details. Notes-string
    // segments (Ref:, Ins:, Sec:) get surgically rewritten while preserving the
    // CONTRA: marker and any free-text reason.
    if (action === "updateReferralCorrections") {
      var crSheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var crRow = data.rowIndex;
      if (!crRow) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Missing rowIndex" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // Direct column updates
      if (data.patientName !== undefined) crSheet.getRange(crRow, 1).setValue(data.patientName || "");
      if (data.dob !== undefined) {
        var dobVal = data.dob ? "'" + String(data.dob).replace(/^'/, "") : "";
        crSheet.getRange(crRow, 2).setValue(dobVal);
      }
      if (data.phone !== undefined) crSheet.getRange(crRow, 3).setValue(data.phone || "");

      // Notes-string surgical rewrite. Existing format from addReferral / parseReferralPhoto:
      //   "Ref: Dr Smith | CONTRA: pacemaker | Ins: Medicare Part B #1EG4 | Sec: BCBS #ABC | reason text"
      // We replace Ref:/Ins:/Sec: segments in place; CONTRA: and trailing reason are preserved untouched.
      if (data.referringProvider !== undefined ||
          data.insuranceName !== undefined || data.memberId !== undefined ||
          data.insuranceName2 !== undefined || data.memberId2 !== undefined) {
        var existingNotes = String(crSheet.getRange(crRow, 18).getValue() || "");
        // Split into pipe-separated segments, then re-emit
        var segments = existingNotes.split("|").map(function(s){ return s.trim(); });
        // Bucket the segments
        var refSeg = "", contraSeg = "", insSeg = "", secSeg = "", otherSegs = [];
        for (var si = 0; si < segments.length; si++) {
          var seg = segments[si];
          if (!seg) continue;
          if (/^Ref:/i.test(seg)) refSeg = seg;
          else if (/^CONTRA:/i.test(seg)) contraSeg = seg;
          else if (/^Ins:/i.test(seg)) insSeg = seg;
          else if (/^Sec:/i.test(seg)) secSeg = seg;
          else otherSegs.push(seg);
        }
        // Apply corrections
        if (data.referringProvider !== undefined) {
          refSeg = data.referringProvider ? ("Ref: " + data.referringProvider) : "";
        }
        if (data.insuranceName !== undefined || data.memberId !== undefined) {
          // Read current values to preserve whatever wasn't sent
          var curInsName = "", curInsId = "";
          var insMatch = /^Ins:\s*([^#]*?)(?:\s*#\s*(.+))?$/i.exec(insSeg);
          if (insMatch) { curInsName = (insMatch[1] || "").trim(); curInsId = (insMatch[2] || "").trim(); }
          var newInsName = data.insuranceName !== undefined ? data.insuranceName : curInsName;
          var newInsId = data.memberId !== undefined ? data.memberId : curInsId;
          if (newInsName || newInsId) {
            insSeg = "Ins: " + (newInsName || "Primary") + (newInsId ? " #" + newInsId : "");
          } else {
            insSeg = "";
          }
        }
        if (data.insuranceName2 !== undefined || data.memberId2 !== undefined) {
          var curSecName = "", curSecId = "";
          var secMatch = /^Sec:\s*([^#]*?)(?:\s*#\s*(.+))?$/i.exec(secSeg);
          if (secMatch) { curSecName = (secMatch[1] || "").trim(); curSecId = (secMatch[2] || "").trim(); }
          var newSecName = data.insuranceName2 !== undefined ? data.insuranceName2 : curSecName;
          var newSecId = data.memberId2 !== undefined ? data.memberId2 : curSecId;
          if (newSecName || newSecId) {
            secSeg = "Sec: " + (newSecName || "Secondary") + (newSecId ? " #" + newSecId : "");
          } else {
            secSeg = "";
          }
        }
        // Re-emit in canonical order: Ref | CONTRA | Ins | Sec | other
        var rebuilt = [refSeg, contraSeg, insSeg, secSeg].concat(otherSegs).filter(function(s){ return s; }).join(" | ");
        crSheet.getRange(crRow, 18).setValue(rebuilt);
      }

      crSheet.getRange(crRow, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); // Last Updated
      // Build a field-list detail string (don't log values, just which fields were touched)
      var crFields = [];
      if (data.patientName !== undefined) crFields.push("name");
      if (data.dob !== undefined) crFields.push("dob");
      if (data.phone !== undefined) crFields.push("phone");
      if (data.referringProvider !== undefined) crFields.push("provider");
      if (data.insuranceName !== undefined || data.memberId !== undefined) crFields.push("ins1");
      if (data.insuranceName2 !== undefined || data.memberId2 !== undefined) crFields.push("ins2");
      __auditWrite("ok", "fields=" + crFields.join(","));
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- ACCESS REQUEST ----
    if (action === "requestAccess") {
      var adminEmail = CLINICIAN_EMAIL || "evan.leitner@gmail.com";
      var subj = "DNC Platform: Access request from " + (data.name || "unknown");
      var body = "A new access request was submitted:\n\n" +
        "Name: " + (data.name || "") + "\n" +
        "Email: " + (data.email || "") + "\n" +
        "Role: " + (data.role || "") + "\n" +
        "Reason: " + (data.reason || "none provided") + "\n\n" +
        "To approve:\n" +
        "1. Add their email to ALLOWED_EMAILS in dpn_assess.html\n" +
        "2. Firebase Console > Authentication > Users > Add user with this email and a temporary password\n" +
        "3. Send them the temporary password\n\n" +
        "Submitted at: " + new Date().toISOString();
      try {
        MailApp.sendEmail({ to: adminEmail, subject: subj, body: body });
      } catch(mailErr) {}
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", action: "requestSubmitted" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- GET USER PROFILE ----
    if (action === "getUserProfile") {
      var profile = getUserRowByEmail(data.email);
      // Inline the signature as a base64 data URL so the frontend can reuse
      // it (e.g., auto-fill witness signature on consents) without needing
      // to round-trip through Drive — Drive isn't directly fetchable from the
      // browser due to CORS. The data URL is small (~5KB typical) so this
      // doesn't bloat the response meaningfully.
      var signatureDataUrl = "";
      if (profile && profile.signatureUrl) {
        try {
          var sigBlob = fetchSignatureBlob(profile.signatureUrl);
          if (sigBlob) {
            var b64 = Utilities.base64Encode(sigBlob.getBytes());
            signatureDataUrl = "data:" + (sigBlob.getContentType() || "image/png") + ";base64," + b64;
          }
        } catch(e) {
          Logger.log("getUserProfile: signature embed failed for " + data.email + ": " + e);
        }
      }
      if (profile) profile.signatureDataUrl = signatureDataUrl;
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", profile: profile
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- SAVE USER PROFILE ----
    if (action === "saveUserProfile") {
      if (!data.email) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "email required"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var usersSheet = initUsersSheet();

      // Save signature image to Drive if provided (PNG, transparent bg expected)
      var signatureUrl = data.signatureUrl || "";
      if (data.signatureImage && SIGNATURES_FOLDER_ID && SIGNATURES_FOLDER_ID !== "PASTE_FOLDER_ID_HERE") {
        try {
          var sigFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SIGNATURES_FOLDER_ID);
          var sigB64 = data.signatureImage.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
          var sigDecoded = Utilities.base64Decode(sigB64);
          var sigFileName = data.email.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_") + ".png";
          // Delete any existing file with same name (replace, don't accumulate)
          var existingSigs = sigFolder.getFilesByName(sigFileName);
          while (existingSigs.hasNext()) { existingSigs.next().setTrashed(true); }
          var sigBlob = Utilities.newBlob(sigDecoded, "image/png", sigFileName);
          var sigFile = sigFolder.createFile(sigBlob);
          signatureUrl = sigFile.getUrl();
        } catch(sigErr) {
          Logger.log("Signature save failed: " + sigErr);
        }
      }

      var existingUser = getUserRowByEmail(data.email);
      var nowTs = new Date();
      // Normalize location values so they match canonical Sites sheet entries.
      // This is the write-time discipline that lets read-time site filtering
      // use strict equality instead of substring matching.
      var normalizedLocations = (data.locations || []).map(normalizeSiteId).filter(Boolean);
      var locationsCsv = normalizedLocations.join(",");
      var statesCsv = (data.licensedStates || []).join(",");
      var rowValues = [
        data.email, data.displayName || "", data.credentials || "", data.role || "",
        locationsCsv, statesCsv, signatureUrl,
        existingUser ? existingUser.createdAt : nowTs, nowTs
      ];
      if (existingUser) {
        usersSheet.getRange(existingUser.rowIndex, 1, 1, 9).setValues([rowValues]);
      } else {
        usersSheet.appendRow(rowValues);
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", profile: getUserRowByEmail(data.email)
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- LIST SITES ----
    if (action === "listSites") {
      var sitesSheet = initSitesSheet();
      var sitesRows = sitesSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var sites = [];
      for (var si = 1; si < sitesRows.length; si++) {
        if (sitesRows[si][0]) sites.push({
          siteId: sitesRows[si][0], name: sitesRows[si][1], state: sitesRows[si][3],
          coveredStates: String(sitesRows[si][4] || sitesRows[si][3] || "").split(",").map(function(s){return s.trim()}).filter(Boolean)
        });
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", sites: sites
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- RESOLVE PAYER UPDATE ----
    // Approve or reject a pending payer update from Floyd's eligibility
    // detection. On approval, the suggested category is promoted to the
    // main Payer Reference table (creating or updating the row).
    // On rejection, the queue row is just marked rejected (kept for audit).
    if (action === "resolvePayerUpdate") {
      var ruUpdateId = data.updateId;
      var ruDecision = String(data.decision || "").toLowerCase(); // "approved" or "rejected"
      var ruReviewer = data.email || "";
      if (!ruUpdateId || (ruDecision !== "approved" && ruDecision !== "rejected")) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "updateId and decision (approved/rejected) required"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var ruSheet = initPayerUpdatesQueue();
      var ruRows = ruSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var ruRowIdx = -1;
      for (var rui = 1; rui < ruRows.length; rui++) {
        if (String(ruRows[rui][0]) === String(ruUpdateId)) { ruRowIdx = rui + 1; break; }
      }
      if (ruRowIdx === -1) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "Update not found"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }

      // Mark queue row resolved
      var nowResolve = new Date().toISOString();
      ruSheet.getRange(ruRowIdx, 6).setValue(ruDecision);
      ruSheet.getRange(ruRowIdx, 7).setValue(ruReviewer);
      ruSheet.getRange(ruRowIdx, 8).setValue(nowResolve);

      // If approved, write/update Payer Reference table
      if (ruDecision === "approved") {
        var refSheet = initPayerReferenceSheet();
        var refPayerName = String(ruRows[ruRowIdx-1][1] || "");
        var refCategory = String(ruRows[ruRowIdx-1][2] || "");
        // Queue-only markers: not real categories. Translate to final values.
        //   needs-reverification → user wants to downgrade → preauth-required
        //   promote-precedent    → user wants to promote   → auto-approved
        if (refCategory === "needs-reverification") {
          refCategory = "preauth-required";
        } else if (refCategory === "promote-precedent") {
          refCategory = "auto-approved";
        }
        // Find or create the row
        var refRows = refSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        var refRowIdx = -1;
        for (var rfi = 1; rfi < refRows.length; rfi++) {
          if (String(refRows[rfi][0]).toLowerCase() === refPayerName.toLowerCase()) {
            refRowIdx = rfi + 1; break;
          }
        }
        var todayDate = nowResolve.substring(0, 10);
        if (refRowIdx > 0) {
          // Update existing
          refSheet.getRange(refRowIdx, 2).setValue(refCategory);
          refSheet.getRange(refRowIdx, 6).setValue(todayDate);
          refSheet.getRange(refRowIdx, 7).setValue(ruReviewer);
        } else {
          // Add new
          refSheet.appendRow([
            refPayerName, refCategory, "", 0, "",
            todayDate, ruReviewer,
            "Auto-promoted from Floyd eligibility detection (queue " + ruUpdateId + ")"
          ]);
        }
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: "ok", updateId: ruUpdateId, decision: ruDecision
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
// ---- RESOLVE PAYER ACTION ----
    // Marks a Payer Action Queue row as resolved/superseded/expired.
    // Body: { _action: "resolvePayerAction", actionId, resolution, notes?, email? }
    // Returns: { status, actionId, newStatus, resolvedAt } or { status: "error", message }
    if (action === "resolvePayerAction") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry) actionId=" + (data.actionId || "") + " resolution=" + (data.resolution || ""));
      var rpaResult = resolvePayerAction({
        actionId: data.actionId,
        resolution: data.resolution,
        notes: data.notes || "",
        email: data.email || ""
      });
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(rpaResult))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }
    // ---- SIGN COSIGN ----
    if (action === "signCosign") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry) cosignId=" + (data.cosignId || ""));
      var signSheet = initCosignSheet();
      var signRows = signSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var signCosignId = data.cosignId;
      var signRowIdx = -1;
      for (var sci = 1; sci < signRows.length; sci++) {
        if (String(signRows[sci][0]) === String(signCosignId)) { signRowIdx = sci + 1; break; }
      }
      if (signRowIdx === -1) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "Cosign entry not found"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var signDocId = signRows[signRowIdx-1][4];
      var signCosignerEmail = signRows[signRowIdx-1][9];
      var signCosigner = getUserRowByEmail(signCosignerEmail);
      if (!signCosigner) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "Cosigner profile missing"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // Append signature block to the doc.
      // If the cosigner has no signature on file (or fetch fails), we still
      // append the caption with their name + timestamp — better to have
      // attribution than nothing — but flag it loudly so it's not silent.
      var signatureMissing = false;
      try {
        var signDoc = DocumentApp.openById(signDocId);
        var signBody = signDoc.getBody();
        var signSigBlob = fetchSignatureBlob(signCosigner.signatureUrl);
        if (!signSigBlob) {
          signatureMissing = true;
          Logger.log("signCosign: NO SIGNATURE for " + signCosignerEmail + " — signing without image. signatureUrl=" + (signCosigner.signatureUrl || "(empty)"));
        }
        appendSignatureBlock(signBody, "Cosigned by", signCosigner, signSigBlob);
        signDoc.saveAndClose();
      } catch(docErr) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "Doc update failed: " + docErr.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      // If signature was missing, alert Evan so the user can fix their profile.
      // Send only on the missing case — a successful sign with image doesn't need email.
      if (signatureMissing) {
        try {
          MailApp.sendEmail({
            to: EVAN_ADMIN_EMAIL,
            subject: "DNC: Cosign signed WITHOUT signature image — " + signCosignerEmail,
            body: "User " + signCosignerEmail + " just cosigned a document, but no signature image was embedded.\n\n" +
                  "Cosign ID: " + signCosignId + "\n" +
                  "Patient: " + (signRows[signRowIdx-1][5] || "") + "\n" +
                  "Doc: " + (signRows[signRowIdx-1][3] || "") + "\n\n" +
                  "Their signatureUrl in Users sheet: " + (signCosigner.signatureUrl || "(empty)") + "\n\n" +
                  "Likely cause: signature never saved to profile, or the Drive file was deleted/moved.\n\n" +
                  "Fix: have user re-save their signature in Settings (gear menu)."
          });
        } catch(e) { Logger.log("Signature-missing alert email failed: " + e); }
      }
      signSheet.getRange(signRowIdx, 11).setValue("signed");
      signSheet.getRange(signRowIdx, 13).setValue(new Date());
      // Check whether ALL cosigns for this patient are signed. If so, advance
      // throughput to "Cosigned" (packet-ready). A patient typically has
      // multiple cosign rows (H&P + LOMN), so we have to look across all rows
      // for that patientId — not just this one.
      try {
        var signedPatientId = String(signRows[signRowIdx-1][5] || "");
        if (signedPatientId) {
          // Re-read the sheet because we just wrote to it above (and there may
          // be other rows we haven't checked yet).
          var freshRows = signSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
          var anyPending = false;
          for (var pri = 1; pri < freshRows.length; pri++) {
            if (String(freshRows[pri][5]) === signedPatientId) {
              var st = String(freshRows[pri][10] || "").toLowerCase();
              if (st === "pending") { anyPending = true; break; }
            }
          }
          if (!anyPending) {
            var tpForSign = initThroughputSheet();
            var rowForSign = findThroughputRow(tpForSign, signedPatientId);
            if (rowForSign > 0) {
              var signCurStatus = String(tpForSign.getRange(rowForSign, 16).getValue() || "");
              // Don't regress if already past Cosigned (e.g., packet already sent)
              var signBlocked = signCurStatus === "Preauth Submitted" ||
                                signCurStatus === "Preauth In Review" ||
                                signCurStatus === "Authorized" ||
                                signCurStatus.indexOf("Preauth Rejected") === 0 ||
                                signCurStatus.indexOf("D0") === 0 ||
                                signCurStatus.indexOf("D20") === 0 ||
                                signCurStatus.indexOf("D90") === 0 ||
                                signCurStatus.indexOf("Trial ") === 0 ||
                                signCurStatus === "Reimbursed";
              if (!signBlocked) {
                tpForSign.getRange(rowForSign, 16).setValue("Cosigned");
                tpForSign.getRange(rowForSign, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
              }
            }
          }
        }
      } catch(cosignStatusErr) {
        Logger.log("signCosign: throughput status update failed: " + cosignStatusErr);
      }
      // Notify original author
      try {
        var signAuthorEmail = signRows[signRowIdx-1][6];
        MailApp.sendEmail({
          to: signAuthorEmail,
          subject: "DNC: Your doc has been cosigned - " + (signRows[signRowIdx-1][5] || ""),
          body: (signCosigner.displayName || signCosigner.email) + " has cosigned " + signRows[signRowIdx-1][1] + " for " + (signRows[signRowIdx-1][5] || "") + ".\n\nDocument: " + signRows[signRowIdx-1][3]
        });
      } catch(e) {}
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- SPRUCE WEBHOOK RECEIVER ----
    // Spruce POSTs events here. URL format: {deployed}?_action=spruceWebhook&secret=XYZ
    // We queue the event to a sheet and return 200 quickly; processSpruceEvents()
    // runs on a time-driven trigger to do the heavy lifting (fetch fax, parse, create referral).
    if (action === "spruceWebhook") {
      var providedSecret = e.parameter && e.parameter.secret;
      var expectedSecret = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SPRUCE_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
      if (!expectedSecret || providedSecret !== expectedSecret) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: "Unauthorized" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      try {
        queueSpruceEvent(e.postData && e.postData.contents);
      } catch(err) {
        Logger.log("spruceWebhook queue failed: " + err);
      }
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- SEND INSURANCE CARD SMS ----
    if (action === "sendInsuranceRequest") {
      var smsResult = sendInsuranceCardSms(data.phone, data.patientName);
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
        status: smsResult.ok ? "ok" : "error",
        message: smsResult.message
      })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- ADD REFERRAL TO THROUGHPUT ----
    if (action === "addReferral") {
      var sheet = initThroughputSheet();
      var notesField = "";
      if (data.referringProvider) notesField += "Ref: " + data.referringProvider;
      if (data.notes) notesField += (notesField ? " | " : "") + data.notes;

      // Normalize for dedup: lowercase trimmed name + last 10 digits of phone
      function normName(s) { return String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); }
      function normPhone(s) { var d = String(s || "").replace(/\D/g, ""); return d.slice(-10); }
      var newName = normName(data.patientName);
      var newPhone = normPhone(data.phone);

      // Dedup check: scan existing non-Complete throughput rows for matching name+phone
      // Skip if forceAdd is true (user already saw the warning and chose to add anyway)
      if (!data.forceAdd && newName && newPhone && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
        var existing = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 18).getValues();
        for (var i = 0; i < existing.length; i++) {
          var exName = normName(existing[i][0]);
          var exPhone = normPhone(existing[i][2]);
          var exStatus = existing[i][15] || "";
          if (exName === newName && exPhone === newPhone && exStatus !== "Complete") {
            return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
              status: "duplicate",
              existing: {
                rowIndex: i + 2,
                name: existing[i][0] || "",
                phone: existing[i][2] || "",
                dncId: existing[i][3] || "",
                status: exStatus,
                referralDate: existing[i][4] || ""
              }
            })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
          }
        }
      }

      // Use order date from the fax if parsed, else fall back to current time
      var refDate = new Date().toISOString();
      if (data.orderDate) {
        var parsed = new Date(data.orderDate);
        if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) refDate = parsed.toISOString();
      }

      // DOB prefixed with ' forces Sheets to store it as text, preventing the
      // auto-date-parse that corrupts DOB on round-trip through getReferrals.
      var dobString = data.dob ? "'" + String(data.dob).replace(/^'/, "") : "";

      // Auto-tag site: referring clinic state preferred, patient state fallback
      var matchedSites = matchReferralToSites(data.referringClinicState || "", data.state || "");
      // Normalize each site ID so the on-disk values are canonical even if
      // the Sites sheet has drift. Read paths can then use strict equality.
      var siteTag = matchedSites.map(normalizeSiteId).filter(Boolean).join(",");

      sheet.appendRow([
        data.patientName || "",
        dobString,
        data.phone || "",
        "",  // DNC ID - assigned later when intake starts
        refDate, // Referral Date (from fax if available, else scan time)
        "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "",
        "Referral Received",
        new Date().toISOString(),
        notesField,
        siteTag,  // 19 Site ID(s)
        0,        // 20 Call Attempts
        "",       // 21 Last Call At
        "",       // 22 Call Log
        ""        // 23 Comm Preference
      ]);

      // If no site matched, email admin for manual assignment
      if (!siteTag && CLINICIAN_EMAIL) {
        sendClinicianEmail({
          subject: "DNC: Referral needs site assignment - " + (data.patientName || "Unknown"),
          body: "A new referral couldn't be auto-assigned to a site.\n\n" +
                "Patient: " + (data.patientName || "Unknown") + "\n" +
                "Patient State: " + (data.state || "not parsed") + "\n" +
                "Referring Clinic State: " + (data.referringClinicState || "not parsed") + "\n\n" +
                "Please assign this referral to a site in the Clinic Throughput sheet (column 19)."
        });
      }

      __auditWrite("ok", "row=" + sheet.getLastRow() + " sites=" + (siteTag || "unassigned"));
      return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", action: "referralAdded" }))
        .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    }

    // ---- SAVE DRAFT ----
    if (action === "saveDraft") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry)");
      // Serialize to prevent concurrent races that would duplicate emails
      var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
      lock.waitLock(10000);
      try {
        var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Drafts");

        // Create headers if empty
        if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
          sheet.appendRow(["Patient ID", "Updated", "Form Data"]);
          sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setFontWeight("bold");
          sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
        }

        var patientId = data.patientId;
        var formJson = JSON.stringify(data);

        // Find existing row for this patient
        var values = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        var foundRow = -1;
        for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
          if (values[i][0] === patientId) { foundRow = i + 1; break; }
        }

        // ---- L3 DEFENSIVE MERGE ----
        // Refuse to overwrite a populated existing row with a strictly-less-
        // populated incoming payload. The frontend's autosave path always
        // sends a complete form (every field present, just possibly empty),
        // so "sparser than existing" is the signature of either a stale
        // autosave that fired against a partially-loaded form, or a load
        // that fell through to defaults and then got autosaved before the
        // user re-entered data. L2 (touchgate + loadguard) already prevents
        // both paths in normal operation; L3 is the backend's last line of
        // defense if a future regression reopens them.
        //
        // Comparison is "does this payload have meaningful clinical content"
        // (mirrors the frontend hasContent check, applied to both sides).
        // If existing has content and incoming doesn't, we refuse the write
        // unless the client explicitly opts in via _explicitClear (reserved
        // for a future "clear this draft" feature; not currently used).
        function __l3HasContent(d) {
          if (!d) return false;
          return !!(
            (d.patientName && String(d.patientName).trim()) ||
            (d.age && String(d.age).trim()) ||
            (d.sex && String(d.sex).trim()) ||
            (d.dob && String(d.dob).trim()) ||
            (d.phone && String(d.phone).trim()) ||
            (d.painRest && String(d.painRest).trim()) ||
            (d.painBest && String(d.painBest).trim()) ||
            (d.painWorst && String(d.painWorst).trim()) ||
            (d.medications && String(d.medications).trim()) ||
            (d.referringProvider && String(d.referringProvider).trim()) ||
            (d.insuranceName && String(d.insuranceName).trim()) ||
            (d.memberId && String(d.memberId).trim()) ||
            (d.deviceSerial && String(d.deviceSerial).trim()) ||
            (Array.isArray(d.pmhx) && d.pmhx.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(d.painDescriptors) && d.painDescriptors.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(d.priorTx) && d.priorTx.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(d.allergies) && d.allergies.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(d.functionalLimitations) && d.functionalLimitations.length > 0)
          );
        }

        if (foundRow > 0) {
          // Read existing JSON to compare against incoming
          var existingJson = String(sheet.getRange(foundRow, 3).getValue() || "");
          var existingData = null;
          try { existingData = existingJson ? JSON.parse(existingJson) : null; } catch(_e) { existingData = null; }
          var existingHasContent = __l3HasContent(existingData);
          var incomingHasContent = __l3HasContent(data);
          var explicitClear = data._explicitClear === true;

          if (existingHasContent && !incomingHasContent && !explicitClear) {
            // REFUSE: would overwrite real data with empty payload.
            // Log to a defensive_merges audit sheet so we can see if/when this
            // fires in production. If it fires repeatedly, L2 has a hole and
            // we need to find it; if it never fires, L3 is pure belt-and-
            // suspenders, which is also fine.
            try {
              var auditSheet = getOrCreateSheet("L3 Defensive Merges");
              if (auditSheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
                auditSheet.appendRow(["Timestamp", "Patient ID", "Source Email", "Existing Has Content", "Incoming Has Content", "Incoming Keys"]);
                auditSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 6).setFontWeight("bold");
                auditSheet.setFrozenRows(1);
              }
              var actor = "";
              try { actor = Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || ""; } catch(_e) {}
              auditSheet.appendRow([
                new Date().toISOString(),
                patientId,
                actor,
                existingHasContent,
                incomingHasContent,
                Object.keys(data || {}).slice(0, 12).join(",")
              ]);
            } catch(_e) { Logger.log("L3 audit log failed: " + _e); }
            // Update only the timestamp so we know a save was attempted, but
            // leave the JSON column untouched. Returning ok keeps the client
            // happy (no error UI), and the audit log gives us visibility.
            sheet.getRange(foundRow, 2).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
            // Skip the throughput status advancement below by returning early.
            // Status only advances when actual content is captured; an empty
            // payload doesn't represent intake progress regardless.
            return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
              status: "ok",
              defensiveMergeApplied: true,
              reason: "incoming payload had no substantive content; existing draft preserved"
            })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
          }

          // Either (a) incoming has content (normal save), or (b) both are
          // empty (no harm in writing), or (c) explicit clear requested.
          // Write incoming as-is.
          sheet.getRange(foundRow, 2).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
          sheet.getRange(foundRow, 3).setValue(formJson);
        } else {
          sheet.appendRow([patientId, new Date().toISOString(), formJson]);
        }

        // Update throughput: mark called and intake done (create row if missing)
        var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
        var tpRow = ensureThroughputRow(tpSheet, patientId, data.patientName || "");
        if (tpRow > 0) {
          // Check prior status. We only want to advance forward in the pipeline,
          // never regress. If patient is already at Awaiting Cosign, Cosigned,
          // Preauth Submitted, or past that, saving an updated draft should NOT
          // knock them back to "Intake In Progress".
          var priorStatus = String(tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).getValue() || "");
          var alreadyPastIntake = priorStatus === "Intake Complete" || // legacy status name, still present on old rows
                                  priorStatus === "Intake In Progress" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Awaiting Cosign" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Cosigned" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Preauth Submitted" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Preauth In Review" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Authorized" ||
                                  priorStatus.indexOf("Preauth Rejected") === 0 ||
                                  priorStatus.indexOf("D0") === 0 ||
                                  priorStatus.indexOf("D20") === 0 ||
                                  priorStatus.indexOf("D90") === 0 ||
                                  priorStatus.indexOf("Trial ") === 0 ||
                                  priorStatus === "Reimbursed" ||
                                  priorStatus === "Complete";

          // A "first meaningful intake save" advances status to Intake In Progress.
          // IMPORTANT: saving a draft does NOT mean intake is "complete" — it means
          // the MA has started capturing patient data. True completion (= ready for
          // biller) only happens further down the pipeline when all cosigns are
          // signed off and the preauth packet has been generated.
          //
          // Require at least one field that could only come from the patient
          // conversation (pain scores, meds, prior therapies, pain descriptors,
          // PMHx). Hydrated referral data (name, phone, insurance) doesn't count
          // because those are copied from the referral card at Start Intake,
          // before any patient contact.
          var hasPhoneIntakeContent =
            (data.painRest && String(data.painRest).trim()) ||
            (data.painBest && String(data.painBest).trim()) ||
            (data.painWorst && String(data.painWorst).trim()) ||
            (data.medications && String(data.medications).trim()) ||
            (Array.isArray(data.priorTx) && data.priorTx.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(data.painDescriptors) && data.painDescriptors.length > 0) ||
            (Array.isArray(data.pmhx) && data.pmhx.length > 0);

          if (hasPhoneIntakeContent) {
            tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 6).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); // Called
            // NOTE: col 7 (Intake Done) is kept stamped for backward compatibility
            // with downstream queries (createPostopReminders, getReferrals sort).
            // It means "MA captured content", not "pipeline-complete".
            tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 7).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
            if (!alreadyPastIntake) {
              tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).setValue("Intake In Progress");
            }
            tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString());
          } else {
            // Draft saved but intake not yet meaningful. Leave status untouched
            // so card keeps showing "Referral Received" until real data arrives.
            tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 17).setValue(new Date().toISOString()); // still bump lastUpdated
          }

          // Persist comm preference (if captured on intake) into col 23 so any
          // future notification (preauth-approved email, D0 reminder, etc.) can
          // route by the patient's stated preference. Only write when a real
          // value is present so we don't clobber a previously-captured pref
          // with empty on re-save.
          if (data.commPref) {
            tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 23).setValue(String(data.commPref));
          }
          // Force the status write to flush before we release the lock
          SpreadsheetApp.flush();

          // NOTE: we no longer email MA "Schedule D0" on intake save. Scheduling
          // doesn't happen until preauth is Authorized — sendPreauthApprovedNotification
          // fires that alert when Floyd's approval email parses, which is the
          // correct trigger point.

          // Auto-fire insurance-card SMS to patient on first intake completion.
          // No-ops gracefully if Twilio isn't configured in Script Properties.
          if (hasPhoneIntakeContent && !alreadyPastIntake && data.phone) {
            try {
              var smsResult = sendInsuranceCardSms(data.phone, data.patientName || "");
              Logger.log("Insurance SMS for " + patientId + ": " + smsResult.message);
            } catch (smsErr) {
              Logger.log("Insurance SMS failed for " + patientId + ": " + smsErr);
            }
          }
        }

        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", action: "draftSaved" }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } finally {
        lock.releaseLock();
      }
    }

    // ---- GENERATE PREAUTH PACKET ----
    if (action === "generatePreauth") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry)");
      if (!NOTES_FOLDER_ID) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "No folder configured"
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
      var preauthLock = LockService.getScriptLock();
      preauthLock.waitLock(15000);
      try {
        var rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(NOTES_FOLDER_ID);
        var patientFolderName = (data.patientName ? data.patientName + " - " : "") + (data.patientId || "Unknown");
        var pFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName(patientFolderName);
        var patientRootFolder = pFolders.hasNext() ? pFolders.next() : rootFolder.createFolder(patientFolderName);
        // All Preauth-phase docs land in their own subfolder
        var pFolder = getOrCreateSubfolder(patientRootFolder, "Preauth");

        // Lookup author profile for signature embedding
        var preauthAuthorProfile = getUserRowByEmail(data.userEmail);
        var preauthAuthorSig = preauthAuthorProfile ? fetchSignatureBlob(preauthAuthorProfile.signatureUrl) : null;

        function writeDoc(name, text) {
          var doc = DocumentApp.create(name);
          var body = doc.getBody();
          body.clear();
          var st = {};
          st[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_FAMILY] = "Calibri";
          st[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_SIZE] = 11;
          var paras = text.split("\n");
          for (var i = 0; i < paras.length; i++) {
            var para = body.appendParagraph(paras[i]);
            if (para && para.setAttributes) para.setAttributes(st);
          }
          // Embed author signature
          if (preauthAuthorProfile) {
            appendSignatureBlock(body, "Authored by", preauthAuthorProfile, preauthAuthorSig);
          }
          doc.saveAndClose();
          var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
          pFolder.addFile(file);
          DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file);
          return { url: doc.getUrl(), id: doc.getId(), name: name };
        }

        var datePrefix = data.date || "";
        var hnpDoc = data.hnpText ? writeDoc(datePrefix + " - Preauth H&P", data.hnpText) : null;
        var lomnDoc = data.lomnText ? writeDoc(datePrefix + " - LOMN", data.lomnText) : null;
        var hnpUrl = hnpDoc ? hnpDoc.url : null;
        var lomnUrl = lomnDoc ? lomnDoc.url : null;

        // Insurance Cards doc (independent file with 2-4 card images, if captured)
        var insuranceDoc = null;
        if (data.insuranceCardPhotos && data.insuranceCardPhotos.length > 0) {
          try {
            insuranceDoc = createInsuranceCardsDoc(pFolder, data.insuranceCardPhotos, datePrefix);
          } catch(icErr) {
            Logger.log("Insurance Cards doc failed: " + icErr);
          }
        }
        var insuranceUrl = insuranceDoc ? insuranceDoc.url : null;

        // Queue cosign entries if author isn't a physician.
        //
        // If author profile lookup failed (e.g. missing userEmail in payload,
        // or email not in Users sheet), we MUST NOT silently skip cosign —
        // that was exactly the bug that let Velez's preauth go out unsigned.
        // Email Lindsey an alert so any future instance surfaces immediately
        // instead of hiding as a quiet "no cosign row got made" slip.
        if (!preauthAuthorProfile) {
          var missingAuthorMsg = "PREAUTH AUTHOR LOOKUP FAILED: patientId=" + data.patientId +
                                 " userEmail=" + (data.userEmail || "(blank)") +
                                 ". Cosign was NOT created. Check Users sheet for this email " +
                                 "or confirm frontend is sending userEmail in the payload.";
          Logger.log(missingAuthorMsg);
          sendClinicianEmail({
            subject: "ALERT: Preauth cosign skipped — author lookup failed",
            body: missingAuthorMsg + "\n\nIf this preauth needs cosign, create the Cosign row manually."
          });
        } else if (String(preauthAuthorProfile.role).toLowerCase() !== "physician") {
          var preauthSiteId = data.userSiteId || (preauthAuthorProfile.locations && preauthAuthorProfile.locations[0]) || "";
          if (hnpDoc) createCosignEntry({
            docType: "Preauth H&P", docName: hnpDoc.name, docUrl: hnpDoc.url, docId: hnpDoc.id,
            patientId: data.patientId, authorEmail: data.userEmail,
            authorRole: preauthAuthorProfile.role, siteId: preauthSiteId
          });
          if (lomnDoc) createCosignEntry({
            docType: "LOMN", docName: lomnDoc.name, docUrl: lomnDoc.url, docId: lomnDoc.id,
            patientId: data.patientId, authorEmail: data.userEmail,
            authorRole: preauthAuthorProfile.role, siteId: preauthSiteId
          });
        }
        // If author IS a physician, no cosign needed — they signed at creation time.

        // Email biller
        if (BILLER_EMAIL) {
          try {
            MailApp.sendEmail({
              to: BILLER_EMAIL,
              subject: "Preauth packet ready - " + (data.patientId || ""),
              body: "Preauth packet ready for submission.\n\n" +
                    "Study ID: " + (data.patientId || "") + "\n" +
                    "Date: " + datePrefix + "\n\n" +
                    "Documents:\n" +
                    (hnpUrl ? "H&P: " + hnpUrl + "\n" : "") +
                    (lomnUrl ? "LOMN: " + lomnUrl + "\n" : "") +
                    (insuranceUrl ? "Insurance Cards: " + insuranceUrl + "\n" : "") +
                    "\nPlease submit to the appropriate payer (Medicare ~1hr turnaround, Part D 3-7 days)."
            });
          } catch(mailErr) {}
        }

        // Update throughput status (create row if missing)
        try {
          var tput = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Clinic Throughput") || initThroughputSheet();
          var tRow = ensureThroughputRow(tput, data.patientId, data.patientName || "");
         if (tRow > 0) {
          // Always submit to Floyd. Coverage type cannot be determined from card image
          // alone (MA plans look like Medicare). memberIdConfidence is still in the
          // payload above so Floyd / clinical reviewers see the dual-pass agreement
          // signal when deciding how hard to scrutinize.
          tput.getRange(tRow, 16).setValue("Preauth Submitted");
          tput.getRange(tRow, 17).setValue(new Date());
        }
        } catch(tErr) {}

        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", hnpUrl: hnpUrl, lomnUrl: lomnUrl, insuranceUrl: insuranceUrl }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } catch(err) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: err.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } finally {
        preauthLock.releaseLock();
      }
    }

    // ---- PARSE MEDICARE CARD ----
    if (action === "parseMedicareCard") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry)");
      var imageData = data.imageData || "";
      var base64 = imageData.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
      var mediaType = "image/jpeg";
      var match = imageData.match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,/);
      if (match) mediaType = match[1];

      var prompt = "Extract insurance information from this Medicare or insurance card image. Return ONLY valid JSON with no preamble, no markdown:\n\n" +
        "{\n" +
        '  "insuranceName": "e.g. Medicare Part B, Blue Cross Blue Shield",\n' +
        '  "memberId": "member ID or MBI number",\n' +
        '  "groupNum": "group number if shown",\n' +
        '  "effectiveDate": "effective/start date if shown",\n' +
        '  "patientName": "name on card"\n' +
        "}\n\n" +
        "Only include fields you can clearly read. Omit unclear or missing fields.";

  try {
        var apiData = callLLM("claude-sonnet-4-6", {
          max_tokens: 500,
          messages: [{
            role: "user",
            content: [
              { type: "image", source: { type: "base64", media_type: mediaType, data: base64 } },
              { type: "text", text: prompt }
            ]
          }]
        });
        var text = apiData.content[0].text;
        text = text.replace(/```json\n?/g, "").replace(/```\n?/g, "").trim();
        var parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", parsed: parsed }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } catch(apiErr) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "API failed: " + apiErr.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
    }
     // ---- PARSE MEDICARE CARD (DUAL PASS) ----
    if (action === "parseMedicareCardDual") {
      try {
        var result = parseMedicareCardDual(data.imageData || "");
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(
          Object.assign({ status: "ok" }, result)
        )).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } catch(err) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: err.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
    }

    // ---- PARSE DEVICE STICKER ----
    if (action === "parseDeviceSticker") {
      var imageData = data.imageData || "";
      var stickerType = data.stickerType || "device"; // "device" or "needles"
      var base64 = imageData.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
      var mediaType = "image/jpeg";
      var match = imageData.match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,/);
      if (match) mediaType = match[1];

      var prompt;
      if (stickerType === "needles") {
        prompt = "Extract needles/consumable information from this NeuroSolutions 100 Needles sticker. Return ONLY valid JSON with no preamble, no markdown:\n\n" +
          "{\n" +
          '  "lot": "lot number after LOT label",\n' +
          '  "expiration": "expiration date (the one next to the hourglass icon, NOT the manufacture date next to the factory icon)",\n' +
          '  "quantity": "quantity if shown (e.g. 4pcs)"\n' +
          "}\n\n" +
          "RULES:\n" +
          "1. Needles stickers have a LOT number but no serial number.\n" +
          "2. There are usually TWO dates: manufacture (factory icon) and expiration (hourglass icon). Return only the expiration.\n" +
          "3. GS1 application identifiers like (10) and (17) are prefixes, not values.\n" +
          "4. Omit fields you cannot clearly read.";
      } else {
        prompt = "Extract device/generator information from this NeuroSolutions 100 device sticker. Return ONLY valid JSON with no preamble, no markdown:\n\n" +
          "{\n" +
          '  "model": "model number after M.NO label",\n' +
          '  "serial": "serial number after SN label",\n' +
          '  "expiration": "expiration date (the one next to the hourglass icon, NOT the manufacture date next to the factory icon)"\n' +
          "}\n\n" +
          "RULES:\n" +
          "1. Device stickers have a serial number (SN) but typically no LOT number.\n" +
          "2. M.NO means Model Number.\n" +
          "3. There are usually TWO dates: manufacture (factory icon) and expiration (hourglass icon). Return only the expiration.\n" +
          "4. GS1 application identifiers like (01), (17), (21) are prefixes, not values. Do not include the prefix in the extracted value.\n" +
          "5. Omit fields you cannot clearly read.";
      }

    try {
        var apiData = callLLM("claude-sonnet-4-6", {
          max_tokens: 300,
          messages: [{
            role: "user",
            content: [
              { type: "image", source: { type: "base64", media_type: mediaType, data: base64 } },
              { type: "text", text: prompt }
            ]
          }]
        });
        var text = apiData.content[0].text;
        text = text.replace(/```json\n?/g, "").replace(/```\n?/g, "").trim();
        var parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "ok", parsed: parsed, stickerType: stickerType }))
          .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } catch(apiErr) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "API failed: " + apiErr.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
    }

    // ---- PARSE REFERRAL PHOTO ----
    if (action === "parseReferralPhoto") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry)");

      var imageData = data.imageData || "";
      // Accept image/* OR application/pdf data URIs
      var base64 = imageData.replace(/^data:[\w\/+.\-]+;base64,/, "");
      var mediaType = "image/jpeg";
      var match = imageData.match(/^data:([\w\/+.\-]+);base64,/);
      if (match) mediaType = match[1];

      try {
        // Two-pass pipeline: tool-use structured extraction, validation, reconciliation
        var result = parseReferralImageBase64(base64, mediaType);
        __auditWrite("ok", "parsed fields=" + Object.keys(result.parsed || {}).length +
          " disagreements=" + (result.disagreements ? result.disagreements.length : 0));
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "ok",
          parsed: result.parsed,
          confidence: result.confidence || {},
          disagreements: result.disagreements || [],
          warnings: result.warnings || []
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      } catch(apiErr) {
        __auditWrite("error", apiErr.toString().substring(0, 200));
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "API failed: " + apiErr.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
    }

    // ---- PARSE TRANSCRIPT ----
    if (action === "parseTranscript") {
      __auditWrite("ok", "(entry)");
      var transcript = data.transcript || "";
      var prompt = 'Extract clinical information from this phone intake transcript for a diabetic neuropathy clinic. Return ONLY valid JSON with no preamble, no markdown, no backticks. Use these exact field names and value formats:\n\n' +
        '{\n' +
        '  "age": "number as string",\n' +
        '  "sex": "M or F",\n' +
        '  "patientName": "Last, First format if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "dob": "MM/DD/YYYY if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "phone": "XXX-XXX-XXXX if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "email": "email if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "address": "street address only",\n' +
        '  "city": "city",\n' +
        '  "state": "2-letter state code",\n' +
        '  "zip": "zip code",\n' +
        '  "insuranceName": "primary insurance carrier name if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "memberId": "primary insurance member/subscriber ID if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "groupNum": "primary insurance group number if mentioned",\n' +
        '  "diabetesType": "Type 1 or Type 2",\n' +
        '  "diabetesDuration": "number as string",\n' +
        '  "referringProvider": "name",\n' +
        '  "painRest": "0-10 as string",\n' +
        '  "painBest": "0-10 as string",\n' +
        '  "painWorst": "0-10 as string",\n' +
        '  "painDuration": "number as string",\n' +
        '  "painDurationUnit": "Months or Years",\n' +
        '  "a1c": "number as string",\n' +
        '  "glucoseControl": "Well controlled or Poorly controlled",\n' +
        '  "medications": "free text, all meds with doses",\n' +
        '  "pshx": "free text, all surgeries",\n' +
        '  "allergiesOther": "free text if not in standard list",\n' +
        '  "neuropathyType": "Polyneuropathy or Mononeuropathy",\n' +
        '  "neuropathyDistribution": "Bilateral LE or Right LE or Left LE or Bilateral UE or Bilateral UE/LE",\n' +
        '  "socialTob": "No or Current or Former",\n' +
        '  "socialEtOH": "No or Social or Daily or Former",\n' +
        '  "socialSubstance": "No or Current or Former",\n' +
        '  "srWounds": "Yes or No (ulcers or wounds on feet or hands)",\n' +
        '  "srWoundsLocation": "location if Yes",\n' +
        '  "srBalance": "Yes or No (balance issues or falls)",\n' +
        '  "srBalanceDetail": "detail if Yes",\n' +
        '  "srSwelling": "Yes or No (swelling in arms or legs)",\n' +
        '  "srSwellingLocation": "location if Yes",\n' +
        '  "srBreathing": "Yes or No (increased work of breathing)",\n' +
        '  "srBreathingDetail": "detail if Yes",\n' +
        '  "srHeartAwareness": "Yes or No (palpitations or heart awareness)",\n' +
        '  "srHeartAwarenessDetail": "detail if Yes",\n' +
        '  "srRhythm": "Yes or No (known rhythm problems)",\n' +
        '  "srRhythmDetail": "detail if Yes",\n' +
        '  "painDescriptors": ["array of matching: Burning, Tingling, Numbness, Dysesthesia, Allodynia, Paresthesias, Hyperalgesia, Throbbing, Sharp, Aching, Prickly, Pressure, Shooting"],\n' +
        '  "pmhx": ["array of matching: HTN, Hyperlipidemia, Obesity, CAD, CHF, PVD, COPD, Asthma, CKD, Retinopathy, Depression, CAN"],\n' +
        '  "priorTx": ["array of matching: Physical therapy, Compression stockings, OTC medications, Gabapentin, Pregabalin, Duloxetine, Amitriptyline, Capsaicin, TENS unit, Opiates (current), Opiates (past), Other Rx"],\n' +
        '  "allergies": ["array of matching: NKDA, Penicillin, Betadine, Chlorhexidine"],\n' +
        '  "contraindications": ["array of matching: Hemophilia, Pregnancy, Cognitive impairment, Pacemaker, Loop recorder, Defibrillator, Spinal cord stimulator, Deep brain stimulator, Bladder stimulator"],\n' +
        '  "functionalLimitations": ["array of matching: Decreased activity, Decreased mobility, Sleep disturbance, Decreased quality of life, Balance issues / fall risk"],\n' +
        '  "painNotes": "any additional pain details not captured above",\n' +
        '  "txNotes": "therapy results, side effects, reasons for stopping",\n' +
        '  "pmhxNotes": "additional medical history details",\n' +
        '  "socialNotes": "additional social history details"\n' +
        '}\n\n' +
        'Only include fields that were discussed. Omit fields with no information. For arrays, only include values that exactly match the provided options.\n\n' +
        'TRANSCRIPT:\n' + transcript;

     try {
        var apiData = callLLM("claude-sonnet-4-6", {
          max_tokens: 2000,
          messages: [{ role: "user", content: prompt }]
        });
        var text = apiData.content[0].text;
        // Strip any markdown backticks just in case
        text = text.replace(/```json\n?/g, "").replace(/```\n?/g, "").trim();
        var parsed = JSON.parse(text);

        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "ok", parsed: parsed
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);

      } catch(apiErr) {
        return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({
          status: "error", message: "API call failed: " + apiErr.toString()
        })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
      }
    }

    // ---- SUBMIT DATA ----
    // Serialize submissions to prevent concurrent appendRow races and throughput overwrites
    var submitLock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    submitLock.waitLock(15000);
    try {
      var sheet = getOrCreateSheet("Sheet1");

    // Create headers if sheet is empty
    if (sheet.getLastRow() === 0) {
      var headers = [
        "Timestamp",
        "Patient ID", "Date", "Visit Type", "Age", "Sex", "DM Type", "DM Duration (yrs)",
        "Referring Provider", "A1C", "PMHx", "Medications", "Prior Therapies",
        "Pain Descriptors", "Pain Notes", "Functional Notes", "PMHx Notes",
        "Therapy Notes", "Social Notes", "Lab Notes",
        "Pain Rest", "Pain Best", "Pain Worst", "Glucose Control",
        "Pain Duration", "Pain Duration Unit",
        "Dx Codes", "Contraindications", "Prognosis",
        "Neuropathy Type", "Neuropathy Distribution",
        "Trigeminal Ohms", "Trigeminal Hz", "C2 Ohms", "C2 Hz",
        "Vagus Ohms", "Vagus Hz", "Device Notes",
        "NRS Pain Pre", "NRS Pain Post", "Pain Change",
        "RMSSD Pre (ms)", "RMSSD Post (ms)", "RMSSD Change (ms)",
        "Left Foot Pre (/12)", "Left Foot Post (/12)",
        "Right Foot Pre (/12)", "Right Foot Post (/12)",
        "Combined Pre (/24)", "Combined Post (/24)", "Monofilament Change",
        "Quarter Pre", "Quarter Post", "Dime Pre", "Dime Post",
        "Penny Pre", "Penny Post",
        "Coin Score Pre (/3)", "Coin Score Post (/3)",
        "SLS Right Sec Pre", "SLS Right Sec Post",
        "SLS Right Fall Pre", "SLS Right Fall Post",
        "SLS Left Sec Pre", "SLS Left Sec Post",
        "SLS Left Fall Pre", "SLS Left Fall Post",
        "10m Walk Pre (s)", "10m Walk Post (s)",
        "Subjective", "Subjective Pre-Explant", "Subjective Post-Explant",
        "Explant Site Clean", "Explant Induration", "Explant Erythema",
        "Explant Tolerated", "Explant Complication", "Explant Complication Detail",
        "Explant Notes", "Plan", "Notes"
      ];
      sheet.appendRow(headers);
      sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setFontWeight("bold");
      sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    }

    var rmssdChange = "";
    if (data.rmssdPre && data.rmssdPost) {
      rmssdChange = (parseFloat(data.rmssdPost) - parseFloat(data.rmssdPre)).toFixed(1);
    }
    var painChange = "";
    if (data.nrsPainPre && data.nrsPainPost) {
      painChange = parseFloat(data.nrsPainPost) - parseFloat(data.nrsPainPre);
    }

    var row = [
      new Date().toISOString(),
      data.patientId || "", data.date || "", data.visitType || "",
      data.age || "", data.sex || "", data.diabetesType || "", data.diabetesDuration || "",
      data.referringProvider || "", data.a1c || "",
      data.pmhx || "", data.medications || "", data.priorTx || "",
      data.painDescriptors || "", data.painNotes || "", data.functionalNotes || "",
      data.pmhxNotes || "", data.txNotes || "", data.socialNotes || "", data.labNotes || "",
      data.painRest || "", data.painBest || "", data.painWorst || "", data.glucoseControl || "",
      data.painDuration || "", data.painDurationUnit || "",
      data.dxCodes || "", data.contraindications || "", data.prognosis || "",
      data.neuropathyType || "", data.neuropathyDistribution || "",
      data.trigeminalOhms || "", data.trigeminalHz || "",
      data.c2Ohms || "", data.c2Hz || "",
      data.vagusOhms || "", data.vagusHz || "", data.deviceNotes || "",
      data.nrsPainPre || "", data.nrsPainPost || "", painChange,
      data.rmssdPre || "", data.rmssdPost || "", rmssdChange,
      data.leftFootPre || "", data.leftFootPost || "",
      data.rightFootPre || "", data.rightFootPost || "",
      data.combinedPre || "", data.combinedPost || "", data.monofilamentChange || "",
      data.coinQuarterPre || "", data.coinQuarterPost || "",
      data.coinDimePre || "", data.coinDimePost || "",
      data.coinPennyPre || "", data.coinPennyPost || "",
      data.coinScorePre || "", data.coinScorePost || "",
      data.slsRightSecPre || "", data.slsRightSecPost || "",
      data.slsRightFallPre || "", data.slsRightFallPost || "",
      data.slsLeftSecPre || "", data.slsLeftSecPost || "",
      data.slsLeftFallPre || "", data.slsLeftFallPost || "",
      data.walkTimePre || "", data.walkTimePost || "",
      data.subjective || "", data.subjectivePre || "", data.subjectivePost || "",
      data.explantSiteClean || "", data.explantInduration || "", data.explantErythema || "",
      data.explantTolerated || "", data.explantComplication || "", data.explantComplicationDetail || "",
      data.explantNotes || "", data.plan || "", data.notes || ""
    ];

    sheet.appendRow(row);

    // Update throughput based on visit type (create row if missing)
    var tpSheet = initThroughputSheet();
    var tpRow = ensureThroughputRow(tpSheet, data.patientId, data._patientName || "");
    if (tpRow > 0) {
      var visitType = data.visitType || "";
      var ts = new Date().toISOString();
      // Read prior status so we can skip email re-sends on duplicate submits
      var priorStatus = String(tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).getValue() || "");

      if (visitType === "D0") {
        var alreadyD0Done = priorStatus.indexOf("D0 Complete") === 0 ||
                            priorStatus.indexOf("D20") === 0 ||
                            priorStatus.indexOf("D90") === 0 ||
                            priorStatus === "Complete";
        tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 9).setValue(ts);  // D0 Complete
        if (!alreadyD0Done) {
          tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).setValue("D0 Complete - Awaiting Medicare");
        }
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
        if (!alreadyD0Done) {
          // Notify biller. Wrapped so email failures don't kill the submit.
          if (BILLER_EMAIL) {
            try {
              MailApp.sendEmail({
                to: BILLER_EMAIL,
                subject: "DNC: Medicare packet ready - " + (data.patientId || ""),
                body: "D0 visit complete for " + (data.patientId || "") + ". Clinical note, LOMN, and documentation are in the patient's Google Drive folder. Please submit Medicare packet.\n\nDiabetic Nerve Clinic"
              });
            } catch (emailErr) {
              Logger.log("Biller notification email failed for " + data.patientId + ": " + emailErr);
            }
          }
          // Notify MA to schedule D20
          if (MA_EMAIL) {
            try {
              MailApp.sendEmail({
                to: MA_EMAIL,
                subject: "DNC: Schedule D20 explant - " + (data.patientId || ""),
                body: "D0 implant complete for " + (data.patientId || "") + ". Please schedule D20 explant appointment approximately 20 days from today.\n\nDiabetic Nerve Clinic"
              });
            } catch (emailErr) {
              Logger.log("MA D20-schedule email failed for " + data.patientId + ": " + emailErr);
            }
          }
        }
      } else if (visitType === "D20") {
        var alreadyD20Done = priorStatus.indexOf("D20 Complete") === 0 ||
                             priorStatus.indexOf("D90") === 0 ||
                             priorStatus === "Complete";
