        tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 13).setValue(ts); // D20 Complete
        if (!alreadyD20Done) {
          tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).setValue("D20 Complete - Schedule D90");
        }
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
        // Notify MA to schedule D90. Wrapped so email failures don't kill the submit.
        if (!alreadyD20Done && MA_EMAIL) {
          try {
            MailApp.sendEmail({
              to: MA_EMAIL,
              subject: "DNC: Schedule D90 follow-up - " + (data.patientId || ""),
              body: "D20 explant complete for " + (data.patientId || "") + ". Please schedule D90 follow-up appointment approximately 70 days from today.\n\nDiabetic Nerve Clinic"
            });
          } catch (emailErr) {
            Logger.log("MA D90-schedule email failed for " + data.patientId + ": " + emailErr);
          }
        }
      } else if (visitType === "D90") {
        tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 15).setValue(ts); // D90 Complete
        if (priorStatus !== "Complete") {
          tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 16).setValue("Complete");
        }
      }
      tpSheet.getRange(tpRow, 17).setValue(ts);
    }

    // Create Google Docs if folder is configured
    var docErrors = [];
    if (NOTES_FOLDER_ID && (data._noteText || data._hnpText || data._opNoteText || data._lomnText || data._requisitionText)) {
      try {
        var folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(NOTES_FOLDER_ID);

        // Get or create patient subfolder, then drill into visit-specific subfolder
        var patientFolderName = (data._patientName ? data._patientName + " - " : "") + (data.patientId || "Unknown");
        var patientFolders = folder.getFoldersByName(patientFolderName);
        var patientRoot;
        if (patientFolders.hasNext()) {
          patientRoot = patientFolders.next();
        } else {
          patientRoot = folder.createFolder(patientFolderName);
        }
        // Route this submission into its own visit subfolder (Preauth/D0/D20/D90)
        var visitSubfolderName = (data.visitType || "Visit");
        var patientFolder = getOrCreateSubfolder(patientRoot, visitSubfolderName);

        // Helper: create a doc in the patient folder with optional photos
        function createDoc(docName, text, photos, fontOverride) {
          var docStyle = {};
          docStyle[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_FAMILY] = fontOverride || "Calibri";
          docStyle[DocumentApp.Attribute.FONT_SIZE] = fontOverride === "Courier New" ? 9 : 11;
          var doc = DocumentApp.create(docName);
          var body = doc.getBody();
          body.clear();
          var paragraphs = text.split("\n");
          for (var p = 0; p < paragraphs.length; p++) {
            var para = body.appendParagraph(paragraphs[p]);
            if (para && para.setAttributes) para.setAttributes(docStyle);
          }
          if (photos && photos.length > 0) {
            body.appendParagraph("");
            body.appendHorizontalRule();
            var hdr = body.appendParagraph("PHOTOS:");
            if (hdr && hdr.setAttributes) {
              hdr.setAttributes(docStyle);
              hdr.editAsText().setBold(true);
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < photos.length; i++) {
              var photo = photos[i];
              var lbl = body.appendParagraph(photo.label || photo.type);
              if (lbl && lbl.setAttributes) lbl.setAttributes(docStyle);
              try {
                var b64 = photo.data.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
                var decoded = Utilities.base64Decode(b64);
                var blob = Utilities.newBlob(decoded, "image/jpeg", photo.type + ".jpg");
                body.appendImage(blob).setWidth(400);
              } catch(imgErr) {
                body.appendParagraph("[Image failed: " + imgErr.toString() + "]");
              }
            }
          }
          // Embed author signature
          if (authorProfileForDoc) {
            appendSignatureBlock(body, "Authored by", authorProfileForDoc, authorSigBlobForDoc);
          }
          doc.saveAndClose();
          var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
          patientFolder.addFile(file);
          DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file);
          return { id: doc.getId(), url: doc.getUrl(), name: docName };
        }

        // Lookup author profile once for all docs in this submission
        var authorProfileForDoc = getUserRowByEmail(data.userEmail);
        var authorSigBlobForDoc = authorProfileForDoc ? fetchSignatureBlob(authorProfileForDoc.signatureUrl) : null;
        var needsCosignForDoc = authorProfileForDoc && String(authorProfileForDoc.role).toLowerCase() !== "physician";
        var siteIdForDoc = data.userSiteId || (authorProfileForDoc ? (authorProfileForDoc.locations[0] || "") : "");

        function queueCosignIfNeeded(docResult, docType) {
          if (!needsCosignForDoc || !docResult) return;
          createCosignEntry({
            docType: docType, docName: docResult.name, docUrl: docResult.url, docId: docResult.id,
            patientId: data.patientId, authorEmail: data.userEmail,
            authorRole: authorProfileForDoc.role, siteId: siteIdForDoc
          });
        }

        var datePrefix = data.date || "";
        var visitType = data.visitType || "";

        // Split photos: insurance cards go to their own doc, everything else attaches to op note
        var opNotePhotos = null;
        var insurancePhotosForD0 = null;
        if (data._photos && data._photos.length > 0) {
          opNotePhotos = data._photos.filter(function(p) { return !p.type || p.type.indexOf("insurance_") !== 0; });
          insurancePhotosForD0 = data._photos.filter(function(p) { return p.type && p.type.indexOf("insurance_") === 0; });
          if (opNotePhotos.length === 0) opNotePhotos = null;
        }

        // D0: four separate documents
        if (visitType === "D0") {
          if (data._hnpText) {
            try { queueCosignIfNeeded(createDoc(datePrefix + " - Preauth H&P", data._hnpText, null), "Preauth H&P"); }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("H&P: " + e.toString()); }
          }
          if (data._opNoteText) {
            try { queueCosignIfNeeded(createDoc(datePrefix + " - Op Note D0", data._opNoteText, opNotePhotos), "Op Note D0"); }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("OpNote: " + e.toString()); }
          }
          if (insurancePhotosForD0 && insurancePhotosForD0.length > 0) {
            try { createInsuranceCardsDoc(patientFolder, insurancePhotosForD0, datePrefix); }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("InsuranceCards: " + e.toString()); }
          }
          if (data._lomnText) {
            try { queueCosignIfNeeded(createDoc(datePrefix + " - LOMN", data._lomnText, null), "LOMN"); }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("LOMN: " + e.toString()); }
          }
          if (data._requisitionText) {
            try {
              // Attach the signed consent image to the requisition doc if available
              var reqPhotos = null;
              if (data._consentImage) {
                reqPhotos = [{
                  type: "consent_procedure",
                  label: "Signed Procedure Consent",
                  data: data._consentImage
                }];
              }
              queueCosignIfNeeded(createDoc(datePrefix + " - Requisition & Consent", data._requisitionText, reqPhotos, "Courier New"), "Requisition & Consent");
            }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("Requisition: " + e.toString()); }
          }
        } else {
          // D20, D90: single progress note
          if (data._noteText) {
            try { queueCosignIfNeeded(createDoc(datePrefix + " - " + visitType, data._noteText, null), visitType + " Progress Note"); }
            catch(e) { docErrors.push("Note: " + e.toString()); }
          }
        }
      } catch(docErr) {
        docErrors.push("Folder/setup: " + docErr.toString());
      }
    }

    var responseMsg = { status: "ok", row: sheet.getLastRow() };
    if (docErrors.length > 0) responseMsg.docWarnings = docErrors;
    __auditWrite("ok", "visitType=" + (data.visitType || "") + (docErrors.length > 0 ? " docWarnings=" + docErrors.length : ""));
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(responseMsg))
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
    } finally {
      submitLock.releaseLock();
    }

  } catch (err) {
    // Top-level catch: audit the failure. Action and user may not be defined if parse failed very early.
    try {
      auditLog({
        action: (typeof action !== "undefined" ? action : "unknown"),
        user: (typeof __auditUser !== "undefined" ? __auditUser : ""),
        patientId: (typeof __auditPatientId !== "undefined" ? __auditPatientId : ""),
        outcome: "exception",
        latencyMs: (typeof __auditStart !== "undefined" ? Date.now() - __auditStart : ""),
        detail: String(err).substring(0, 400)
      });
    } catch(auditErr) {}
    return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: "error", message: err.toString() }))
      .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
  }
}
// ============================================================
// MBI VERIFICATION LOG
// Every dual-pass parse writes a row here. Use it to track
// disagreement rate and decide if the verify pass is earning
// its cost. After a month of data: if disagreement rate is
// near zero, downgrade pass-1 to Haiku to halve cost. If
// disagreement rate is nonzero, keep dual Sonnet — it's
// catching real misreads that would have hit Medicare as
// rejections.
// ============================================================
function initMbiVerificationLog() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("MBI Verification Log");
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("MBI Verification Log");
    sheet.appendRow([
      "Timestamp", "Patient ID", "Insurance Name",
      "Pass 1 MBI", "Pass 2 MBI", "MBI Agreement",
      "Pass 1 Group", "Pass 2 Group", "Group Agreement",
      "Final MBI", "Final Confidence", "MBI Format Valid",
      "Disagreements (JSON)", "Auto-Authorize Eligible"
    ]);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 14).setFontWeight("bold");
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}

// ============================================================
// DUAL-PASS MEDICARE CARD PARSE
// Two independent Sonnet 4.6 extract passes. Reconcile per-field.
// Returns { parsed, confidence, disagreements } where confidence
// is per-field with values: "confirmed" | "suspect" | "format_error" | "missing"
// ============================================================
function parseMedicareCardDual(imageData) {
  var base64 = imageData.replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "");
  var mediaType = "image/jpeg";
  var match = imageData.match(/^data:(image\/\w+);base64,/);
  if (match) mediaType = match[1];

  var prompt =
    "Extract insurance information from this Medicare or insurance card image. " +
    "Return ONLY valid JSON with no preamble, no markdown:\n\n" +
    "{\n" +
    '  "insuranceName": "e.g. Medicare Part B, Blue Cross Blue Shield",\n' +
    '  "memberId": "member ID or MBI exactly as printed",\n' +
    '  "groupNum": "group number if shown",\n' +
    '  "effectiveDate": "effective/start date if shown",\n' +
    '  "patientName": "name on card"\n' +
    "}\n\n" +
    "MEMBER ID ACCURACY: Read each character of memberId with extreme care. " +
    "Common confusions: 0 vs O, 1 vs I vs l, 5 vs S, 8 vs B. If you cannot " +
    "read a character with full confidence, use '?' as a placeholder for that character. " +
    "Do not guess. A '?' is more useful than a wrong character.\n\n" +
    "Only include fields you can clearly read. Omit unclear or missing fields.";

  function runOnce() {
  var resp = callLLM("claude-sonnet-4-6", {
    max_tokens: 500,
    messages: [{
        role: "user",
        content: [
          { type: "image", source: { type: "base64", media_type: mediaType, data: base64 } },
          { type: "text", text: prompt }
        ]
      }]
    });
    var text = resp.content[0].text;
    text = text.replace(/```json\n?/g, "").replace(/```\n?/g, "").trim();
    return JSON.parse(text);
  }

  var pass1, pass2;
  try { pass1 = runOnce(); } catch(e) { pass1 = { _error: e.toString() }; }
  try { pass2 = runOnce(); } catch(e) { pass2 = { _error: e.toString() }; }

  // If either pass crashed entirely, fall back to whichever succeeded
  // and mark everything as suspect so the gate refuses to auto-authorize.
  if (pass1._error && pass2._error) {
    return {
      parsed: {},
      confidence: { memberId: "missing", insuranceName: "missing", groupNum: "missing" },
      disagreements: [{ field: "_both_passes_failed", pass1: pass1._error, pass2: pass2._error }]
    };
  }
  if (pass1._error) pass1 = pass2;
  if (pass2._error) pass2 = pass1;

  // Reconcile each field. Normalize: trim, uppercase, strip spaces/dashes for comparison.
  function norm(s) {
    if (s == null) return "";
    return String(s).replace(/[\s-]/g, "").toUpperCase();
  }
  function reconcile(field, v1, v2) {
    var n1 = norm(v1), n2 = norm(v2);
    if (!n1 && !n2) return { value: "", confidence: "missing" };
    // Question marks indicate the model couldn't read characters confidently
    if (n1.indexOf("?") >= 0 || n2.indexOf("?") >= 0) {
      return { value: v1 || v2, confidence: "format_error" };
    }
    if (!n1) return { value: v2, confidence: "suspect" };
    if (!n2) return { value: v1, confidence: "suspect" };
    if (n1 === n2) return { value: v1, confidence: "confirmed" };
    return { value: v1, confidence: "suspect", alt: v2 };
  }

  var memberIdR = reconcile("memberId", pass1.memberId, pass2.memberId);
  var insuranceR = reconcile("insuranceName", pass1.insuranceName, pass2.insuranceName);
  var groupR = reconcile("groupNum", pass1.groupNum, pass2.groupNum);
  var effectiveR = reconcile("effectiveDate", pass1.effectiveDate, pass2.effectiveDate);
  var patientR = reconcile("patientName", pass1.patientName, pass2.patientName);

  // Format validation on the final memberId. Only applies to traditional
  // Medicare — Medicare Advantage and commercial use different ID formats.
  var finalInsurance = insuranceR.value || "";
  var finalMemberId = memberIdR.value || "";
  var mbiFormatValid = null;
  if (isMedicareTraditional_(finalInsurance) && finalMemberId) {
    mbiFormatValid = isValidMBI_(finalMemberId);
    if (!mbiFormatValid && memberIdR.confidence === "confirmed") {
      // Both passes agreed but the result doesn't match MBI format.
      // Demote to format_error — this is the case where Sonnet saw e.g.
      // a Medicare Advantage card mislabeled or got the format wrong.
      memberIdR.confidence = "format_error";
    }
  }

  var disagreements = [];
  ["memberId", "insuranceName", "groupNum", "effectiveDate", "patientName"].forEach(function(f) {
    var v1 = pass1[f], v2 = pass2[f];
    if (v1 && v2 && norm(v1) !== norm(v2)) {
      disagreements.push({ field: f, pass1: v1, pass2: v2 });
    }
  });

  // Log this parse for later analysis. Wrapped in try so a log failure
  // doesn't break the parse itself — we'd rather have a successful parse
  // and a missing log row than fail the whole flow because the sheet
  // was renamed or something.
  try {
    var logSheet = initMbiVerificationLog();
    logSheet.appendRow([
      new Date().toISOString(),
      "",  // Patient ID — caller can fill this in if known; left blank from this layer
      finalInsurance,
      pass1.memberId || "",
      pass2.memberId || "",
      memberIdR.confidence === "confirmed",
      pass1.groupNum || "",
      pass2.groupNum || "",
      groupR.confidence === "confirmed",
      finalMemberId,
      memberIdR.confidence,
      mbiFormatValid === null ? "n/a" : (mbiFormatValid ? "valid" : "invalid"),
      disagreements.length ? JSON.stringify(disagreements) : "",
      memberIdR.confidence === "confirmed" && mbiFormatValid === true
    ]);
  } catch(logErr) {}

  return {
    parsed: {
      insuranceName: finalInsurance,
      memberId: finalMemberId,
      groupNum: groupR.value || "",
      effectiveDate: effectiveR.value || "",
      patientName: patientR.value || ""
    },
    confidence: {
      memberId: memberIdR.confidence,
      insuranceName: insuranceR.confidence,
      groupNum: groupR.confidence,
      effectiveDate: effectiveR.confidence,
      patientName: patientR.confidence,
      mbiFormatValid: mbiFormatValid
    },
    disagreements: disagreements,
    alternates: {
      memberId: memberIdR.alt || null,
      insuranceName: insuranceR.alt || null,
      groupNum: groupR.alt || null
    }
  };
}

// ============================================================
// MBI / Medicare helpers — server-side mirrors of the frontend
// versions so the gate can trust them without round-tripping.
// Suffix _ to avoid collision if you later expose them.
// ============================================================
function isValidMBI_(s) {
  if (!s) return false;
  var clean = String(s).replace(/[\s-]/g, "").toUpperCase();
  if (clean.length !== 11) return false;
  return /^[1-9][AC-HJ-KMNP-RT-Y][0-9AC-HJ-KMNP-RT-Y][0-9][AC-HJ-KMNP-RT-Y][0-9AC-HJ-KMNP-RT-Y][0-9][AC-HJ-KMNP-RT-Y]{2}[0-9]{2}$/.test(clean);
}

function isMedicareTraditional_(insuranceName) {
  if (!insuranceName) return false;
  var p = String(insuranceName).toLowerCase();
  return /\bmedicare\b/.test(p) && !/advantage|ma\s*plan|hmo|ppo/.test(p);
}

// ============================================================
// AUTO-AUTHORIZE GATE
// Single source of truth for whether a preauth should bypass
// Floyd. Callers should NOT replicate this logic — call this.
//
// AUTO_AUTHORIZE_ENABLED flag lets us ship the dual-pass + log
// infrastructure first and watch logs for a week before flipping
// the gate live. To enable: set the flag below to true and redeploy.
// ============================================================
// AUTO-AUTHORIZE — REMOVED 2026-05-04
// Briefly attempted to bypass Floyd for clean traditional Medicare cards.
// Killed because card image cannot reliably distinguish traditional Medicare
// from MA plans that issue similar-looking cards. Real-world failure: HMO-
// disguised-as-Medicare patient blew up reimbursement entirely. The gate was
// solving the wrong problem — eligibility ≠ identity. CMS eligibility
// verification (which Floyd already does) is the only authoritative source
// for coverage type. Dual-pass MBI extraction + format validation + log all
// remain in service. They prevent OCR errors and surface low-confidence
// extractions to Floyd. If a CMS eligibility API integration is ever wired
// in, the auto-auth path can be reintroduced safely on top of THAT signal.


// ============================================================
// SMOKE TESTS - Run these from the editor to verify everything
// Select function from dropdown, click Run
// Check: Sheet1 for data rows, Drafts tab, Drive folder for docs
// ============================================================

// Tiny 1x1 white JPEG for photo testing
var TEST_PHOTO = "data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQSkZJRgABAQEASABIAAD/2wBDAP//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////2wBDAf//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////wAARCAABAAEDASIAAhEBAxEB/8QAFAABAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACf/EABQQAQAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAD/xAAUAQEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA/8QAFBEBAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAP/aAAwDAQACEQMRAD8AKwA//9k=";

// Run this function from the editor to trigger Drive/Document authorization prompts.
// After running successfully, doPost will have the needed scopes.
function testAuth() {
  Logger.log("Testing authorization...");

  // This forces the Drive scope
  var folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(NOTES_FOLDER_ID);
  Logger.log("Drive OK - folder: " + folder.getName());

  // This forces the Document scope
  var doc = DocumentApp.create("AUTH_TEST_" + new Date().getTime());
  doc.getBody().setText("Authorization test - safe to delete");
  doc.saveAndClose();
  Logger.log("Document OK - created: " + doc.getName());

  // Move to patient folder so it's not orphaned in root
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  folder.addFile(file);
  DriveApp.getRootFolder().removeFile(file);
  Logger.log("File moved to patient folder");

  Logger.log("Authorization complete. All scopes granted.");
  return "OK - delete AUTH_TEST file manually";
}

    })}

  // Test loading the draft
  var loadResult = doGet({ parameter: { action: "loadDraft", id: "DNC-DRFT" } });
  Logger.log("Draft load: " + loadResult.getContent());
}

function testVertexAuth() {
  var token = ScriptApp.getOAuthToken();
  Logger.log("Token starts with: " + token.substring(0, 20) + "...");
  Logger.log("Token length: " + token.length);
  // Hit the Vertex endpoint with a minimal request to verify the token works
  var url = "https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/575474053150/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/";
  var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
    method: "get",
    headers: { "Authorization": "Bearer " + token },
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  });
  Logger.log("Response code: " + resp.getResponseCode());
  Logger.log("Response body (first 500): " + resp.getContentText().substring(0, 500));
}

function debugCheckTokenLength() {
  var t = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("DNC_INTERNAL_TOKEN");
  Logger.log("length: " + (t ? t.length : "null"));
  Logger.log("first char code: " + (t ? t.charCodeAt(0) : "null"));
  Logger.log("last char code: " + (t ? t.charCodeAt(t.length - 1) : "null"));
  Logger.log("has trailing whitespace: " + (t ? /\s$/.test(t) : "null"));
  Logger.log("has leading whitespace: " + (t ? /^\s/.test(t) : "null"));
}

/**
 * Diagnostic: prints the full state of the Spruce fax-intake pipeline.
 * Read-only — no writes. Run from the Apps Script editor.
 */
function spruceStatus() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  Logger.log("=== SPRUCE FAX-INTAKE STATUS ===\n");

  // 1. Script Properties
  Logger.log("--- Script Properties ---");
  var token = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_API_TOKEN");
  var faxId = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_FAX_ENDPOINT_ID");
  var phoneId = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_PHONE_ENDPOINT_ID");
  var webhookSecret = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  var hmacSecret = props.getProperty("SPRUCE_HMAC_SECRET");
  Logger.log("SPRUCE_API_TOKEN: " + (token ? "present (" + token.length + " chars)" : "MISSING"));
  Logger.log("SPRUCE_FAX_ENDPOINT_ID: " + (faxId || "MISSING"));
  Logger.log("SPRUCE_PHONE_ENDPOINT_ID: " + (phoneId || "MISSING"));
  Logger.log("SPRUCE_WEBHOOK_SECRET: " + (webhookSecret ? "present" : "MISSING"));
  Logger.log("SPRUCE_HMAC_SECRET: " + (hmacSecret ? "present" : "not set (optional)"));
  Logger.log("");

  // 2. Events sheet
  Logger.log("--- Spruce Events Sheet ---");
  var eventsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Spruce Events");
  if (!eventsSheet) {
    Logger.log("Sheet does NOT exist. No webhook has ever fired through queueSpruceEvent.");
  } else {
    var rows = eventsSheet.getLastRow();
    Logger.log("Sheet exists, " + rows + " row(s) (including header)");
    if (rows >= 2) {
      var start = Math.max(2, rows - 4);
      var data = eventsSheet.getRange(start, 1, rows - start + 1, 6).getValues();
      Logger.log("Last " + data.length + " event(s):");
      data.forEach(function(r) {
        Logger.log("  " + r[0] + " | type=" + r[1] + " | status=" + r[2] + " | err=" + (r[5] || "-"));
      });
    }
  }
  Logger.log("");

 // 3. Triggers
  Logger.log("--- Triggers ---");
  var processTrigger = null;
  try {
    var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
    triggers.forEach(function(t) {
      if (t.getHandlerFunction() === "processSpruceEvents") processTrigger = t;
    });
    if (!processTrigger) {
      Logger.log("processSpruceEvents trigger: MISSING. Queued events will never process.");
    } else {
      Logger.log("processSpruceEvents trigger: present (" + processTrigger.getEventType() + ")");
    }
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("Trigger check skipped (missing scope: script.scriptapp). Doesn't block setup. Error: " + String(e).substring(0, 100));
  }
  Logger.log("");

  // 4. Deployment URL
  Logger.log("--- Deployment ---");
  try {
    var deployedUrl = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl();
    Logger.log("Current deployed web app URL: " + (deployedUrl || "not deployed"));
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("Could not get deployed URL: " + e);
  }
  Logger.log("");

  // 5. Registered webhooks on Spruce
  Logger.log("--- Registered Webhooks (Spruce side) ---");
  if (!token) {
    Logger.log("Skipping — no SPRUCE_API_TOKEN");
  } else {
    try {
      var hooks = spruceFetch("GET", "/webhooks/endpoints");
      var arr = Array.isArray(hooks) ? hooks : (hooks && hooks.data) || [];
      Logger.log("Webhooks registered: " + arr.length);
      arr.forEach(function(h, i) {
        Logger.log("  [" + i + "] name=" + (h.name || "-") + " url=" + (h.url || "-") + " id=" + (h.id || "-"));
      });
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log("Failed to list webhooks: " + e);
    }
  }
  Logger.log("");

  // 6. Internal endpoints (the fax/phone/email lines registered on Spruce)
  Logger.log("--- Internal Endpoints (Spruce side) ---");
  if (!token) {
    Logger.log("Skipping — no SPRUCE_API_TOKEN");
  } else {
    try {
      var ends = spruceFetch("GET", "/internalendpoints");
      var earr = Array.isArray(ends) ? ends : (ends && ends.data) || [];
      Logger.log("Endpoints: " + earr.length);
      earr.forEach(function(ep, i) {
        Logger.log("  [" + i + "] channel=" + (ep.channel || "-") +
                   " name=" + (ep.displayName || ep.name || "-") +
                   " id=" + (ep.id || "-"));
      });
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log("Failed to list endpoints: " + e);
    }
  }
  Logger.log("");

  // 7. Verdict
  Logger.log("--- Verdict ---");
  if (!token) {
    Logger.log("CRITICAL: SPRUCE_API_TOKEN missing. Set it in Script Properties first, then run setupSpruceIntegration(deployedUrl).");
  } else if (!faxId || !webhookSecret) {
    Logger.log("INCOMPLETE: setupSpruceIntegration never finished. Run it now: setupSpruceIntegration(deployedUrl)");
  } else if (!processTrigger) {
    Logger.log("INCOMPLETE: processSpruceEvents trigger missing. Apps Script editor → Triggers → Add Trigger → processSpruceEvents → Time-driven → Minutes → Every minute.");
  } else if (!eventsSheet) {
    Logger.log("MAYBE WEBHOOK URL STALE: setup ran but no events ever queued. Compare the deployed URL above to the registered webhook URL. If they differ, re-deploy and re-run setupSpruceIntegration(currentDeployedUrl).");
  } else {
    Logger.log("Pipeline appears complete. Issue is upstream — check Spruce inbox directly for the USFAS fax, and verify what fax number USFAS used.");
  }
}
function runSpruceSetupNow() {
  setupSpruceIntegration("https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbzF12eGtZJesIFLDkusV-MUx7OS88XuXC-fr6-EqoEeW6TnQLts5u2vSNtk5PpI5nUn/exec");
}

// Test the notification path end-to-end. Builds a fake parsed referral and
// fires the notification with priority context. Useful for verifying the email
// goes to the right recipients and SMS triggers when expected. Doesn't touch
// the throughput sheet.
function testReferralAddedNotification() {
  var fakeParsed = {
    name: "TestPatient, Notification",
    dob: "01/15/1955",
    phone: "+15555550199",
    provider: "Dr Thomas (Salisbury Clinic)",
    address: "123 Test St", city: "Salisbury", state: "MD", zip: "21801"
  };
  sendReferralAddedNotification(
    fakeParsed,
    "Secure msg via Spruce (test)",
    "DEL",           // pretend site
    999,             // fake row
    "Dr Thomas requests this patient be high priority she is having alot of diabetic nerve pain."
  );
  Logger.log("Test fired. Check your inbox (and cell if EVAN_CELL set).");
}
